```
1→"""Resolve command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import sys
     7→
     8→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     9→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import resolve_lang
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.queue_progress import show_score_with_plan_context
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.guardrails import require_triage_current_or_exit
    13→from desloppify.core import config as config_mod  # noqa: F401 (compat export)
    14→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    15→from desloppify.engine.plan import append_log_entry, has_living_plan, load_plan, purge_ids, save_plan
    16→from desloppify.intelligence import narrative as narrative_mod
    17→from desloppify.state import coerce_assessment_score
    18→
    19→from .apply import _resolve_all_patterns, _write_resolve_query_entry
    20→from .ignore_cmd import cmd_ignore_pattern
    21→from .persist import _save_state_or_exit
    22→from .queue_guard import _check_queue_order_guard
    23→from .render import (
    24→    _print_next_command,
    25→    _print_resolve_summary,
    26→    _print_subjective_reset_hint,
    27→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning,
    28→)
    29→from .selection import (
    30→    ResolveQueryContext,
    31→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation,
    32→    _previous_score_snapshot,
    33→    _validate_resolve_inputs,
    34→    show_note_length_requirement,
    35→    validate_note_length,
    36→)
    37→
    38→
    39→def cmd_resolve(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    40→    """Resolve finding(s) matching one or more patterns."""
    41→    attestation = getattr(args, "attest", None)
    42→    _validate_resolve_inputs(args, attestation)
    43→
    44→    if args.status == "fixed":
    45→        note = getattr(args, "note", None)
    46→        if not validate_note_length(note):
    47→            show_note_length_requirement(note)
    48→            return
    49→
    50→    state_file = state_path(args)
    51→    state = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
    52→
    53→    if _check_queue_order_guard(state, args.patterns, args.status):
    54→        return
    55→
    56→    if args.status == "fixed":
    57→        require_triage_current_or_exit(
    58→            state=state,
    59→            bypass=bool(getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)),
    60→            attest=getattr(args, "attest", "") or "",
    61→        )
    62→
    63→    _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
    64→        state,
    65→        args,
    66→        attestation=attestation,
    67→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=_resolve_all_patterns,
    68→    )
    69→    prev = _previous_score_snapshot(state)
    70→    prev_subjective_scores = {
    71→        str(dim): (coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0)
    72→        for dim, payload in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {}).items()
    73→        if isinstance(dim, str)
    74→    }
    75→
    76→    all_resolved = _resolve_all_patterns(state, args, attestation=attestation)
    77→    if not all_resolved:
    78→        status_label = "resolved" if args.status == "open" else "open"
    79→        print(colorize(f"No {status_label} findings matching: {' '.join(args.patterns)}", "yellow"))
    80→        return
    81→
    82→    _save_state_or_exit(state, state_file)
    83→
    84→    try:
    85→        if has_living_plan():
    86→            plan = load_plan()
    87→            purged = purge_ids(plan, all_resolved)
    88→            append_log_entry(
    89→                plan,
    90→                "resolve",
    91→                finding_ids=all_resolved,
    92→                actor="user",
    93→                note=getattr(args, "note", None),
    94→                detail={"status": args.status, "attestation": attestation},
    95→            )
    96→            if purged:
    97→                save_plan(plan)
    98→                print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: {purged} item(s) removed from queue.", "dim"))
    99→            else:
   100→                save_plan(plan)
   101→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
   102→        print(colorize("  Warning: could not update living plan.", "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
   103→
   104→    _print_resolve_summary(status=args.status, all_resolved=all_resolved)
   105→    _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
   106→        state,
   107→        status=args.status,
   108→        resolved_count=len(all_resolved),
   109→    )
   110→    show_score_with_plan_context(state, prev)
   111→    _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   112→        args=args,
   113→        state=state,
   114→        all_resolved=all_resolved,
   115→        prev_subjective_scores=prev_subjective_scores,
   116→    )
   117→
   118→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   119→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   120→    narrative = narrative_mod.compute_narrative(
   121→        state,
   122→        context=narrative_mod.NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="resolve"),
   123→    )
   124→    if narrative.get("milestone"):
   125→        print(colorize(f"  → {narrative['milestone']}", "green"))
   126→
   127→    next_command = _print_next_command(state)
   128→    _write_resolve_query_entry(
   129→        ResolveQueryContext(
   130→            patterns=args.patterns,
   131→            status=args.status,
   132→            resolved=all_resolved,
   133→            next_command=next_command,
   134→            prev_overall=prev.overall,
   135→            prev_objective=prev.objective,
   136→            prev_strict=prev.strict,
   137→            prev_verified=prev.verified,
   138→            attestation=attestation,
   139→            narrative=narrative,
   140→            state=state,
   141→        )
   142→    )
   143→
   144→
   145→__all__ = ["_check_queue_order_guard", "cmd_ignore_pattern", "cmd_resolve"]
   146→
```

```
1→"""Validation and preflight helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import copy
     7→import sys
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass
     9→
    10→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    11→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
    12→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    13→
    14→_REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES = ("i have actually", "not gaming")
    15→_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT = ("I have actually", "not gaming")
    16→_MIN_NOTE_LENGTH = 50
    17→
    18→
    19→def _emit_warning(message: str) -> None:
    20→    """Write resolve preflight warnings to stderr consistently."""
    21→    print(colorize(message, "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
    22→
    23→
    24→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    25→class ResolveQueryContext:
    26→    patterns: list[str]
    27→    status: str
    28→    resolved: list[str]
    29→    next_command: str
    30→    prev_overall: float | None
    31→    prev_objective: float | None
    32→    prev_strict: float | None
    33→    prev_verified: float | None
    34→    attestation: str | None
    35→    narrative: dict
    36→    state: dict
    37→
    38→
    39→def _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation: str | None) -> list[str]:
    40→    normalized = " ".join((attestation or "").strip().lower().split())
    41→    return [
    42→        phrase for phrase in _REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES if phrase not in normalized
    43→    ]
    44→
    45→
    46→def validate_attestation(attestation: str | None) -> bool:
    47→    return not _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation)
    48→
    49→
    50→def show_attestation_requirement(
    51→    label: str,
    52→    attestation: str | None,
    53→    example: str,
    54→) -> None:
    55→    missing = _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation)
    56→    if not attestation:
    57→        _emit_warning(f"{label} requires --attest.")
    58→    elif missing:
    59→        missing_str = ", ".join(f"'{keyword}'" for keyword in missing)
    60→        _emit_warning(
    61→            f"{label} attestation is missing required keyword(s): {missing_str}."
    62→        )
    63→    _emit_warning(
    64→        f"Required keywords: '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[0]}' and '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[1]}'."
    65→    )
    66→    print(colorize(f'Example: --attest "{example}"', "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    67→
    68→
    69→def validate_note_length(note: str | None) -> bool:
    70→    """Return True if the note meets the minimum length requirement."""
    71→    return note is not None and len(note.strip()) >= _MIN_NOTE_LENGTH
    72→
    73→
    74→def show_note_length_requirement(note: str | None) -> None:
    75→    """Emit a warning about minimum note length."""
    76→    current = len((note or "").strip())
    77→    _emit_warning(
    78→        f"Note must be at least {_MIN_NOTE_LENGTH} characters (got {current}). "
    79→        f"Describe what you actually did."
    80→    )
    81→
    82→
    83→def _validate_resolve_inputs(args: argparse.Namespace, attestation: str | None) -> None:
    84→    if args.status == "wontfix" and not args.note:
    85→        _emit_warning(
    86→            "Wontfix items become technical debt. Add --note to record your reasoning for future review."
    87→        )
    88→        sys.exit(1)
    89→    if args.status == "open":
    90→        return
    91→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
    92→        show_attestation_requirement(
    93→            "Manual resolve",
    94→            attestation,
    95→            ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    96→        )
    97→        sys.exit(1)
    98→
    99→
   100→def _previous_score_snapshot(state: dict) -> state_mod.ScoreSnapshot:
   101→    """Load a score snapshot for comparison after resolve operations."""
   102→    return state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   103→
   104→
   105→def _preview_resolve_count(state: dict, patterns: list[str]) -> int:
   106→    """Count unique open findings matching the provided patterns."""
   107→    matched_ids: set[str] = set()
   108→    for pattern in patterns:
   109→        for finding in state_mod.match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter="open"):
   110→            finding_id = finding.get("id")
   111→            if finding_id:
   112→                matched_ids.add(finding_id)
   113→    return len(matched_ids)
   114→
   115→
   116→def _estimate_wontfix_strict_delta(
   117→    state: dict,
   118→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   119→    *,
   120→    attestation: str | None,
   121→    resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   122→) -> float:
   123→    """Estimate strict score drop if this resolve command is applied as wontfix."""
   124→    before = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   125→    if before is None:
   126→        return 0.0
   127→
   128→    preview_state = copy.deepcopy(state)
   129→    resolve_all_patterns_fn(preview_state, args, attestation=attestation)
   130→    after = state_mod.get_strict_score(preview_state)
   131→    if after is None:
   132→        return 0.0
   133→    return max(0.0, before - after)
   134→
   135→
   136→def _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
   137→    state: dict,
   138→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   139→    *,
   140→    attestation: str | None,
   141→    resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   142→) -> None:
   143→    if args.status != "wontfix":
   144→        return
   145→
   146→    preview_count = _preview_resolve_count(state, args.patterns)
   147→    if preview_count <= 10:
   148→        return
   149→    if getattr(args, "confirm_batch_wontfix", False):
   150→        return
   151→
   152→    strict_delta = _estimate_wontfix_strict_delta(
   153→        state,
   154→        args,
   155→        attestation=attestation,
   156→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   157→    )
   158→    _emit_warning(f"Large wontfix batch detected ({preview_count} findings).")
   159→    if strict_delta > 0:
   160→        _emit_warning(f"Estimated strict-score debt added now: {strict_delta:.1f} points.")
   161→    _emit_warning(
   162→        "Re-run with --confirm-batch-wontfix if this debt is intentional."
   163→    )
   164→    sys.exit(1)
   165→
```

```
1→"""Terminal rendering helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import _print_strict_target_nudge
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.render_support import (
     9→    print_post_resolve_guidance,
    10→    print_strict_gap_note,
    11→    score_snapshot_or_warn,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→
    15→
    16→def _print_resolve_summary(*, status: str, all_resolved: list[str]) -> None:
    17→    verb = "Reopened" if status == "open" else "Resolved"
    18→    print(colorize(f"\n{verb} {len(all_resolved)} finding(s) as {status}:", "green"))
    19→    for fid in all_resolved[:20]:
    20→        print(f"  {fid}")
    21→    if len(all_resolved) > 20:
    22→        print(f"  ... and {len(all_resolved) - 20} more")
    23→
    24→
    25→def _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
    26→    state: dict,
    27→    *,
    28→    status: str,
    29→    resolved_count: int,
    30→) -> None:
    31→    if status != "wontfix" or resolved_count <= 10:
    32→        return
    33→    wontfix_count = sum(
    34→        1 for finding in state["findings"].values() if finding["status"] == "wontfix"
    35→    )
    36→    actionable = sum(
    37→        1
    38→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
    39→        if finding["status"]
    40→        in ("open", "wontfix", "fixed", "auto_resolved", "false_positive")
    41→    )
    42→    wontfix_pct = round(wontfix_count / actionable * 100) if actionable else 0
    43→    print(
    44→        colorize(
    45→            f"\n  ⚠ Wontfix debt is now {wontfix_count} findings ({wontfix_pct}% of actionable).",
    46→            "yellow",
    47→        )
    48→    )
    49→    print(
    50→        colorize(
    51→            '    The strict score reflects this. Run `desloppify show "*" --status wontfix` to review.',
    52→            "dim",
    53→        )
    54→    )
    55→
    56→
    57→def _delta_suffix(delta: float) -> str:
    58→    if abs(delta) < 0.05:
    59→        return ""
    60→    return f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})"
    61→
    62→
    63→def _print_score_movement(
    64→    *,
    65→    status: str,
    66→    prev_overall: float | None,
    67→    prev_objective: float | None,
    68→    prev_strict: float | None,
    69→    prev_verified: float | None,
    70→    state: dict,
    71→    has_review_findings: bool = False,
    72→    target_strict: float | None = None,
    73→) -> None:
    74→    new = score_snapshot_or_warn(state)
    75→    if new is None:
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    overall_delta = new.overall - (prev_overall or 0)
    79→    objective_delta = new.objective - (prev_objective or 0)
    80→    strict_delta = new.strict - (prev_strict or 0)
    81→    verified_delta = new.verified - (prev_verified or 0)
    82→    print(
    83→        f"\n  Scores: overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(overall_delta)}"
    84→        + colorize(
    85→            f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(objective_delta)}",
    86→            "dim",
    87→        )
    88→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(strict_delta)}", "dim")
    89→        + colorize(
    90→            f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(verified_delta)}", "dim"
    91→        )
    92→    )
    93→    if target_strict is not None:
    94→        _print_strict_target_nudge(new.strict, target_strict, show_next=False)
    95→    print_strict_gap_note(status, overall=new.overall, strict=new.strict)
    96→    print_post_resolve_guidance(
    97→        status=status,
    98→        has_review_findings=has_review_findings,
    99→        overall_delta=overall_delta,
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→def _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   104→    *,
   105→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   106→    state: dict,
   107→    all_resolved: list[str],
   108→    prev_subjective_scores: dict[str, float],
   109→) -> None:
   110→    has_review = any(
   111→        state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   112→        for fid in all_resolved
   113→    )
   114→    if not has_review or not state.get("subjective_assessments"):
   115→        return
   116→
   117→    stale_dims = sorted(
   118→        dim
   119→        for dim in {
   120→            str(
   121→                state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   122→            ).strip()
   123→            for fid in all_resolved
   124→            if state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   125→        }
   126→        if dim and dim in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {})
   127→    )
   128→    if not stale_dims:
   129→        return
   130→
   131→    shown = ", ".join(stale_dims[:3])
   132→    if len(stale_dims) > 3:
   133→        shown = f"{shown}, +{len(stale_dims) - 3} more"
   134→    print(
   135→        colorize(
   136→            f"  Subjective scores unchanged — re-run review for updated scores: {shown}",
   137→            "yellow",
   138→        )
   139→    )
   140→    print(
   141→        colorize(
   142→            "  Next subjective step: "
   143→            + (
   144→                "`desloppify review --prepare --dimensions "
   145→                f"{','.join(stale_dims)} --force-review-rerun`"
   146→            ),
   147→            "dim",
   148→        )
   149→    )
   150→
   151→
   152→def _print_next_command(state: dict) -> str:
   153→    remaining = sum(
   154→        1
   155→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
   156→        if finding["status"] == "open"
   157→        and not finding.get("suppressed")
   158→    )
   159→    next_command = "desloppify scan"
   160→    if remaining > 0:
   161→        suffix = "s" if remaining != 1 else ""
   162→        print(
   163→            colorize(
   164→                f"\n  {remaining} finding{suffix} remaining — run `desloppify next`",
   165→                "dim",
   166→            )
   167→        )
   168→        next_command = "desloppify next"
   169→    print(colorize(f"  Next command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
   170→    print()
   171→    return next_command
   172→
```

Explore the desloppify codebase to understand the plan/state data structures and persistence. Specifically:

1. Read `desloppify/engine/_plan/schema.py` — what is the full data model for the plan? What fields exist?
2. Read `desloppify/engine/_plan/operations.py` — what operations can be performed on plan items?
3. Read `desloppify/engine/_state/schema.py` — what is tracked per-finding in state?

```
1→"""State resolution operations (match + resolve)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import copy
     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "coerce_assessment_score",
     9→    "match_findings",
    10→    "resolve_findings",
    11→]
    12→
    13→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import _matches_pattern
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    16→    StateModel,
    17→    ensure_state_defaults,
    18→    utc_now,
    19→    validate_state_invariants,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
    22→
    23→
    24→def coerce_assessment_score(value: object) -> float | None:
    25→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float.
    26→
    27→    Returns ``None`` when the value cannot be interpreted as a numeric score
    28→    (e.g. bools, non-numeric strings, missing keys).
    29→    """
    30→    if is_numeric(value):
    31→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))), 1)
    32→    if isinstance(value, dict):
    33→        raw = value.get("score")
    34→        if not is_numeric(raw):
    35→            return None
    36→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(raw))), 1)
    37→    return None
    38→
    39→
    40→def _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
    41→    state: StateModel,
    42→    *,
    43→    status: str,
    44→    resolved_findings: list[dict],
    45→    now: str,
    46→) -> None:
    47→    """Mark subjective assessments as stale when review findings are resolved.
    48→
    49→    The assessment score is preserved (not zeroed) — only a fresh review import
    50→    should change dimension scores.  The stale marker tells the UI to prompt
    51→    for a re-review.
    52→    """
    53→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    54→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    55→        return
    56→
    57→    touched_dimensions: set[str] = set()
    58→    for finding in resolved_findings:
    59→        if finding.get("detector") != "review":
    60→            continue
    61→        dimension = str(finding.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")).strip()
    62→        if dimension:
    63→            touched_dimensions.add(dimension)
    64→
    65→    for dimension in sorted(touched_dimensions):
    66→        if dimension not in assessments:
    67→            continue
    68→
    69→        payload = assessments.get(dimension)
    70→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
    71→            payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    72→            payload["refresh_reason"] = f"review_finding_{status}"
    73→            payload["stale_since"] = now
    74→        else:
    75→            assessments[dimension] = {
    76→                "score": coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0,
    77→                "needs_review_refresh": True,
    78→                "refresh_reason": f"review_finding_{status}",
    79→                "stale_since": now,
    80→            }
    81→
    82→
    83→def match_findings(
    84→    state: StateModel, pattern: str, status_filter: str = "open"
    85→) -> list[dict]:
    86→    """Return findings matching *pattern* with the given status."""
    87→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
    88→    return [
    89→        finding
    90→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
    91→        if not finding.get("suppressed")
    92→        if (status_filter == "all" or finding["status"] == status_filter)
    93→        and _matches_pattern(finding_id, finding, pattern)
    94→    ]
    95→
    96→
    97→def resolve_findings(
    98→    state: StateModel,
    99→    pattern: str,
   100→    status: str,
   101→    note: str | None = None,
   102→    attestation: str | None = None,
   103→) -> list[str]:
   104→    """Set finding status for matches and return affected finding IDs."""
   105→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   106→    now = utc_now()
   107→    resolved: list[str] = []
   108→    resolved_findings: list[dict] = []
   109→    status_filter = "all" if status == "open" else "open"
   110→    for finding in match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter):
   111→        previous_status = str(finding.get("status", "open")).strip() or "open"
   112→        if status == "open" and previous_status == "open":
   113→            continue
   114→
   115→        extra_updates: dict[str, object] = {}
   116→        if status == "wontfix":
   117→            snapshot_scan_count = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   118→            extra_updates["wontfix_scan_count"] = snapshot_scan_count
   119→            extra_updates["wontfix_snapshot"] = {
   120→                "captured_at": now,
   121→                "scan_count": snapshot_scan_count,
   122→                "tier": finding.get("tier"),
   123→                "confidence": finding.get("confidence"),
   124→                "detail": copy.deepcopy(finding.get("detail", {})),
   125→            }
   126→        if status == "open":
   127→            finding["reopen_count"] = int(finding.get("reopen_count", 0) or 0) + 1
   128→            finding.pop("wontfix_scan_count", None)
   129→            finding.pop("wontfix_snapshot", None)
   130→            previous_note = finding.get("note")
   131→            next_note = note if note is not None else previous_note
   132→            extra_updates["resolved_at"] = None
   133→            extra_updates["note"] = next_note
   134→            reopen_attestation = {
   135→                "kind": "manual_reopen",
   136→                "text": attestation or note,
   137→                "attested_at": now,
   138→                "scan_verified": False,
   139→            }
   140→            reopen_attestation["previous_status"] = previous_status
   141→            extra_updates["resolution_attestation"] = reopen_attestation
   142→
   143→        updates: dict[str, object] = {
   144→            "status": status,
   145→            "note": note,
   146→            "resolved_at": now,
   147→            "suppressed": False,
   148→            "suppressed_at": None,
   149→            "suppression_pattern": None,
   150→            "resolution_attestation": {
   151→                "kind": "manual",
   152→                "text": attestation,
   153→                "attested_at": now,
   154→                "scan_verified": False,
   155→            },
   156→        }
   157→        updates.update(extra_updates)
   158→        finding.update(updates)
   159→        resolved.append(finding["id"])
   160→        resolved_findings.append(finding)
   161→
   162→    _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
   163→        state,
   164→        status=status,
   165→        resolved_findings=resolved_findings,
   166→        now=now,
   167→    )
   168→
   169→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   170→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   171→    return resolved
   172→
```

```
1→"""next command: show next highest-priority queue items."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations

    37→
    38→def _scorecard_subjective(
    39→    state: dict,
    40→    dim_scores: dict,
    41→) -> list[dict]:
    42→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    43→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    44→
    45→
    46→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    47→    state: dict,
    48→    dim_scores: dict,
    49→    *,
    50→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    51→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    52→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    53→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    54→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    55→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    56→            continue
    57→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    58→        if strict_val < threshold:
    59→            low.append(
    60→                (
    61→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    62→                    strict_val,
    63→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    64→                )
    65→            )
    66→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    67→    return low
    68→
    69→
    70→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    71→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    72→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    73→    state = runtime.state
    74→    config = runtime.config
    75→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    79→    if skill_warning:
    80→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    81→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    82→    if config_warning:
    83→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    84→
    85→    print_triage_guardrail_info(state=state)
    86→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    87→
    88→
    89→def _resolve_cluster_focus(
    90→    plan_data: dict | None,
    91→    *,
    92→    cluster_arg: str | None,
    93→    scope: str | None,
    94→) -> str | None:
    95→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
    96→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
    97→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    98→        if active_cluster:
    99→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   100→    return effective_cluster
   101→
   102→
   103→def _build_next_payload(
   104→    *,
   105→    queue: dict,
   106→    items: list[dict],
   107→    state: dict,
   108→    narrative: dict,
   109→    plan_data: dict | None,
   110→) -> dict:
   111→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   112→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   113→    )
   114→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   115→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   116→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   117→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   118→        state,
   119→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   120→    )
   121→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   122→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   123→    ]
   124→    return payload
   125→
   126→
   127→def _emit_requested_output(
   128→    args,
   129→    payload: dict,
   130→    items: list[dict],
   131→) -> bool:
   132→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   133→    if output_file:
   134→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   135→            output_file,
   136→            payload,
   137→            len(items),
   138→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   139→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   140→        ):
   141→            return True
   142→        raise SystemExit(1)
   143→
   144→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
   145→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   146→        return True
   147→    return False
   148→
   149→
   150→def _plan_queue_context(
   151→    *,
   152→    state: dict,
   153→    plan_data: dict | None,
   154→) -> tuple[float | None, QueueBreakdown | None]:
   155→    plan_start_strict = get_plan_start_strict(plan_data)
   156→    try:
   157→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan_data)
   158→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   159→        breakdown = None
   160→    return plan_start_strict, breakdown
   161→
   162→
   163→def _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials: dict | None) -> dict | None:
   164→    try:
   165→        return merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   166→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   167→        return raw_potentials or None
   168→
   169→
   170→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
   171→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
   172→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
   173→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
   174→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
   175→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
   176→    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   177→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   178→
   179→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
   180→
   181→    # Load the living plan
   182→    plan = load_plan()
   183→    plan_data: dict | None = None
   184→    if (
   185→        plan.get("queue_order")
   186→        or plan.get("overrides")
   187→        or plan.get("clusters")
   188→    ):
   189→        plan_data = plan
   190→
   191→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   192→    effective_cluster = _resolve_cluster_focus(
   193→        plan_data,
   194→        cluster_arg=cluster_arg,
   195→        scope=scope,
   196→    )
   197→
   198→    queue = build_work_queue(
   199→        state,
   200→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   201→            count=count,
   202→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   203→            scope=scope,
   204→            status=status,
   205→            include_subjective=True,
   206→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   207→            explain=explain,
   208→            plan=plan_data,
   209→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   210→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   211→        ),
   212→    )
   213→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   214→
   215→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   216→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   217→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   218→        state,
   219→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   220→    )
   221→
   222→    payload = _build_next_payload(
   223→        queue=queue,
   224→        items=items,
   225→        state=state,
   226→        narrative=narrative,
   227→        plan_data=plan_data,
   228→    )
   229→    write_query(payload)
   230→
   231→    if _emit_requested_output(args, payload, items):
   232→        return
   233→
   234→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   235→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   236→        state.get("findings", {}),
   237→        state.get("scan_path"),
   238→    )
   239→
   240→    # Extract frozen plan-start score and queue breakdown for lifecycle display
   241→    plan_start_strict, breakdown = _plan_queue_context(
   242→        state=state,
   243→        plan_data=plan_data,
   244→    )
   245→    queue_total = breakdown.queue_total if breakdown else 0
   246→
   247→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   248→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   249→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(
   250→        queue,
   251→        strict_score,
   252→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   253→        target_strict=target_strict,
   254→    ):
   255→        return
   256→
   257→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   258→    potentials = _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials)
   259→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   260→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   261→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   262→        cluster_filter=effective_cluster,
   263→    )
   264→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   265→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   266→        state,
   267→        dim_scores,
   268→        findings_scoped,
   269→        strict_score=strict_score,
   270→        target_strict_score=target_strict,
   271→        queue_total=queue_total,
   272→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   273→        breakdown=breakdown,
   274→    )
   275→    print()
   276→
   277→
   278→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]
```

```
1→"""Plan schema types, defaults, and validation."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any, Required, TypedDict
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema_migrations import (
     8→    ensure_container_types as _ensure_container_types,
     9→    migrate_deferred_to_skipped as _migrate_deferred_to_skipped,
    10→    migrate_epics_to_clusters as _migrate_epics_to_clusters,
    11→    migrate_synthesis_to_triage as _migrate_synthesis_to_triage,
    12→    migrate_v5_to_v6 as _migrate_v5_to_v6,
    13→    normalize_cluster_defaults as _normalize_cluster_defaults,
    14→)
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    16→
    17→PLAN_VERSION = 7
    18→
    19→VALID_SKIP_KINDS = {"temporary", "permanent", "false_positive", "triaged_out"}
    20→
    21→EPIC_PREFIX = "epic/"
    22→VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS = {
    23→    "delete", "merge", "flatten", "enforce",
    24→    "simplify", "decompose", "extract", "inline",
    25→}
    26→
    27→
    28→class SkipEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    29→    finding_id: Required[str]
    30→    kind: Required[str]  # "temporary" | "permanent" | "false_positive"
    31→    reason: str | None
    32→    note: str | None  # required for permanent (wontfix note)
    33→    attestation: str | None  # required for permanent/false_positive
    34→    created_at: str
    35→    review_after: int | None  # re-surface after N scans (temporary only)
    36→    skipped_at_scan: int  # state.scan_count when skipped
    37→
    38→
    39→class ItemOverride(TypedDict, total=False):
    40→    finding_id: Required[str]
    41→    description: str | None
    42→    note: str | None
    43→    cluster: str | None
    44→    created_at: str
    45→    updated_at: str
    46→
    47→
    48→class Cluster(TypedDict, total=False):
    49→    name: Required[str]
    50→    description: str | None
    51→    finding_ids: list[str]
    52→    created_at: str
    53→    updated_at: str
    54→    auto: bool  # True for auto-generated clusters
    55→    cluster_key: str  # Deterministic grouping key (for regeneration)
    56→    action: str | None  # Primary resolution command/guidance text
    57→    user_modified: bool  # True when user manually edits membership
    58→
    59→
    60→class ExecutionLogEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    61→    timestamp: Required[str]
    62→    action: Required[str]  # "done", "skip", "unskip", "resolve", "reconcile", "cluster_done", "focus", "reset"
    63→    finding_ids: list[str]
    64→    cluster_name: str | None
    65→    actor: str  # "user" | "system" | "agent"
    66→    note: str | None
    67→    detail: dict  # action-specific extra data
    68→
    69→
    70→class SupersededEntry(TypedDict, total=False):
    71→    original_id: Required[str]
    72→    original_detector: str
    73→    original_file: str
    74→    original_summary: str
    75→    status: str  # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
    76→    superseded_at: str
    77→    remapped_to: str | None
    78→    candidates: list[str]
    79→    note: str | None
    80→
    81→
    82→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    83→    version: Required[int]
    84→    created: Required[str]
    85→    updated: Required[str]
    86→    queue_order: list[str]
    87→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
    88→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
    89→    active_cluster: str | None
    90→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
    91→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
    92→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
    93→    promoted_ids: list[str]  # IDs user explicitly positioned via move_items()
    94→    plan_start_scores: dict  # frozen score snapshot from plan creation cycle
    95→    execution_log: list[ExecutionLogEntry]
    96→    epic_triage_meta: dict  # triage engine metadata
    97→
    98→
    99→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
   100→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
   101→    now = utc_now()
   102→    return {
   103→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
   104→        "created": now,
   105→        "updated": now,
   106→        "queue_order": [],
   107→        "deferred": [],
   108→        "skipped": {},
   109→        "active_cluster": None,
   110→        "overrides": {},
   111→        "clusters": {},
   112→        "superseded": {},
   113→        "promoted_ids": [],
   114→        "plan_start_scores": {},
   115→        "execution_log": [],
   116→        "epic_triage_meta": {},
   117→    }
   118→
   119→
   120→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   121→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   122→
   123→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict),
   124→    v3 (top-level epics) to v4 (epics unified into clusters),
   125→    v5 (gates) to v6 (unified queue),
   126→    and v6 (synthesis naming) to v7 (triage naming).
   127→    """
   128→    defaults = empty_plan()
   129→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   130→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   131→
   132→    _ensure_container_types(plan)
   133→    _migrate_deferred_to_skipped(plan)
   134→    _migrate_epics_to_clusters(plan)
   135→    _normalize_cluster_defaults(plan)
   136→    _migrate_v5_to_v6(plan)
   137→    _migrate_synthesis_to_triage(plan)
   138→
   139→
   140→def triage_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   141→    """Return clusters whose name starts with ``EPIC_PREFIX``."""
   142→    return {
   143→        name: cluster
   144→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   145→        if name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   146→    }
   147→
   148→
   149→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   150→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
   151→    if not isinstance(plan.get("version"), int):
   152→        raise ValueError("plan.version must be an int")
   153→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
   154→        raise ValueError("plan.queue_order must be a list")
   155→
   156→    # No ID should appear in both queue_order and skipped
   157→    skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   158→    overlap = set(plan["queue_order"]) & skipped_ids
   159→    if overlap:
   160→        raise ValueError(
   161→            f"IDs cannot appear in both queue_order and skipped: {sorted(overlap)}"
   162→        )
   163→
   164→    # Validate skip entry kinds
   165→    for fid, entry in plan.get("skipped", {}).items():
   166→        kind = entry.get("kind")
   167→        if kind not in VALID_SKIP_KINDS:
   168→            raise ValueError(
   169→                f"Invalid skip kind {kind!r} for {fid}; must be one of {sorted(VALID_SKIP_KINDS)}"
   170→            )
   171→
   172→
   173→__all__ = [
   174→    "EPIC_PREFIX",
   175→    "ExecutionLogEntry",
   176→    "PLAN_VERSION",
   177→    "Cluster",
   178→    "ItemOverride",
   179→    "PlanModel",
   180→    "SkipEntry",
   181→    "SupersededEntry",
   182→    "VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS",
   183→    "VALID_SKIP_KINDS",
   184→    "empty_plan",
   185→    "ensure_plan_defaults",
   186→    "triage_clusters",
   187→    "validate_plan",
   188→]
   189→
```

```
1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    Cluster,
     7→    ExecutionLogEntry,
     8→    PlanModel,
     9→    SkipEntry,
    10→    empty_plan,
    11→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    14→
    15→
    16→_DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES = 10000
    17→
    18→
    19→def _get_log_cap() -> int:
    20→    """Read execution_log_max_entries from config. Returns 0 for unlimited."""
    21→    try:
    22→        from desloppify.core.config import load_config
    23→
    24→        config = load_config()
    25→        value = config.get("execution_log_max_entries", _DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES)
    26→        return max(0, int(value))
    27→    except (ImportError, OSError, ValueError, TypeError):
    28→        return _DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES
    29→
    30→
    31→def append_log_entry(
    32→    plan: PlanModel,
    33→    action: str,
    34→    *,
    35→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    36→    cluster_name: str | None = None,
    37→    actor: str = "user",
    38→    note: str | None = None,
    39→    detail: dict | None = None,
    40→) -> None:
    41→    """Append a structured entry to the plan's execution log."""
    42→    log = plan.get("execution_log", [])
    43→    entry: ExecutionLogEntry = {
    44→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    45→        "action": action,
    46→        "finding_ids": finding_ids or [],
    47→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
    48→        "actor": actor,
    49→        "note": note,
    50→        "detail": detail or {},
    51→    }
    52→    log.append(entry)
    53→    cap = _get_log_cap()
    54→    if cap > 0 and len(log) > cap:
    55→        plan["execution_log"] = log[-cap:]
    56→
    57→
    58→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    59→# Helpers
    60→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    61→
    62→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    63→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    64→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    65→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    66→    if finding_id in order:
    67→        order.remove(finding_id)
    68→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    69→
    70→
    71→def _resolve_position(
    72→    order: list[str],
    73→    position: str,
    74→    target: str | None = None,
    75→    offset: int | None = None,
    76→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    77→) -> int:
    78→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    79→
    80→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    81→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    82→    """
    83→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    84→
    85→    if position == "top":
    86→        return 0
    87→    if position == "bottom":
    88→        return len(order)
    89→
    90→    if position in {"before", "after"} and target:
    91→        return _resolve_relative_position(order, moving, position=position, target=target)
    92→
    93→    if position in {"up", "down"} and offset is not None:
    94→        return _resolve_offset_position(
    95→            order,
    96→            moving,
    97→            position=position,
    98→            offset=offset,
    99→            finding_ids=finding_ids,
   100→        )
   101→
   102→    return len(order)
   103→
   104→
   105→def _resolve_relative_position(
   106→    order: list[str],
   107→    moving: set[str],
   108→    *,
   109→    position: str,
   110→    target: str,
   111→) -> int:
   112→    for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
   113→        if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
   114→            return i if position == "before" else i + 1
   115→    return 0 if position == "before" else len(order)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _find_index(items: list[str], target: str) -> int | None:
   119→    for i, item_id in enumerate(items):
   120→        if item_id == target:
   121→            return i
   122→    return None
   123→
   124→
   125→def _resolve_offset_position(
   126→    order: list[str],
   127→    moving: set[str],
   128→    *,
   129→    position: str,
   130→    offset: int,
   131→    finding_ids: list[str] | None,
   132→) -> int:
   133→    if not finding_ids:
   134→        return 0 if position == "up" else len(order)
   135→    first_id = finding_ids[0]
   136→    clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
   137→    current_idx = _find_index(clean_order, first_id)
   138→    if current_idx is None:
   139→        if position == "up":
   140→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
   141→        return len(clean_order)
   142→    if position == "up":
   143→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
   144→    return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
   145→
   146→
   147→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   148→# Move items
   149→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   150→
   151→def move_items(
   152→    plan: PlanModel,
   153→    finding_ids: list[str],
   154→    position: str,
   155→    target: str | None = None,
   156→    offset: int | None = None,
   157→) -> int:
   158→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   159→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   160→
   161→    # Triage stage IDs are workflow-managed and cannot be manually reordered
   162→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import TRIAGE_IDS
   163→    finding_ids = [fid for fid in finding_ids if fid not in TRIAGE_IDS]
   164→    if not finding_ids:
   165→        return 0
   166→
   167→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   168→
   169→    # Remove from skipped if present
   170→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   171→    for fid in finding_ids:
   172→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   173→
   174→    # Remove from current position in order
   175→    for fid in finding_ids:
   176→        if fid in order:
   177→            order.remove(fid)
   178→
   179→    # Resolve insertion point
   180→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   181→
   182→    # Insert in original order
   183→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   184→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   185→
   186→    # Track user-promoted IDs only when explicitly moved to the front.
   187→    # Moving to bottom/down/after should not create a promotion barrier.
   188→    if position == "top":
   189→        promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   190→        existing_promoted = set(promoted)
   191→        for fid in finding_ids:
   192→            if fid not in existing_promoted:
   193→                promoted.append(fid)
   194→                existing_promoted.add(fid)
   195→        plan["promoted_ids"] = promoted
   196→
   197→    return len(finding_ids)
   198→
   199→
   200→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   201→# Skip / unskip
   202→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   203→
   204→def skip_items(
   205→    plan: PlanModel,
   206→    finding_ids: list[str],
   207→    *,
   208→    kind: str = "temporary",
   209→    reason: str | None = None,
   210→    note: str | None = None,
   211→    attestation: str | None = None,
   212→    review_after: int | None = None,
   213→    scan_count: int = 0,
   214→) -> int:
   215→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   216→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   217→    now = utc_now()
   218→    count = 0
   219→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   220→    skip_set = set(finding_ids)
   221→    promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   222→    plan["promoted_ids"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in skip_set]
   223→    for fid in finding_ids:
   224→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   225→        skipped[fid] = {
   226→            "finding_id": fid,
   227→            "kind": kind,
   228→            "reason": reason,
   229→            "note": note,
   230→            "attestation": attestation,
   231→            "created_at": now,
   232→            "review_after": review_after,
   233→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
   234→        }
   235→        count += 1
   236→    return count
   237→
   238→
   239→def unskip_items(
   240→    plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]
   241→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   242→    """Bring finding IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   243→
   244→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
   245→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   246→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
   247→    """
   248→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   249→    count = 0
   250→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   251→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   252→    for fid in finding_ids:
   253→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
   254→        if entry is not None:
   255→            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   256→                need_reopen.append(fid)
   257→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   258→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   259→            count += 1
   260→    return count, need_reopen
   261→
   262→
   263→def resurface_stale_skips(
   264→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   265→) -> list[str]:
   266→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   267→
   268→    Returns list of resurfaced finding IDs.
   269→    """
   270→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   271→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   272→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   273→    for fid in list(skipped):
   274→        entry = skipped[fid]
   275→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   276→            continue
   277→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   278→        if review_after is None:
   279→            continue
   280→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   281→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   282→            skipped.pop(fid)
   283→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   284→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   285→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   286→    return resurfaced
   287→

   289→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   290→# Describe / annotate

   292→
   293→def describe_finding(
   294→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, description: str | None
   295→) -> None:
   296→    """Set or clear an augmented description on a finding."""
   297→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   298→    now = utc_now()
   299→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   300→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   301→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   302→    overrides[finding_id]["description"] = description
   303→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now

   306→def annotate_finding(

   308→) -> None:
   309→    """Set or clear a note on a finding."""
   310→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)

   313→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   314→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   315→    overrides[finding_id]["note"] = note
   316→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now

   319→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   320→# Clusters
   321→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   322→
   323→def create_cluster(
   324→    plan: PlanModel,
   325→    name: str,
   326→    description: str | None = None,
   327→    action: str | None = None,
   328→) -> Cluster:
   329→    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
   330→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   331→    if name.startswith("auto/"):
   332→        raise ValueError(
   333→            f"Cluster names starting with 'auto/' are reserved for auto-clusters: {name!r}"
   334→        )
   335→    if name.startswith("epic/"):
   336→        raise ValueError(
   337→            f"Cluster names starting with 'epic/' are reserved for triage epics: {name!r}"
   338→        )
   339→    if name in plan["clusters"]:
   340→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} already exists")
   341→    now = utc_now()
   342→    cluster: Cluster = {
   343→        "name": name,
   344→        "description": description,
   345→        "finding_ids": [],
   346→        "created_at": now,
   347→        "updated_at": now,
   348→        "auto": False,
   349→        "cluster_key": "",
   350→        "action": action,
   351→        "user_modified": False,
   352→    }
   353→    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
   354→    return cluster
   355→
   356→
   357→def add_to_cluster(
   358→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   359→) -> int:
   360→    """Add finding IDs to a cluster. Returns count added."""
   361→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   362→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   363→    if cluster is None:
   364→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   365→
   366→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   367→    count = 0
   368→    now = utc_now()
   369→    for fid in finding_ids:
   370→        if fid not in member_ids:
   371→            member_ids.append(fid)
   372→            count += 1
   373→        # Update override to track cluster membership
   374→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   375→        if fid not in overrides:
   376→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   377→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name
   378→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   379→
   380→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   381→    return count
   382→
   383→
   384→def remove_from_cluster(
   385→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   386→) -> int:
   387→    """Remove finding IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed."""
   388→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   389→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   390→    if cluster is None:
   391→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   392→
   393→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   394→    now = utc_now()
   395→    count = 0
   396→    for fid in finding_ids:
   397→        if fid in member_ids:
   398→            member_ids.remove(fid)
   399→            count += 1
   400→        # Clear cluster from override
   401→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   402→        if override and override.get("cluster") == cluster_name:
   403→            override["cluster"] = None
   404→            override["updated_at"] = now
   405→
   406→    # Mark auto-clusters as user_modified when items are manually removed
   407→    if count > 0 and cluster.get("auto"):
   408→        cluster["user_modified"] = True
   409→
   410→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   411→    return count
   412→
   413→
   414→def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:
   415→    """Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs."""
   416→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   417→    cluster = plan["clusters"].pop(name, None)
   418→    if cluster is None:
   419→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")
   420→
   421→    orphaned = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   422→    now = utc_now()
   423→    for fid in orphaned:
   424→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   425→        if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   426→            override["cluster"] = None
   427→            override["updated_at"] = now
   428→
   429→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   430→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   431→
   432→    return orphaned
   433→
   434→
   435→def merge_clusters(
   436→    plan: PlanModel, source_name: str, target_name: str
   437→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   438→    """Move all source findings to target, copy missing metadata, delete source.
   439→
   440→    Returns ``(added_count, source_finding_ids)``.
   441→    """
   442→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   443→    if source_name == target_name:
   444→        raise ValueError("Cannot merge a cluster into itself")
   445→    source = plan["clusters"].get(source_name)
   446→    if source is None:
   447→        raise ValueError(f"Source cluster {source_name!r} does not exist")
   448→    target = plan["clusters"].get(target_name)
   449→    if target is None:
   450→        raise ValueError(f"Target cluster {target_name!r} does not exist")
   451→
   452→    source_ids = list(source.get("finding_ids", []))
   453→    target_ids: list[str] = target["finding_ids"]
   454→    now = utc_now()
   455→
   456→    # Add source findings to target (deduplicate)
   457→    existing = set(target_ids)
   458→    added = 0
   459→    for fid in source_ids:
   460→        if fid not in existing:
   461→            target_ids.append(fid)
   462→            existing.add(fid)
   463→            added += 1
   464→        # Update override to point to target cluster
   465→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   466→        if fid not in overrides:
   467→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   468→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = target_name
   469→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   470→
   471→    # Copy metadata from source if target is missing them
   472→    if not target.get("description") and source.get("description"):
   473→        target["description"] = source["description"]
   474→    if not target.get("action_steps") and source.get("action_steps"):
   475→        target["action_steps"] = list(source["action_steps"])
   476→    if not target.get("action") and source.get("action"):
   477→        target["action"] = source["action"]
   478→
   479→    target["updated_at"] = now
   480→
   481→    # Delete source cluster
   482→    plan["clusters"].pop(source_name, None)
   483→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == source_name:
   484→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   485→
   486→    return added, source_ids
   487→
   488→
   489→def move_cluster(
   490→    plan: PlanModel,
   491→    cluster_name: str,
   492→    position: str,
   493→    target: str | None = None,
   494→    offset: int | None = None,
   495→) -> int:
   496→    """Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved."""
   497→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   498→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   499→    if cluster is None:
   500→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   501→
   502→    member_ids = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   503→    if not member_ids:
   504→        return 0
   505→
   506→    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)
   507→
   508→
   509→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   510→# Focus
   511→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   512→
   513→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
   514→    """Set the active cluster focus."""
   515→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   516→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
   517→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   518→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
   519→
   520→
   521→def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   522→    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
   523→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   524→    plan["active_cluster"] = None
   525→

   527→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   528→# Reset
   529→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   530→
   531→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   532→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp."""
   533→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
   534→    plan.clear()
   535→    for k, v in empty_plan().items():
   536→        plan[k] = v
   537→    plan["created"] = created
   538→
   539→
   540→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   541→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   542→
   543→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   544→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   545→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   546→    """
   547→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   548→    found = 0
   549→
   550→    purge_set = set(finding_ids)
   551→    promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   552→    plan["promoted_ids"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in purge_set]
   553→
   554→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   555→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   556→    for fid in finding_ids:
   557→        was_present = False
   558→        if fid in order:
   559→            order.remove(fid)
   560→            was_present = True
   561→        if fid in skipped:
   562→            skipped.pop(fid)
   563→            was_present = True
   564→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   565→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   566→            if fid in ids:
   567→                ids.remove(fid)
   568→                was_present = True
   569→        # Clear stale cluster reference from override (preserve notes/descriptions)
   570→        override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   571→        if override and override.get("cluster"):
   572→            override["cluster"] = None
   573→            override["updated_at"] = utc_now()
   574→        if was_present:
   575→            found += 1
   576→
   577→    return found
   578→
   579→
   580→__all__ = [
   581→    "add_to_cluster",
   582→    "annotate_finding",
   583→    "append_log_entry",
   584→    "clear_focus",
   585→    "create_cluster",
   586→    "delete_cluster",
   587→    "describe_finding",
   588→    "merge_clusters",
```

```
   192→def utc_now() -> str:
   193→    """Return current UTC timestamp with second-level precision."""
   194→    return datetime.now(UTC).isoformat(timespec="seconds")
   195→
   196→
   197→def empty_state() -> StateModel:
   198→    """Return a new empty state payload."""
   199→    return {
   200→        "version": CURRENT_VERSION,
   201→        "created": utc_now(),
   202→        "last_scan": None,
   203→        "scan_count": 0,
   204→        "overall_score": 0,
   205→        "objective_score": 0,
   206→        "strict_score": 0,
   207→        "verified_strict_score": 0,
   208→        "stats": {},
   209→        "findings": {},
   210→        "scan_coverage": {},
   211→        "score_confidence": {},
   212→        "subjective_integrity": {},
   213→        "subjective_assessments": {},
   214→    }
   215→
   216→
   217→def _as_non_negative_int(value: Any, default: int = 0) -> int:
   218→    try:
   219→        parsed = int(value)
   220→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
   221→        return default
   222→    return parsed if parsed >= 0 else 0
   223→
   224→
   225→def ensure_state_defaults(state: StateModel | dict) -> None:
   226→    """Normalize loose/legacy state payloads to a valid base shape in-place."""
   227→    for key, value in empty_state().items():
   228→        state.setdefault(key, value)
   229→
   230→    if not isinstance(state.get("findings"), dict):
   231→        state["findings"] = {}
   232→    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
   233→        state["stats"] = {}
   234→    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_history"), list):
   235→        state["scan_history"] = []
   236→    if not isinstance(state.get("scan_coverage"), dict):
   237→        state["scan_coverage"] = {}
   238→    if not isinstance(state.get("score_confidence"), dict):
   239→        state["score_confidence"] = {}
   240→    if not isinstance(state.get("subjective_integrity"), dict):
   241→        state["subjective_integrity"] = {}
   242→
   243→    findings = state["findings"]
   244→    to_remove: list[str] = []
   245→    for finding_id, finding in findings.items():
   246→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   247→            to_remove.append(finding_id)
   248→            continue
   249→
   250→        finding.setdefault("id", finding_id)
   251→        finding.setdefault("detector", "unknown")
   252→        finding.setdefault("file", "")
   253→        finding.setdefault("tier", 3)
   254→        finding.setdefault("confidence", "low")
   255→        finding.setdefault("summary", "")
   256→        finding.setdefault("detail", {})
   257→        finding.setdefault("status", "open")
   258→        finding["status"] = canonical_finding_status(
   259→            finding.get("status"),
   260→            default="open",
   261→        )
   262→        finding.setdefault("note", None)
   263→        finding.setdefault("first_seen", state.get("created") or utc_now())
   264→        finding.setdefault("last_seen", finding["first_seen"])
   265→        finding.setdefault("resolved_at", None)
   266→        finding["reopen_count"] = _as_non_negative_int(
   267→            finding.get("reopen_count", 0), default=0
   268→        )
   269→        finding.setdefault("suppressed", False)
   270→        finding.setdefault("suppressed_at", None)
   271→        finding.setdefault("suppression_pattern", None)
   272→
   273→    for finding_id in to_remove:
   274→        findings.pop(finding_id, None)
   275→
   276→    for entry in state["scan_history"]:
   277→        if not isinstance(entry, dict):
   278→            continue
   279→        integrity = entry.get("subjective_integrity")
   280→        if integrity is not None and not isinstance(integrity, dict):
   281→            entry["subjective_integrity"] = None
   282→
   283→    state["scan_count"] = _as_non_negative_int(state.get("scan_count", 0), default=0)
   284→    return None
   285→
   286→
   287→def validate_state_invariants(state: StateModel) -> None:
   288→    """Raise ValueError when core state invariants are violated."""
   289→    if not isinstance(state.get("findings"), dict):
   290→        raise ValueError("state.findings must be a dict")
   291→    if not isinstance(state.get("stats"), dict):
   292→        raise ValueError("state.stats must be a dict")
   293→
   294→    findings = state["findings"]
   295→    for finding_id, finding in findings.items():
   296→        if not isinstance(finding, dict):
   297→            raise ValueError(f"finding {finding_id!r} must be a dict")
   298→        if finding.get("id") != finding_id:
   299→            raise ValueError(f"finding id mismatch for {finding_id!r}")
   300→        if finding.get("status") not in _ALLOWED_FINDING_STATUSES:
   301→            raise ValueError(
   302→                f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid status {finding.get('status')!r}"
   303→            )
   304→
   305→        tier = finding.get("tier")
   306→        if not isinstance(tier, int) or tier < 1 or tier > 4:
   307→            raise ValueError(f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid tier {tier!r}")
   308→
   309→        reopen_count = finding.get("reopen_count")
   310→        if not isinstance(reopen_count, int) or reopen_count < 0:
   311→            raise ValueError(
   312→                f"finding {finding_id!r} has invalid reopen_count {reopen_count!r}"
   313→            )
   314→
   315→
```

```
    92→def triage_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel) -> str:
    93→    """Return a banner string when triage stage IDs are in the queue."""
    94→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    95→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    96→    has_triage = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
    97→    if not has_triage:
    98→        return ""
    99→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   100→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   101→    completed = [s for s in ("observe", "reflect", "organize") if s in stages]
   102→    if completed:
   103→        return (
   104→            f"TRIAGE MODE ({len(completed)}/4 stages complete) — "
   105→            "complete all stages to exit. Run: desloppify plan triage"
   106→        )
   107→    return (
   108→        "TRIAGE MODE — review findings need analysis before fixing. "
   109→        "Run: desloppify plan triage"
   110→    )
```

```
1→"""State-application helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query_best_effort as _write_query_best_effort
     9→from desloppify.core.output_contract import OutputResult
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan
    11→
    12→from .selection import ResolveQueryContext
    13→
    14→
    15→def _try_expand_cluster(pattern: str) -> list[str] | None:
    16→    """If pattern matches a cluster name, return its finding IDs."""
    17→    try:
    18→        if not has_living_plan():
    19→            return None
    20→        plan = load_plan()
    21→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(pattern)
    22→        if cluster and cluster.get("finding_ids"):
    23→            return list(cluster["finding_ids"])
    24→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as exc:
    25→        _ = exc
    26→    return None
    27→
    28→
    29→def _resolve_all_patterns(
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    32→    *,
    33→    attestation: str | None,
    34→) -> list[str]:
    35→    all_resolved: list[str] = []
    36→    for pattern in args.patterns:
    37→        # Check if pattern is a cluster name — expand to member IDs
    38→        cluster_ids = _try_expand_cluster(pattern)
    39→        if cluster_ids:
    40→            for fid in cluster_ids:
    41→                resolved = state_mod.resolve_findings(
    42→                    state,
    43→                    fid,
    44→                    args.status,
    45→                    args.note,
    46→                    attestation=attestation,
    47→                )
    48→                all_resolved.extend(resolved)
    49→            continue
    50→
    51→        resolved = state_mod.resolve_findings(
    52→            state,
    53→            pattern,
    54→            args.status,
    55→            args.note,
    56→            attestation=attestation,
    57→        )
    58→        all_resolved.extend(resolved)
    59→    return all_resolved
    60→
    61→
    62→def write_query(payload: dict) -> OutputResult:
    63→    """Backward-compatible resolve query writer seam."""
    64→    return _write_query_best_effort(
    65→        payload,
    66→        context="resolve query payload update",
    67→    )
    68→
    69→
    70→def _write_resolve_query_entry(context: ResolveQueryContext) -> None:
    71→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(context.state)
    72→    write_query(
    73→        {
    74→            "command": "resolve",
    75→            "patterns": context.patterns,
    76→            "status": context.status,
    77→            "resolved": context.resolved,
    78→            "count": len(context.resolved),
    79→            "next_command": context.next_command,
    80→            "overall_score": scores.overall,
    81→            "objective_score": scores.objective,
    82→            "strict_score": scores.strict,
    83→            "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
    84→            "prev_overall_score": context.prev_overall,
    85→            "prev_objective_score": context.prev_objective,
    86→            "prev_strict_score": context.prev_strict,
    87→            "prev_verified_strict_score": context.prev_verified,
    88→            "attestation": context.attestation,
    89→            "narrative": context.narrative,
    90→        }
    91→    )
    92→
```

```
    29→def _render_item(
    30→    item: dict, dim_scores: dict, findings_scoped: dict, explain: bool,
    31→    potentials: dict | None = None,
    32→) -> None:
    33→    # Delegate to cluster renderer for cluster meta-items
    34→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    35→        _render_cluster_item(item)
    36→        return
    37→
    38→    # Workflow stage item (triage stages)
    39→    if item.get("kind") == "workflow_stage":
    40→        blocked = item.get("is_blocked", False)
    41→        stage = item.get("stage_name", "")
    42→        tag = " [blocked]" if blocked else ""
    43→        style = "dim" if blocked else "bold"
    44→        print(colorize(f"  (Planning stage: {stage}{tag})", style))
    45→        print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
    46→        print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
    47→        detail = item.get("detail", {})
    48→        total = detail.get("total_review_findings", 0)
    49→        if total:
    50→            print(colorize(f"  {total} review findings to analyze", "dim"))
    51→        if blocked:
    52→            blocked_by = item.get("blocked_by", [])
    53→            deps = ", ".join(b.replace("triage::", "") for b in blocked_by)
    54→            print(colorize(f"  Blocked by: {deps}", "dim"))
    55→            # Show the unblocking action
    56→            first_dep = blocked_by[0] if blocked_by else ""
    57→            dep_name = first_dep.replace("triage::", "")
    58→            if dep_name:
    59→                print(colorize(f"  Next step: desloppify plan triage --stage {dep_name}", "dim"))
    60→        else:
    61→            print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {item.get('primary_command', '')}", "cyan"))
    62→        return
    63→
    64→    # Workflow action item (e.g. create-plan)
    65→    if item.get("kind") == "workflow_action":
    66→        print(colorize("  (Workflow step)", "bold"))
    67→        print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
    68→        print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
    69→        print(colorize(f"\n  Action: {item.get('primary_command', '')}", "cyan"))
    70→        return
    71→
    72→    confidence = item.get("confidence", "medium")
    73→    print(colorize(f"  ({confidence} confidence)", "bold"))
    74→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 60, "dim"))
    75→    print(f"  {colorize(item.get('summary', ''), 'yellow')}")
    76→
    77→    # Effort/type label
    78→    if item.get("detector") == "review":
    79→        print(colorize("  Type: Design review (requires judgment)", "dim"))
    80→    elif is_auto_fix_command(item.get("primary_command")):
    81→        print(colorize("  Type: Auto-fixable", "dim"))
    82→
    83→    kind = item.get("kind", "finding")
    84→    if kind == "subjective_dimension":
    85→        detail = item.get("detail", {})
    86→        subjective_score = float(
    87→            detail.get("strict_score", item.get("subjective_score", 100.0))
    88→        )
    89→        print(f"  Dimension: {detail.get('dimension_name', 'unknown')}")
    90→        print(f"  Score: {subjective_score:.1f}%")
    91→        print(
    92→            colorize(
    93→                f"  Action: {item.get('primary_command', 'desloppify review --prepare')}",
    94→                "cyan",
    95→            )
    96→        )
    97→        print(colorize(
    98→            "  Note: re-review scores what it finds — scores can go down if issues are discovered.",
    99→            "dim",
   100→        ))
```
