1→"""Output helpers for the `next` command."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
     7→from typing import Any
     8→
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_write_error
    10→
    11→_CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT = 25
    12→
    13→
    14→def _serialize_cluster_member(member: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    15→    """Serialize a cluster member without nested plan metadata."""
    16→    return {
    17→        "id": member.get("id"),
    18→        "confidence": member.get("confidence"),
    19→        "detector": member.get("detector"),
    20→        "file": member.get("file"),
    21→        "summary": member.get("summary"),
    22→        "status": member.get("status"),
    23→        "primary_command": member.get("primary_command"),
    24→    }
    25→
    26→
    27→def serialize_item(item: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    28→    """Build a serializable output dict from a queue item."""
    29→    # Cluster meta-items get their own serialization
    30→    if item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    31→        members_raw = item.get("members", [])
    32→        serialized_members = [
    33→            _serialize_cluster_member(member)
    34→            for member in members_raw[:_CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT]
    35→        ]
    36→        member_count = int(item.get("member_count", len(members_raw)))
    37→        serialized_cluster: dict[str, Any] = {
    38→            "id": item.get("id"),
    39→            "kind": "cluster",
    40→            "action_type": item.get("action_type", "manual_fix"),
    41→            "summary": item.get("summary"),
    42→            "member_count": member_count,
    43→            "members": serialized_members,
    44→            "cluster_name": item.get("cluster_name", item.get("id")),
    45→            "cluster_auto": item.get("cluster_auto", True),
    46→            "detector": item.get("detector"),
    47→        }
    48→        if member_count > len(serialized_members):
    49→            serialized_cluster["members_truncated"] = True
    50→            serialized_cluster["members_sample_limit"] = _CLUSTER_MEMBER_SAMPLE_LIMIT
    51→        serialized_cluster["primary_command"] = item.get("primary_command")
    52→        return serialized_cluster
    53→
    54→    serialized: dict[str, Any] = {
    55→        "id": item.get("id"),
    56→        "kind": item.get("kind", "finding"),
    57→        "confidence": item.get("confidence"),
    58→        "detector": item.get("detector"),
    59→        "file": item.get("file"),
    60→        "summary": item.get("summary"),
    61→        "detail": item.get("detail", {}),
    62→        "status": item.get("status"),
    63→    }
    64→    serialized["primary_command"] = item.get("primary_command")
    65→
    66→    # Workflow dependency state
    67→    if item.get("blocked_by"):
    68→        serialized["blocked_by"] = item["blocked_by"]
    69→    if item.get("is_blocked"):
    70→        serialized["is_blocked"] = True
    71→
    72→    explain = item.get("explain")
    73→    if explain is not None:
    74→        serialized["explain"] = explain
    75→
    76→    # Plan metadata
    77→    if item.get("queue_position"):
    78→        serialized["queue_position"] = item["queue_position"]
    79→    if item.get("plan_description"):
    80→        serialized["plan_description"] = item["plan_description"]
    81→    if item.get("plan_note"):
    82→        serialized["plan_note"] = item["plan_note"]
    83→    if item.get("plan_cluster"):
    84→        serialized["plan_cluster"] = item["plan_cluster"]
    85→    if item.get("plan_skipped"):
    86→        serialized["plan_skipped"] = True
    87→        serialized["plan_skip_kind"] = item.get("plan_skip_kind", "temporary")
    88→        if item.get("plan_skip_reason"):
    89→            serialized["plan_skip_reason"] = item["plan_skip_reason"]
    90→
    91→    return serialized
    92→
    93→
    94→def build_query_payload(
    95→    queue: Mapping[str, Any],
    96→    items: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
    97→    *,
    98→    command: str,
    99→    narrative: Mapping[str, Any] | None,
   100→    plan: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,

     2→

    29→def _print_pattern_hints() -> None:
    30→    """Print valid pattern format hints after a no-match error."""
    31→    print(colorize("  Valid patterns:", "dim"))
    32→    print(colorize("    f41b3eb7              (8-char hash suffix from dashboard)", "dim"))
    33→    print(colorize("    review::path::name    (ID prefix)", "dim"))
    34→    print(colorize("    review                (all findings from detector)", "dim"))
    35→    print(colorize("    src/foo.py            (all findings in file)", "dim"))
    36→    print(colorize("    timing_attack         (finding name — last ::segment of ID)", "dim"))
    37→    print(colorize("    review::*naming*      (glob pattern)", "dim"))
    38→    print(colorize("    my-cluster            (cluster name — expands to members)", "dim"))
    39→
    40→
    41→def _cmd_cluster_create(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    42→    name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    43→    description: str | None = getattr(args, "description", None)
    44→    action: str | None = getattr(args, "action", None)
    45→    plan = load_plan()
    46→    try:
    47→        create_cluster(plan, name, description, action=action)
    48→    except ValueError as ex:
    49→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    50→        return
    51→    append_log_entry(
    52→        plan, "cluster_create", cluster_name=name, actor="user",
    53→        detail={"description": description, "action": action},
    54→    )
    55→    save_plan(plan)
    56→    print(colorize(f"  Created cluster: {name}", "green"))
    57→
    58→
    59→def _cmd_cluster_add(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    60→    state = command_runtime(args).state
    61→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    62→        return
    63→
    64→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
    65→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    66→
    67→    plan = load_plan()
    68→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
    69→    if not finding_ids:
    70→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
    71→        _print_pattern_hints()
    72→        return
    73→    try:
    74→        count = add_to_cluster(plan, cluster_name, finding_ids)
    75→    except ValueError as ex:
    76→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
    77→        return
    78→
    79→    # Check for overlap with other manual clusters
    80→    member_set = set(finding_ids)
    81→    for other_name, other_cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items():
    82→        if other_name == cluster_name or other_cluster.get("auto"):
    83→            continue
    84→        other_ids = set(other_cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    85→        if not other_ids:
    86→            continue
    87→        overlap = member_set & other_ids
    88→        if len(overlap) > len(other_ids) * 0.5:
    89→            print(colorize(
    90→                f"  Warning: {len(overlap)} finding(s) also in cluster '{other_name}' "
    91→                f"({len(overlap)}/{len(other_ids)} = {int(len(overlap)/len(other_ids)*100)}% overlap).",
    92→                "yellow",
    93→            ))
    94→
    95→    append_log_entry(
    96→        plan, "cluster_add", finding_ids=finding_ids, cluster_name=cluster_name, actor="user",
    97→    )
    98→    save_plan(plan)
    99→    print(colorize(f"  Added {count} item(s) to cluster {cluster_name}.", "green"))
   100→
   101→

   103→    state = command_runtime(args).state
   104→    if not require_completed_scan(state):

   112→    if not finding_ids:

   123→    )

   126→
   127→
   128→def _cmd_cluster_delete(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   129→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   130→    plan = load_plan()
   131→    try:
   132→        orphaned = delete_cluster(plan, cluster_name)
   133→    except ValueError as ex:
   134→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   135→        return
   136→    append_log_entry(
   137→        plan, "cluster_delete", finding_ids=orphaned, cluster_name=cluster_name, actor="user",
   138→    )
   139→    save_plan(plan)
   140→    print(colorize(f"  Deleted cluster {cluster_name} ({len(orphaned)} items orphaned).", "green"))
   141→
   142→
   143→def _cmd_cluster_move(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   144→    raw_names: str = getattr(args, "cluster_names", "") or getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   145→    cluster_names: list[str] = [n.strip() for n in raw_names.split(",") if n.strip()]
   146→    position: str = getattr(args, "position", "top")
   147→    target: str | None = getattr(args, "target", None)
   148→
   149→    plan = load_plan()
   150→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   151→
   152→    # Validate all names exist
   153→    for name in cluster_names:
   154→        if name not in clusters:
   155→            print(colorize(f"  Cluster {name!r} does not exist.", "red"))
   156→            return
   157→
   158→    # Resolve cluster-name targets for before/after
   159→    target = resolve_target(plan, target, position)
   160→
   161→    offset: int | None = None
   162→    if position in ("up", "down") and target is not None:
   163→        try:
   164→            offset = int(target)
   165→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   166→            print(colorize(f"  Invalid offset: {target}", "red"))
   167→            return
   168→        target = None
   169→
   170→    # Collect all member IDs from all clusters, preserving order, deduplicating
   171→    seen: set[str] = set()
   172→    all_member_ids: list[str] = []
   173→    for name in cluster_names:
   174→        for fid in clusters[name].get("finding_ids", []):
   175→            if fid not in seen:
   176→                seen.add(fid)
   177→                all_member_ids.append(fid)
   178→
   179→    if not all_member_ids:
   180→        print(colorize("  No members in the specified cluster(s).", "yellow"))
   181→        return
   182→
   183→    count = move_items(plan, all_member_ids, position, target=target, offset=offset)
   184→    append_log_entry(
   185→        plan, "cluster_move", cluster_name=",".join(cluster_names), actor="user",
   186→        detail={"position": position, "count": count},
   187→    )
   188→    save_plan(plan)
   189→    label = ", ".join(cluster_names)
   190→    print(colorize(f"  Moved cluster(s) {label} ({count} items) to {position}.", "green"))
   191→
   192→
   193→def _print_cluster_member(idx: int, fid: str, finding: dict | None) -> None:
   194→    """Print a single cluster member line with optional finding details."""
   195→    print(f"    {idx}. {fid}")
   196→    if not finding:
   197→        return
   198→    file = finding.get("file", "")
   199→    lines = finding.get("detail", {}).get("lines", [])
   200→    line_str = f" at lines: {', '.join(str(ln) for ln in lines)}" if lines else ""
   201→    if file:
   202→        print(colorize(f"       File: {file}{line_str}", "dim"))
   203→    summary = finding.get("summary", "")
   204→    if summary:
   205→        print(colorize(f"       {summary}", "dim"))
   206→

   208→def _load_findings_best_effort(args: argparse.Namespace) -> dict:
   209→    """Load findings from state, returning empty dict on failure."""
   210→    rt = command_runtime(args)
   211→    return rt.state.get("findings", {})

   214→def _cmd_cluster_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   215→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   216→    plan = load_plan()
   217→    cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name)
   218→    if cluster is None:
   219→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist.", "red"))
   220→        return
   221→
   222→    # Header
   223→    auto_tag = "Auto-generated" if cluster.get("auto") else "Manual"
   224→    cluster_key = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   225→    key_type = f" ({cluster_key.split('::', 1)[0]})" if cluster_key else ""
   226→    print(colorize(f"  Cluster: {cluster_name}", "bold"))

   231→    action = cluster.get("action") or ""
   232→    if action:

   235→    # Members

   237→    if not finding_ids:
   238→        print(colorize("  Members: (none)", "dim"))

   241→        print(colorize(f"  Members ({len(finding_ids)}):", "dim"))
   242→        for idx, fid in enumerate(finding_ids, 1):

   250→    print(colorize(f"    Skip:         desloppify plan skip {cluster_name}", "dim"))

   253→def _cmd_cluster_list(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   254→    plan = load_plan()
   255→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   256→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")

   269→
   270→def _cmd_cluster_update(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   271→    """Update cluster description and/or action_steps."""
   272→    cluster_name: str = getattr(args, "cluster_name", "")
   273→    description: str | None = getattr(args, "description", None)
   274→    steps: list[str] | None = getattr(args, "steps", None)
   275→
   276→    if description is None and steps is None:
   277→        print(colorize("  Nothing to update. Use --description and/or --steps.", "yellow"))
   278→        return
   279→
   280→    plan = load_plan()
   281→    cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(cluster_name)
   282→    if cluster is None:
   283→        print(colorize(f"  Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist.", "red"))
   284→        return
   285→
   286→    if description is not None:
   287→        cluster["description"] = description
   288→    if steps is not None:
   289→        cluster["action_steps"] = list(steps)
   290→        print(colorize(f"  Stored {len(steps)} action step(s):", "dim"))
   291→        for i, step in enumerate(steps, 1):
   292→            clean = _LEADING_NUM_RE.sub('', step)
   293→            print(colorize(f"    {i}. {clean}", "dim"))
   294→        if len(steps) == 0:
   295→            print(colorize("  Warning: 0 steps stored. Did you forget the --steps values?", "yellow"))
   296→        elif len(steps) == 1 and len(steps[0]) > 100:
   297→            print(colorize("  Warning: only 1 step stored and it's quite long. Check shell quoting.", "yellow"))
   298→    cluster["user_modified"] = True
   299→
   300→    from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
   301→
   302→    cluster["updated_at"] = utc_now()
   303→    append_log_entry(
   304→        plan, "cluster_update", cluster_name=cluster_name, actor="user",
   305→        detail={"description": description, "steps": steps},
   306→    )
   307→    save_plan(plan)
   308→    print(colorize(f"  Updated cluster: {cluster_name}", "green"))
   309→
   310→
   311→def _cmd_cluster_merge(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   312→    """Merge source cluster into target cluster."""
   313→    source: str = getattr(args, "source", "")
   314→    target: str = getattr(args, "target", "")
   315→
   316→    plan = load_plan()
   317→    try:
   318→        added, source_ids = merge_clusters(plan, source, target)
   319→    except ValueError as ex:
   320→        print(colorize(f"  {ex}", "red"))
   321→        return
   322→
   323→    append_log_entry(
   324→        plan, "cluster_merge", finding_ids=source_ids,
   325→        cluster_name=target, actor="user",
   326→        detail={"source": source, "added": added},
   327→    )
   328→    save_plan(plan)
   329→    print(colorize(
   330→        f"  Merged cluster {source!r} into {target!r}: "
   331→        f"{added} finding(s) added, {len(source_ids)} total moved. Source deleted.",
   332→        "green",
   333→    ))
   334→
   335→
   336→def cmd_cluster_dispatch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   337→    """Route cluster subcommands."""
   338→    cluster_action = getattr(args, "cluster_action", None)
   339→    dispatch = {
   340→        "create": _cmd_cluster_create,
   341→        "add": _cmd_cluster_add,
   342→        "remove": _cmd_cluster_remove,
   343→        "delete": _cmd_cluster_delete,
   344→        "move": _cmd_cluster_move,
   345→        "show": _cmd_cluster_show,
   346→        "list": _cmd_cluster_list,
   347→        "update": _cmd_cluster_update,
   348→        "merge": _cmd_cluster_merge,
   349→    }
   350→    handler = dispatch.get(cluster_action)
   351→    if handler is None:
   352→        _cmd_cluster_list(args)
   353→        return
   354→    handler(args)
   355→
   356→
   357→__all__ = ["cmd_cluster_dispatch"]
   358→

1→"""Plan persistence — load/save with atomic writes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import shutil
     8→import sys
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→
    11→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    13→    PLAN_VERSION,
    14→    PlanModel,
    15→    empty_plan,
    16→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    17→    validate_plan,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import STATE_DIR, json_default
    20→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    21→
    22→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    23→
    24→PLAN_FILE = STATE_DIR / "plan.json"
    25→
    26→
    27→def load_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> PlanModel:
    28→    """Load plan from disk, or return empty plan on missing/corruption."""
    29→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    30→    if not plan_path.exists():
    31→        return empty_plan()
    32→
    33→    try:
    34→        data = json.loads(plan_path.read_text())
    35→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as ex:
    36→        # Try backup before giving up
    37→        backup = plan_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
    38→        if backup.exists():
    39→            try:
    40→                data = json.loads(backup.read_text())
    41→                logger.warning("Plan file corrupted (%s), loaded from backup.", ex)
    42→                print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}), loaded from backup.", file=sys.stderr)
    43→                # Fall through to validation below
    44→            except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as backup_ex:
    45→                logger.warning("Plan file and backup both corrupted: %s / %s", ex, backup_ex)
    46→                print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    47→                return empty_plan()
    48→        else:
    49→            logger.warning("Plan file corrupted (%s). Starting fresh.", ex)
    50→            print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    51→            return empty_plan()
    52→
    53→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    54→        logger.warning("Plan file root is not a JSON object. Starting fresh.")
    55→        print("  Warning: Plan file root must be a JSON object. Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    56→        return empty_plan()
    57→
    58→    version = data.get("version", 1)
    59→    if version > PLAN_VERSION:
    60→        logger.warning("Plan file version %d > supported %d.", version, PLAN_VERSION)
    61→        print(
    62→            f"  Warning: Plan file version {version} is newer than supported "
    63→            f"({PLAN_VERSION}). Some features may not work correctly.",
    64→            file=sys.stderr,
    65→        )
    66→
    67→    ensure_plan_defaults(data)
    68→    try:
    69→        validate_plan(data)
    70→    except ValueError as ex:
    71→        logger.warning("Plan invariants invalid (%s). Starting fresh.", ex)
    72→        print(f"  Warning: Plan invariants invalid ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    73→        return empty_plan()
    74→
    75→    return data  # type: ignore[return-value]
    76→
    77→
    78→def save_plan(plan: PlanModel | dict, path: Path | None = None) -> None:
    79→    """Validate and save plan to disk atomically."""
    80→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    81→    plan["updated"] = utc_now()
    82→    validate_plan(plan)
    83→
    84→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    85→    plan_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    86→
    87→    content = json.dumps(plan, indent=2, default=json_default) + "\n"
    88→
    89→    if plan_path.exists():
    90→        backup = plan_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
    91→        try:
    92→            shutil.copy2(str(plan_path), str(backup))
    93→        except OSError as backup_ex:
    94→            logger.debug("Failed to create plan backup: %s", backup_ex)
    95→
    96→    try:
    97→        safe_write_text(plan_path, content)
    98→    except OSError as ex:
    99→        print(f"  Warning: Could not save plan: {ex}", file=sys.stderr)
   100→        raise
   101→
   102→
   103→def plan_path_for_state(state_path: Path) -> Path:
   104→    """Derive plan.json path from a state file path."""
   105→    return state_path.parent / "plan.json"
   106→
   107→
   108→def has_living_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> bool:
   109→    """Return True if a plan.json exists and has user intent."""
   110→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
   111→    if not plan_path.exists():
   112→        return False
   113→    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   114→    return bool(
   115→        plan.get("queue_order")
   116→        or plan.get("overrides")
   117→        or plan.get("clusters")
   118→    )
   119→
   120→
   121→__all__ = [
   122→    "PLAN_FILE",
   123→    "has_living_plan",
   124→    "load_plan",
   125→    "plan_path_for_state",
   126→    "save_plan",
   127→]
   128→

1→"""Auto-clustering algorithm — groups findings into task clusters."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→from collections import defaultdict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.core.registry import DETECTORS, DetectorMeta
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import Cluster, PlanModel, ensure_plan_defaults
    10→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
    11→    SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX,
    12→    _current_stale_ids,
    13→    current_under_target_ids,
    14→    current_unscored_ids,
    15→)
    16→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    17→
    18→AUTO_PREFIX = "auto/"
    19→_MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE = 2
    20→_STALE_KEY = "subjective::stale"
    21→_STALE_NAME = "auto/stale-review"
    22→_UNSCORED_KEY = "subjective::unscored"
    23→_UNSCORED_NAME = "auto/initial-review"
    24→_UNDER_TARGET_KEY = "subjective::under-target"
    25→_UNDER_TARGET_NAME = "auto/under-target-review"
    26→_MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE = 1
    27→
    28→
    29→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    30→# Grouping key computation
    31→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    32→
    33→def _extract_subtype(finding: dict) -> str | None:
    34→    """Extract the subtype/kind from a finding.
    35→
    36→    Checks detail.kind first, then falls back to parsing the finding ID
    37→    (format: ``detector::file::subtype`` or ``detector::file::symbol::subtype``).
    38→    """
    39→    detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    40→    kind = detail.get("kind")
    41→    if kind:
    42→        return kind
    43→
    44→    # Parse from finding ID — third segment is often the subtype
    45→    fid = finding.get("id", "")
    46→    parts = fid.split("::")
    47→    if len(parts) >= 3:
    48→        # For IDs like smells::file.py::silent_except, the subtype is parts[2]
    49→        # For IDs like dict_keys::file.py::key::phantom_read, subtype is last part
    50→        candidate = parts[-1]
    51→        # Skip if it looks like a file path or symbol name (contains / or .)
    52→        if "/" not in candidate and "." not in candidate:
    53→            return candidate
    54→    return None
    55→
    56→
    57→def _grouping_key(finding: dict, meta: DetectorMeta | None) -> str | None:
    58→    """Compute a deterministic grouping key for a finding.
    59→
    60→    Returns None if the finding should not be auto-clustered.
    61→    """
    62→    detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    63→
    64→    if meta is None:
    65→        # Unknown detector — group by detector name
    66→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    67→
    68→    # Review findings → group by dimension
    69→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
    70→        detail = finding.get("detail") or {}
    71→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", "")
    72→        if dimension:
    73→            return f"review::{dimension}"
    74→        return f"detector::{detector}"
    75→
    76→    # Per-file detectors (structural, responsibility_cohesion)
    77→    if meta.needs_judgment and detector in (
    78→        "structural", "responsibility_cohesion",
    79→    ):
    80→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    81→        if file:
    82→            basename = os.path.basename(file)
    83→            return f"file::{detector}::{basename}"
    84→
    85→    # Needs-judgment detectors — group by subtype when available
    86→    if meta.needs_judgment:
    87→        subtype = _extract_subtype(finding)
    88→        if subtype:
    89→            return f"typed::{detector}::{subtype}"
    90→
    91→    # Pure auto-fix (no judgment needed) → all findings by detector
    92→    if meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
    93→        return f"auto::{detector}"
    94→
    95→    # Everything else → by detector
    96→    return f"detector::{detector}"
    97→
    98→
    99→def _cluster_name_from_key(key: str) -> str:
   100→    """Convert a grouping key to a cluster name with auto/ prefix."""
   101→    # auto::unused → auto/unused
   102→    # typed::dict_keys::phantom_read → auto/dict_keys-phantom_read
   103→    # file::structural::big_file.py → auto/structural-big_file.py
   104→    # review::abstraction_fitness → auto/review-abstraction_fitness
   105→    # detector::security → auto/security
   106→    parts = key.split("::")
   107→    if len(parts) == 2:
   108→        prefix_type = parts[0]
   109→        # For review keys, keep the prefix for clarity
   110→        if prefix_type == "review":
   111→            return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}review-{parts[1]}"
   112→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}"
   113→    if len(parts) == 3:
   114→        return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{parts[1]}-{parts[2]}"
   115→    return f"{AUTO_PREFIX}{key.replace('::', '-')}"
   116→
   117→
   118→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   119→# Description and action generation
   120→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   121→
   122→def _generate_description(
   123→    cluster_name: str,
   124→    members: list[dict],
   125→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   126→    subtype: str | None,
   127→) -> str:
   128→    """Generate a human-readable cluster description."""
   129→    count = len(members)
   130→    detector = members[0].get("detector", "") if members else ""
   131→
   132→    if detector in ("review", "subjective_review"):
   133→        detail = (members[0].get("detail") or {}) if members else {}
   134→        dimension = detail.get("dimension", detector)
   135→        return f"Address {count} {dimension} review findings"
   136→
   137→    if detector == "structural":
   138→        files = {os.path.basename(m.get("file", "")) for m in members}
   139→        if len(files) == 1:
   140→            return f"Decompose {next(iter(files))}"
   141→        return f"Decompose {count} large files"
   142→
   143→    display = meta.display if meta else detector
   144→    if subtype:
   145→        label = subtype.replace("_", " ")
   146→        return f"Fix {count} {label} issues"
   147→
   148→    if meta and meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and not meta.needs_judgment:
   149→        return f"Remove {count} {display} findings"
   150→
   151→    return f"Fix {count} {display} issues"
   152→
   153→
   154→def _subtype_has_fixer(meta: DetectorMeta, subtype: str | None) -> str | None:
   155→    """Check if a subtype maps to a specific fixer."""
   156→    if not meta.fixers or not subtype:
   157→        return None
   158→    # Convention: fixer name is subtype with _ replaced by -
   159→    fixer_name = subtype.replace("_", "-")
   160→    if fixer_name in meta.fixers:
   161→        return fixer_name
   162→    # Also check if subtype is a substring (e.g. "unused" matches "unused-imports")
   163→    for fixer in meta.fixers:
   164→        if subtype in fixer:
   165→            return fixer
   166→    return None
   167→
   168→
   169→def _strip_guidance_examples(guidance: str) -> str:
   170→    """Strip subtype-specific examples from guidance text.
   171→
   172→    Many guidance strings have format "verb — specific examples".
   173→    Strip after the dash to get the core action:
   174→      "fix code smells — dead useEffect, empty if chains" → "fix code smells"
   175→    """
   176→    if " — " in guidance:
   177→        return guidance.split(" — ", 1)[0].strip()
   178→    return guidance
   179→
   180→
   181→_ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES: dict[str, str] = {
   182→    "reorganize": "reorganize with desloppify move",
   183→    "refactor": "review and refactor each finding",
   184→    "manual_fix": "review and fix each finding",
   185→}
   186→
   187→
   188→def _generate_action(
   189→    meta: DetectorMeta | None,
   190→    subtype: str | None,
   191→) -> str:
   192→    """Generate an action string from detector metadata.
   193→
   194→    Always returns a non-empty string — every cluster gets an action.
   195→    """
   196→    if meta is None:
   197→        return "review and fix each finding"
   198→
   199→    # For detectors with subtypes, only suggest a fixer if the subtype matches
   200→    if subtype and meta.fixers:
   201→        matched_fixer = _subtype_has_fixer(meta, subtype)
   202→        if matched_fixer:
   203→            return f"desloppify fix {matched_fixer} --dry-run"
   204→    elif meta.action_type == "auto_fix" and meta.fixers and not meta.needs_judgment:
   205→        # Pure auto-fix detector, no subtypes
   206→        return f"desloppify fix {meta.fixers[0]} --dry-run"
   207→
   208→    if meta.tool == "move":
   209→        return "desloppify move"
   210→
   211→    # Guidance available — use it (strip examples for subtyped detectors)
   212→    if meta.guidance:
   213→        if subtype:
   214→            return _strip_guidance_examples(meta.guidance)
   215→        return meta.guidance
   216→
   217→    # Final fallback: action_type template
   218→    return _ACTION_TYPE_TEMPLATES.get(
   219→        meta.action_type, "review and fix each finding"
   220→    )
   221→
   222→
   223→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   224→# Repair
   225→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   226→
   227→def _repair_ghost_cluster_refs(plan: PlanModel, now: str) -> int:
   228→    """Clear override cluster refs that point to non-existent clusters."""
   229→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   230→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   231→    repaired = 0
   232→    for override in overrides.values():
   233→        cluster_name = override.get("cluster")
   234→        if cluster_name and cluster_name not in clusters:
   235→            override["cluster"] = None
   236→            override["updated_at"] = now
   237→            repaired += 1
   238→    return repaired
   239→
   240→
   241→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   242→# Shared create-or-update helper
   243→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   244→
   245→def _sync_auto_cluster(
   246→    plan: PlanModel,
   247→    clusters: dict,
   248→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str],
   249→    *,
   250→    cluster_key: str,
   251→    cluster_name: str,
   252→    member_ids: list[str],
   253→    description: str,
   254→    action: str,
   255→    now: str,
   256→    optional: bool = False,
   257→) -> int:
   258→    """Create or update an auto-cluster and sync its override entries.
   259→
   260→    Handles the common pattern: check if cluster exists by key, update
   261→    membership/metadata if changed, or create a new cluster.  Always syncs
   262→    override entries for all *member_ids*.
   263→
   264→    Returns 1 if a change was made, 0 otherwise.
   265→    """
   266→    changes = 0
   267→    existing_name = existing_by_key.get(cluster_key)
   268→    if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   269→        cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   270→        old_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   271→        new_ids_set = set(member_ids)
   272→        if old_ids != new_ids_set or cluster.get("description") != description or cluster.get("action") != action:
   273→            cluster["finding_ids"] = list(member_ids)
   274→            cluster["description"] = description
   275→            cluster["action"] = action
   276→            cluster["updated_at"] = now
   277→            changes = 1
   278→    else:
   279→        new_cluster: Cluster = {
   280→            "name": cluster_name,
   281→            "description": description,
   282→            "finding_ids": list(member_ids),
   283→            "created_at": now,
   284→            "updated_at": now,
   285→            "auto": True,
   286→            "cluster_key": cluster_key,
   287→            "action": action,
   288→            "user_modified": False,
   289→        }
   290→        if optional:
   291→            new_cluster["optional"] = True
   292→        clusters[cluster_name] = new_cluster
   293→        existing_by_key[cluster_key] = cluster_name
   294→        changes = 1
   295→
   296→    # Sync overrides
   297→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   298→    current_name = existing_by_key.get(cluster_key, cluster_name)
   299→    for fid in member_ids:
   300→        if fid not in overrides:
   301→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   302→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = current_name
   303→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   304→
   305→    return changes
   306→
   307→
   308→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   309→# Main algorithm
   310→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   311→
   312→def auto_cluster_findings(
   313→    plan: PlanModel,
   314→    state: StateModel,
   315→    *,
   316→    target_strict: float = 95.0,
   317→) -> int:
   318→    """Regenerate auto-clusters from current open findings.
   319→
   320→    Returns count of changes made (clusters created, updated, or deleted).
   321→    """
   322→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   323→    changes = 0
   324→
   325→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   326→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   327→
   328→    # Set of finding IDs in manual (non-auto) clusters
   329→    manual_member_ids: set[str] = set()
   330→    for cluster in clusters.values():
   331→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   332→            manual_member_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   333→
   334→    # Collect open, non-suppressed findings and group by key
   335→    groups: dict[str, list[str]] = defaultdict(list)
   336→    finding_data: dict[str, dict] = {}
   337→    for fid, finding in findings.items():
   338→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
   339→            continue
   340→        if finding.get("suppressed"):
   341→            continue
   342→        if fid in manual_member_ids:
   343→            continue
   344→
   345→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
   346→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   347→        key = _grouping_key(finding, meta)
   348→        if key is None:
   349→            continue
   350→
   351→        groups[key].append(fid)
   352→        finding_data[fid] = finding
   353→
   354→    # Drop singleton groups
   355→    groups = {k: v for k, v in groups.items() if len(v) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE}
   356→
   357→    # Map existing auto-clusters by cluster_key
   358→    existing_by_key: dict[str, str] = {}  # cluster_key → cluster_name
   359→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   360→        if cluster.get("auto"):
   361→            ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   362→            if ck:
   363→                existing_by_key[ck] = name
   364→
   365→    now = utc_now()
   366→    active_auto_keys: set[str] = set()
   367→
   368→    for key, member_ids in groups.items():
   369→        active_auto_keys.add(key)
   370→        cluster_name = _cluster_name_from_key(key)
   371→
   372→        # Representative finding for metadata
   373→        rep = finding_data.get(member_ids[0], {})
   374→        detector = rep.get("detector", "")
   375→        meta = DETECTORS.get(detector)
   376→        members = [finding_data[fid] for fid in member_ids if fid in finding_data]
   377→
   378→        # Extract subtype from grouping key (typed::detector::subtype)
   379→        key_parts = key.split("::")
   380→        subtype = key_parts[2] if len(key_parts) >= 3 else None
   381→
   382→        description = _generate_description(cluster_name, members, meta, subtype)
   383→        action = _generate_action(meta, subtype)
   384→
   385→        # User-modified clusters: merge new members only, keep user edits
   386→        existing_name = existing_by_key.get(key)
   387→        if existing_name and existing_name in clusters:
   388→            cluster = clusters[existing_name]
   389→            if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   390→                existing_ids = set(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   391→                new_ids = [fid for fid in member_ids if fid not in existing_ids]
   392→                if new_ids:
   393→                    cluster["finding_ids"].extend(new_ids)
   394→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   395→                    changes += 1
   396→                # Still sync overrides for all members
   397→                overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
   398→                for fid in member_ids:
   399→                    if fid not in overrides:
   400→                        overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   401→                    overrides[fid]["cluster"] = existing_name
   402→                    overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   403→                continue
   404→
   405→        # Handle name collision (existing auto-cluster with different key)
   406→        if cluster_name in clusters and clusters[cluster_name].get("cluster_key") != key:
   407→            cluster_name = f"{cluster_name}-{len(member_ids)}"
   408→
   409→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   410→            plan, clusters, existing_by_key,
   411→            cluster_key=key,
   412→            cluster_name=cluster_name,
   413→            member_ids=member_ids,
   414→            description=description,
   415→            action=action,
   416→            now=now,
   417→        )
   418→
   419→    # --- Subjective dimension clusters (unscored + stale) -------------------
   420→    all_subjective_ids = sorted(
   421→        fid for fid in plan.get("queue_order", [])
   422→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   423→    )
   424→    unscored_state_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   425→    unscored_queue_ids = sorted(
   426→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid in unscored_state_ids
   427→    )
   428→    stale_queue_ids = sorted(
   429→        fid for fid in all_subjective_ids if fid not in unscored_state_ids
   430→    )
   431→
   432→    # -- Initial review cluster (unscored, min size 1) ---------------------
   433→    if len(unscored_queue_ids) >= _MIN_UNSCORED_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   434→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNSCORED_KEY)
   435→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in unscored_queue_ids]
   436→        description = f"Initial review of {len(unscored_queue_ids)} unscored subjective dimensions"
   437→        action = f"desloppify review --prepare --dimensions {','.join(cli_keys)}"
   438→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   439→            plan, clusters, existing_by_key,
   440→            cluster_key=_UNSCORED_KEY,
   441→            cluster_name=_UNSCORED_NAME,
   442→            member_ids=unscored_queue_ids,
   443→            description=description,
   444→            action=action,
   445→            now=now,
   446→        )
   447→
   448→    # -- Stale review cluster (previously scored, min size 2) --------------
   449→    if len(stale_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   450→        active_auto_keys.add(_STALE_KEY)
   451→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in stale_queue_ids]
   452→        description = f"Re-review {len(stale_queue_ids)} stale subjective dimensions"
   453→        action = (
   454→            "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions "
   455→            + ",".join(cli_keys)
   456→        )
   457→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   458→            plan, clusters, existing_by_key,
   459→            cluster_key=_STALE_KEY,
   460→            cluster_name=_STALE_NAME,
   461→            member_ids=stale_queue_ids,
   462→            description=description,
   463→            action=action,
   464→            now=now,
   465→        )
   466→
   467→    # -- Under-target review cluster (optional, current but below target) ----
   468→    # current_under_target_ids already excludes stale and unscored dims.
   469→    # Don't filter through all_subjective_ids — these IDs may not be in
   470→    # queue_order yet (no sync function adds them).  We inject them into
   471→    # queue_order below so they get proper positions.
   472→    under_target_ids = current_under_target_ids(state, target_strict=target_strict)
   473→    stale_state_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   474→    under_target_queue_ids = sorted(under_target_ids)
   475→
   476→    # Prune: remove IDs that were previously in the under-target cluster
   477→    # but are no longer under target (they've improved above threshold).
   478→    prev_ut_cluster = clusters.get(_UNDER_TARGET_NAME, {})
   479→    prev_ut_ids = set(prev_ut_cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   480→    order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   481→    _ut_prune = [
   482→        fid for fid in prev_ut_ids
   483→        if fid not in under_target_ids
   484→        and fid not in stale_state_ids
   485→        and fid not in unscored_state_ids
   486→        and fid in order
   487→    ]
   488→    for fid in _ut_prune:
   489→        order.remove(fid)
   490→        changes += 1
   491→    if len(under_target_queue_ids) >= _MIN_CLUSTER_SIZE:
   492→        active_auto_keys.add(_UNDER_TARGET_KEY)
   493→        cli_keys = [fid.removeprefix(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) for fid in under_target_queue_ids]
   494→        description = (
   495→            f"Consider re-reviewing {len(under_target_queue_ids)} "
   496→            f"dimensions under target score"
   497→        )
   498→        action = (
   499→            "desloppify review --prepare --dimensions "
   500→            + ",".join(cli_keys)
   501→        )
   502→        changes += _sync_auto_cluster(
   503→            plan, clusters, existing_by_key,
   504→            [REDACTED],
   505→            cluster_name=_UNDER_TARGET_NAME,
   506→            member_ids=under_target_queue_ids,
   507→            description=description,
   508→            action=action,
   509→            now=now,
   510→            optional=True,
   511→        )
   512→
   513→        # Ensure under-target IDs are in queue_order (at the back)
   514→        order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   515→        existing_order = set(order)
   516→        for fid in under_target_queue_ids:
   517→            if fid not in existing_order:
   518→                order.append(fid)
   519→
   520→    # Delete stale auto-clusters (no matching group, not user_modified)
   521→    for name in list(clusters.keys()):
   522→        cluster = clusters[name]
   523→        if not cluster.get("auto"):
   524→            continue
   525→        ck = cluster.get("cluster_key", "")
   526→        if ck in active_auto_keys:
   527→            continue
   528→        if cluster.get("user_modified"):
   529→            # Keep user-modified clusters but prune dead member IDs
   530→            alive = [fid for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   531→                     if fid in findings and findings[fid].get("status") == "open"]
   532→            if alive:
   533→                if len(alive) != len(cluster.get("finding_ids", [])):
   534→                    cluster["finding_ids"] = alive
   535→                    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   536→                    changes += 1
   537→                continue
   538→            # All members gone — delete even if user_modified
   539→        # Delete stale cluster
   540→        del clusters[name]
   541→        # Clear cluster refs from overrides
   542→        for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", []):
   543→            override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   544→            if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   545→                override["cluster"] = None
   546→                override["updated_at"] = now
   547→        if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   548→            plan["active_cluster"] = None
   549→        changes += 1
   550→
   551→    # Repair any orphaned override cluster references from prior bugs
   552→    repaired = _repair_ghost_cluster_refs(plan, now)
   553→    changes += repaired
   554→
   555→    plan["updated"] = now
   556→    return changes
   557→
   558→
   559→__all__ = [
   560→    "AUTO_PREFIX",
   561→    "auto_cluster_findings",
   562→]
   563→

1→"""Shared persistence helpers for resolve command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import sys
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.core import config as config_mod
     9→from desloppify.core.fallbacks import print_error
    10→
    11→
    12→def _save_state_or_exit(state: dict, state_file: str) -> None:
    13→    """Persist state with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    14→    try:
    15→        state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
    16→    except OSError as exc:
    17→        print_error(f"could not save state: {exc}")
    18→        sys.exit(1)
    19→
    20→
    21→def _save_config_or_exit(config: dict) -> None:
    22→    """Persist config with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    23→    try:
    24→        config_mod.save_config(config)
    25→    except OSError as exc:
    26→        print_error(f"could not save config: {exc}")
    27→        sys.exit(1)
    28→
    29→
    30→__all__ = ["_save_config_or_exit", "_save_state_or_exit"]

1→"""Helper routines shared by resolve render output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     7→
     8→
     9→def score_snapshot_or_warn(state: dict):
    10→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    11→    if (
    12→        snapshot.overall is None
    13→        or snapshot.objective is None
    14→        or snapshot.strict is None
    15→        or snapshot.verified is None
    16→    ):
    17→        print(colorize("\n  Scores unavailable — run `desloppify scan`.", "yellow"))
    18→        return None
    19→    return snapshot
    20→
    21→
    22→def print_strict_gap_note(status: str, *, overall: float, strict: float) -> None:
    23→    if status != "wontfix":
    24→        return
    25→    strict_gap = round(overall - strict, 1)
    26→    if strict_gap <= 0:
    27→        return
    28→    print(
    29→        colorize(
    30→            f"  Note: wontfix items still count against strict score. "
    31→            f"Current gap: overall {overall:.1f} vs strict {strict:.1f} ({strict_gap:.1f} pts of hidden debt).",
    32→            "yellow",
    33→        )
    34→    )
    35→
    36→
    37→def print_post_resolve_guidance(
    38→    *,
    39→    status: str,
    40→    has_review_findings: bool,
    41→    overall_delta: float,
    42→) -> None:
    43→    if has_review_findings and abs(overall_delta) < 0.05:
    44→        print(
    45→            colorize(
    46→                "  Scores unchanged (review findings don't affect scores directly).",
    47→                "yellow",
    48→            )
    49→        )
    50→        print(
    51→            colorize(
    52→                "  Run `desloppify review --prepare` to get updated assessment scores.",
    53→                "dim",
    54→            )
    55→        )
    56→        return
    57→    if status == "fixed":
    58→        print(
    59→            colorize(
    60→                "  Verified score updates after a scan confirms the finding disappeared.",
    61→                "yellow",
    62→            )
    63→        )
    64→
    65→
    66→__all__ = [
    67→    "print_post_resolve_guidance",
    68→    "print_strict_gap_note",
    69→    "score_snapshot_or_warn",
    70→]
    71→

1→"""Queue-order enforcement helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     6→from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan
     7→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.core import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
     8→
     9→
    10→def _check_queue_order_guard(
    11→    state: dict,
    12→    patterns: list[str],
    13→    status: str,
    14→) -> bool:
    15→    """Warn and block if resolving items not at the front of the plan queue."""
    16→    if status != "fixed":
    17→        return False
    18→    try:
    19→        if not has_living_plan():
    20→            return False
    21→        plan = load_plan()
    22→        queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    23→        if not queue_order:
    24→            return False
    25→
    26→        result = build_work_queue(
    27→            state,
    28→            options=QueueBuildOptions(
    29→                plan=plan,
    30→                count=1,
    31→                collapse_clusters=True,
    32→                include_subjective=True,
    33→            ),
    34→        )
    35→        if not result["items"]:
    36→            return False
    37→
    38→        front_item = result["items"][0]
    39→        front_id = front_item["id"]
    40→
    41→        front_ids: set[str] = set()
    42→        if front_item.get("kind") == "cluster":
    43→            for member in front_item.get("members", []):
    44→                front_ids.add(member["id"])
    45→            front_ids.add(front_id)
    46→        else:
    47→            front_ids.add(front_id)
    48→
    49→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    50→        resolved_ids: set[str] = set()
    51→        for pattern in patterns:
    52→            if pattern in clusters:
    53→                resolved_ids.update(clusters[pattern].get("finding_ids", []))
    54→                resolved_ids.add(pattern)
    55→            else:
    56→                resolved_ids.add(pattern)
    57→
    58→        findings = state.get("findings", {})
    59→        resolved_ids = {
    60→            finding_id
    61→            for finding_id in resolved_ids
    62→            if finding_id in clusters
    63→            or (finding_id in findings and findings[finding_id].get("status") == "open")
    64→        }
    65→        if not resolved_ids:
    66→            return False
    67→
    68→        out_of_order = resolved_ids - front_ids
    69→        for cluster_id in list(out_of_order):
    70→            if cluster_id in clusters:
    71→                alive_members = {
    72→                    finding_id
    73→                    for finding_id in clusters[cluster_id].get("finding_ids", [])
    74→                    if finding_id in findings and findings[finding_id].get("status") == "open"
    75→                }
    76→                if alive_members and alive_members <= front_ids:
    77→                    out_of_order.discard(cluster_id)
    78→        if not out_of_order:
    79→            return False
    80→
    81→        print(colorize("\n  Queue order violation: these items are not next in the plan queue:\n", "yellow"))
    82→        for finding_id in sorted(out_of_order):
    83→            print(f"    {finding_id}")
    84→        print(colorize(f"\n  The current next item is: {front_id}", "dim"))
    85→        print(colorize("  Items must be resolved in plan order. If you need to reprioritize:", "dim"))
    86→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan move <pattern> --position top    # move to front", "dim"))
    87→        print(colorize("    desloppify plan skip <pattern> --reason '...'    # skip for now", "dim"))
    88→        print(colorize("    desloppify next                                  # see what's next\n", "dim"))
    89→        return True
    90→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
    91→        return False
    92→
    93→
    94→__all__ = ["_check_queue_order_guard"]
    95→

1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle finding churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import EPIC_PREFIX, PlanModel, SupersededEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES
    10→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    11→
    12→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    13→
    14→
    15→@dataclass
    16→class ReconcileResult:
    17→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    18→
    19→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    20→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    21→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    22→    changes: int = 0
    23→
    24→
    25→def _find_candidates(
    26→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    27→) -> list[str]:
    28→    """Find open findings that could be remaps for a disappeared finding."""
    29→    candidates: list[str] = []
    30→    for fid, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
    31→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    32→            continue
    33→        if finding.get("detector") == detector and finding.get("file") == file:
    34→            candidates.append(fid)
    35→    return candidates
    36→
    37→
    38→def _is_finding_alive(state: StateModel, finding_id: str) -> bool:
    39→    """Return True if the finding exists and is open."""
    40→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    41→    if finding is None:
    42→        return False
    43→    return finding.get("status") == "open"
    44→
    45→
    46→def _supersede_id(
    47→    plan: PlanModel,
    48→    state: StateModel,
    49→    finding_id: str,
    50→    now: str,
    51→) -> bool:
    52→    """Move a disappeared finding to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    53→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    54→    detector = ""
    55→    file = ""
    56→    summary = ""
    57→    if finding:
    58→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    59→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    60→        summary = finding.get("summary", "")
    61→
    62→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    63→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    64→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != finding_id]
    65→
    66→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    67→        "original_id": finding_id,
    68→        "original_detector": detector,
    69→        "original_file": file,
    70→        "original_summary": summary,
    71→        "status": "superseded",
    72→        "superseded_at": now,
    73→        "remapped_to": None,
    74→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    75→    }
    76→
    77→    # Preserve any existing override note
    78→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
    79→    if override and override.get("note"):
    80→        entry["note"] = override["note"]
    81→
    82→    plan["superseded"][finding_id] = entry
    83→
    84→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, promoted_ids, cluster finding_ids
    85→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    86→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    87→    promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
    88→    if finding_id in order:
    89→        order.remove(finding_id)
    90→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    91→    if finding_id in promoted:
    92→        promoted.remove(finding_id)
    93→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():

   105→def _coerce_target_strict_score(value: object) -> tuple[int, bool]:
   106→    """Coerce target strict score and report whether it is in range."""

   117→

   122→    if p.exists():
   123→        try:
   124→            config = json.loads(p.read_text())
   125→        except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError):
   126→            config = {}
   127→    else:
   128→        # First run — try migrating from state files
   129→        config = _migrate_from_state_files(p)
   130→
   131→    changed = False
   132→
   133→    # Fill missing keys with defaults
   134→    for key, schema in CONFIG_SCHEMA.items():
   135→        if key not in config:
   136→            config[key] = copy.deepcopy(schema.default)
   137→            changed = True
   138→        elif key == "badge_path":
   139→            try:
   140→                normalized = _validate_badge_path(str(config[key]))
   141→                if normalized != config[key]:

   143→                    changed = True
   144→            except ValueError:
   145→                config[key] = copy.deepcopy(schema.default)
   146→                changed = True
   147→
   148→    if changed and p.exists():
   149→        try:
   150→            save_config(config, p)
   151→        except OSError as exc:
   152→            log_best_effort_failure(logger, f"persist migrated config to {p}", exc)
   153→

   155→
   156→
   157→def save_config(config: dict, path: Path | None = None) -> None:
   158→    """Save config to disk atomically."""
   159→    p = path or _default_config_file()
   160→    safe_write_text(p, json.dumps(config, indent=2) + "\n")
   161→
   162→
   163→def add_ignore_pattern(config: dict, pattern: str) -> None:
   164→    """Append a pattern to the ignore list (deduplicates)."""
   165→    ignores = config.setdefault("ignore", [])
   166→    if pattern not in ignores:
   167→        ignores.append(pattern)
   168→
   169→
   170→def add_exclude_pattern(config: dict, pattern: str) -> None:
   171→    """Append a pattern to the exclude list (deduplicates)."""
   172→    excludes = config.setdefault("exclude", [])
   173→    if pattern not in excludes:
   174→        excludes.append(pattern)
   175→
   176→
   177→def set_ignore_metadata(config: dict, pattern: str, *, note: str, added_at: str) -> None:
   178→    """Record note + timestamp for an ignore pattern."""
   179→    meta = config.setdefault("ignore_metadata", {})
   180→    if not isinstance(meta, dict):
   181→        meta = {}
   182→        config["ignore_metadata"] = meta
   183→    meta[pattern] = {"note": note, "added_at": added_at}
   184→

   186→def _validate_badge_path(raw: str) -> str:
   187→    """Require badge_path to point to a filename (root or nested path)."""
   188→    value = raw.strip()
   189→    path = Path(value)
   190→    if (
   191→        not value
   192→        or value.endswith(("/", "\\"))
   193→        or path.name in {"", ".", ".."}
   194→    ):
   195→        raise ValueError(
   196→            "Expected file path for badge_path "
   197→            f"(example: scorecard.png or assets/scorecard.png), got: {raw}"
   198→        )
   199→    return value
   200→
   201→
   202→def _set_int_config_value(config: dict, key: str, raw: str) -> None:
   203→    if raw.lower() == "never":
   204→        config[key] = 0
   205→    else:
   206→        config[key] = int(raw)
   207→    if key != "target_strict_score":
   208→        return

   210→    if not target_valid:

   212→            f"Expected integer {MIN_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE}-{MAX_TARGET_STRICT_SCORE} "
   213→            f"for {key}, got: {raw}"
   214→        )
   215→    config[key] = target_strict_score
   216→
   217→
   218→def _set_bool_config_value(config: dict, key: str, raw: str) -> None:
   219→    normalized = raw.lower()
   220→    if normalized in ("true", "1", "yes"):
   221→        config[key] = True
   222→        return
   223→    if normalized in ("false", "0", "no"):
   224→        config[key] = False
   225→        return
   226→    raise ValueError(f"Expected true/false for {key}, got: {raw}")
   227→
   228→
   229→def _set_str_config_value(config: dict, key: str, raw: str) -> None:
   230→    if key == "badge_path":
   231→        config[key] = _validate_badge_path(raw)
   232→        return
   233→    config[key] = raw
   234→
   235→
   236→def _set_list_config_value(config: dict, key: str, raw: str) -> None:
   237→    config.setdefault(key, [])
   238→    if raw not in config[key]:
   239→        config[key].append(raw)
   240→
   241→
   242→def set_config_value(config: dict, key: str, raw: str) -> None:
   243→    """Parse and set a config value from a raw string.
   244→
   245→    Handles special cases:
   246→    - "never" → 0 for age keys
   247→    - "true"/"false" for bools
   248→    """
   249→    if key not in CONFIG_SCHEMA:
   250→        raise KeyError(f"Unknown config key: {key}")
   251→
   252→    schema = CONFIG_SCHEMA[key]
   253→
   254→    if schema.type is int:
   255→        _set_int_config_value(config, key, raw)
   256→        return
   257→    if schema.type is bool:
   258→        _set_bool_config_value(config, key, raw)
   259→        return
   260→    if schema.type is str:
   261→        _set_str_config_value(config, key, raw)
   262→        return
   263→    if schema.type is list:
   264→        _set_list_config_value(config, key, raw)
   265→        return
   266→    if schema.type is dict:
   267→        raise ValueError(f"Cannot set dict key '{key}' via CLI — use subcommands")
   268→    config[key] = raw
   269→
   270→
   271→def unset_config_value(config: dict, key: str) -> None:
   272→    """Reset a config key to its default value."""
   273→    if key not in CONFIG_SCHEMA:
   274→        raise KeyError(f"Unknown config key: {key}")
   275→    config[key] = copy.deepcopy(CONFIG_SCHEMA[key].default)
   276→
   277→
   278→def config_for_query(config: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
   279→    """Return a sanitized config dict suitable for query.json."""
   280→    return {k: config.get(k, schema.default) for k, schema in CONFIG_SCHEMA.items()}
   281→
   282→
   283→def _merge_config_value(config: dict, key: str, value: object) -> None:
   284→    """Merge a config value into the target dict."""
   285→    if key not in config:
   286→        config[key] = copy.deepcopy(value)
   287→        return
   288→    if isinstance(value, list) and isinstance(config[key], list):
   289→        for item in value:
   290→            if item not in config[key]:
   291→                config[key].append(item)
   292→        return
   293→    if isinstance(value, dict) and isinstance(config[key], dict):
   294→        for dk, dv in value.items():
   295→            if dk not in config[key]:
   296→                config[key][dk] = copy.deepcopy(dv)
   297→        return
   298→
   299→
   300→def _load_state_file_payload(path: Path) -> dict | None:
   301→    try:
   302→        payload = json.loads(path.read_text())
   303→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as exc:
   304→        logger.debug("Skipping unreadable state file %s: %s", path, exc)
   305→        return None
   306→    if isinstance(payload, dict):
   307→        return payload
   308→    return None
   309→
   310→
   311→def _merge_legacy_state_config(config: dict, old_config: dict) -> None:
   312→    for key, value in old_config.items():
   313→        if key not in CONFIG_SCHEMA:
   314→            continue
   315→        _merge_config_value(config, key, value)
   316→
   317→
   318→def _strip_config_from_state_file(path: Path, state_data: dict) -> None:
   319→    if "config" not in state_data:
   320→        return
   321→    del state_data["config"]
   322→    try:
   323→        safe_write_text(path, json.dumps(state_data, indent=2) + "\n")
   324→    except OSError as exc:
   325→        log_best_effort_failure(
   326→            logger,
   327→            f"rewrite state file {path} after config migration",
   328→            exc,
   329→        )
   330→
   331→
   332→def _migrate_single_state_file(config: dict, path: Path) -> bool:
   333→    state_data = _load_state_file_payload(path)
   334→    if state_data is None:
   335→        return False
   336→    old_config = state_data.get("config")
   337→    if not isinstance(old_config, dict) or not old_config:
   338→        return False
   339→
   340→    _merge_legacy_state_config(config, old_config)
   341→    _strip_config_from_state_file(path, state_data)
   342→    return True
   343→
   344→
   345→def _migrate_from_state_files(config_path: Path) -> dict:
   346→    """Migrate config keys from state-*.json files into config.json.
   347→
   348→    Reads state["config"] from all state files, merges them (union for
   349→    lists, merge for dicts), writes config.json, and strips "config" from
   350→    the state files.
   351→    """
   352→    config: dict = {}
   353→    state_dir = config_path.parent
   354→    if not state_dir.exists():
   355→        return config
   356→
   357→    state_files = list(state_dir.glob("state-*.json")) + list(
   358→        state_dir.glob("state.json")
   359→    )
   360→    migrated_any = False
   361→    for sf in state_files:
   362→        migrated_any = _migrate_single_state_file(config, sf) or migrated_any
   363→
   364→    if migrated_any and config:
   365→        try:
   366→            save_config(config, config_path)
   367→        except OSError as exc:
   368→            log_best_effort_failure(
   369→                logger, f"save migrated config to {config_path}", exc
   370→            )
   371→
   372→    return config
   373→

1→"""Path resolution and exclusion matching helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import os
     6→import tempfile
     7→import fnmatch
     8→from pathlib import Path
     9→
    10→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import get_project_root
    11→
    12→
    13→def matches_exclusion(rel_path: str, exclusion: str) -> bool:
    14→    """Check if a relative path matches an exclusion pattern."""
    15→    parts = Path(rel_path).parts
    16→    if exclusion in parts:
    17→        return True
    18→    if "*" in exclusion:
    19→        if any(fnmatch.fnmatch(part, exclusion) for part in parts):
    20→            return True
    21→        # Full-path glob match for patterns with directory separators
    22→        # (e.g. "Wan2GP/**" should match "Wan2GP/models/rf.py").
    23→        if "/" in exclusion or os.sep in exclusion:
    24→            normalized_path = rel_path.lstrip("./")
    25→            if fnmatch.fnmatch(normalized_path, exclusion):
    26→                return True
    27→    if "/" in exclusion or os.sep in exclusion:
    28→        normalized = exclusion.rstrip("/").rstrip(os.sep)
    29→        return rel_path == normalized or rel_path.startswith(normalized + "/") or rel_path.startswith(
    30→            normalized + os.sep
    31→        )
    32→    return False
    33→
    34→
    35→def normalize_path_separators(path: str) -> str:
    36→    return path.replace("\\", "/")
    37→
    38→
    39→def safe_relpath(path: str | Path, start: str | Path) -> str:
    40→    try:
    41→        return os.path.relpath(str(path), str(start))
    42→    except ValueError:
    43→        return str(Path(path).resolve())
    44→
    45→
    46→def rel(path: str) -> str:
    47→    root = get_project_root()
    48→    resolved = Path(path).resolve()
    49→    try:
    50→        return normalize_path_separators(str(resolved.relative_to(root)))
    51→    except ValueError:
    52→        return normalize_path_separators(safe_relpath(resolved, root))
    53→
    54→
    55→def resolve_path(filepath: str) -> str:
    56→    """Resolve a filepath to absolute, handling both relative and absolute."""
    57→    p = Path(filepath)
    58→    if p.is_absolute():
    59→        return str(p.resolve())
    60→    return str((get_project_root() / filepath).resolve())
    61→
    62→
    63→def resolve_scan_file(
    64→    filepath: str | Path,
    65→    *,
    66→    scan_root: str | Path | None = None,
    67→) -> Path:
    68→    """Resolve a scan file path with explicit scan-root-first semantics.
    69→
    70→    Relative file paths are resolved against ``scan_root`` first (when provided)
    71→    and then against the process project root as a fallback.
    72→    """
    73→    p = Path(filepath)
    74→    if p.is_absolute():
    75→        return p.resolve()
    76→
    77→    root = get_project_root()
    78→    if scan_root is not None:
    79→        scan_root_path = Path(scan_root)
    80→        scan_root_abs = (
    81→            scan_root_path.resolve()
    82→            if scan_root_path.is_absolute()
    83→            else (root / scan_root_path).resolve()
    84→        )
    85→        scan_candidate = (scan_root_abs / p).resolve()
    86→        if scan_candidate.exists():
    87→            return scan_candidate
    88→
    89→    return (root / p).resolve()
    90→
    91→
    92→def safe_write_text(filepath: str | Path, content: str) -> None:
    93→    """Atomically write text to a file using temp+rename."""
    94→    p = Path(filepath)
    95→    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    96→    fd, tmp = tempfile.mkstemp(dir=p.parent, suffix=".tmp")
    97→    try:
    98→        with os.fdopen(fd, "w") as f:
    99→            f.write(content)
   100→        os.replace(tmp, str(p))
   101→    except OSError:
   102→        if os.path.exists(tmp):
   103→            os.unlink(tmp)
   104→        raise
   105→
   106→
   107→__all__ = [
   108→    "matches_exclusion",
   109→    "normalize_path_separators",
   110→    "rel",
   111→    "resolve_path",
   112→    "resolve_scan_file",
   113→    "safe_relpath",
   114→    "safe_write_text",
   115→]
   116→
```

    31→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    32→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    33→    # Auto-resolve the create-plan workflow item when plan runs
    34→    plan = load_plan()
    35→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    36→        purge_ids(plan, [WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID])
    37→        save_plan(plan)
    38→    _plan_output_impl(args)

    41→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    42→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    43→    plan = load_plan()
    44→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    45→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    46→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    47→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    48→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    49→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    50→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    51→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    52→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    53→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    54→
    55→    # Count meaningful annotations (descriptions and notes)
    56→    described = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("description"))
    57→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))
    58→
    59→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    60→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    61→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
    62→    if total_skipped:
    63→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    64→    else:
    65→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    66→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    67→    if clusters:
    68→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    69→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    70→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    71→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    72→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    73→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    74→    if described or noted:
    75→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
    76→    if active:
    77→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    78→    if superseded:
    79→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
    80→
    81→
    82→def _cmd_plan_reset(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    83→    """Reset the plan to empty."""
    84→    plan = load_plan()
    85→    queue_len = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    86→    cluster_count = len(plan.get("clusters", {}))
    87→    reset_plan(plan)
    88→    append_log_entry(
    89→        plan, "reset", actor="user",
    90→        detail={"previous_queue_size": queue_len, "previous_cluster_count": cluster_count},
    91→    )
    92→    save_plan(plan)
    93→    print(colorize("  Plan reset to empty.", "green"))
    94→
    95→
    96→def cmd_plan(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    97→    """Dispatch plan subcommand or generate markdown output."""
    98→    plan_action = getattr(args, "plan_action", None)
    99→
   100→    if plan_action is None:
   101→        _cmd_plan_generate(args)
   102→        return
   103→
   104→    if plan_action == "show":
   105→        _cmd_plan_show(args)
   106→        return
   107→
   108→    if plan_action == "queue":
   109→        cmd_plan_queue(args)
   110→        return
   111→
   112→    if plan_action == "reset":
   113→        _cmd_plan_reset(args)
   114→        return
   115→
   116→    if plan_action == "move":
   117→        cmd_plan_move(args)
   118→        return
   119→
   120→    if plan_action in (
   121→        "describe", "note", "focus",
   122→        "skip", "unskip", "reopen", "done",
   123→    ):
   124→        dispatch = {
   125→            "describe": cmd_plan_describe,
   126→            "done": cmd_plan_done,
   127→            "note": cmd_plan_note,
   128→            "focus": cmd_plan_focus,
   129→            "skip": cmd_plan_skip,
   130→            "unskip": cmd_plan_unskip,
   131→            "reopen": cmd_plan_reopen,
   132→        }
   133→        dispatch[plan_action](args)
   134→        return
   135→
   136→    if plan_action == "cluster":
   137→        cmd_cluster_dispatch(args)
   138→        return
   139→
   140→    if plan_action == "triage":
   141→        from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage_handlers import cmd_plan_triage
   142→
   143→        cmd_plan_triage(args)
   144→        return
   145→
   146→    print(f"Unknown plan action: {plan_action}")
   147→
   148→
   149→# Backwards-compatible alias
   150→cmd_plan_output = cmd_plan
   151→
   152→__all__ = ["cmd_plan", "cmd_plan_output"]
   153→
```

    92→def triage_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel) -> str:
    93→    """Return a banner string when triage stage IDs are in the queue."""
    94→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    95→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    96→    has_triage = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
    97→    if not has_triage:

    99→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   100→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   101→    completed = [s for s in ("observe", "reflect", "organize") if s in stages]
   102→    if completed:
   103→        return (
   104→            f"TRIAGE MODE ({len(completed)}/4 stages complete) — "
   105→            "complete all stages to exit. Run: desloppify plan triage"
   106→        )
   107→    return (
   108→        "TRIAGE MODE — review findings need analysis before fixing. "
   109→        "Run: desloppify plan triage"
   110→    )
