```
1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    Cluster,
     7→    ExecutionLogEntry,
     8→    PlanModel,
     9→    SkipEntry,
    10→    empty_plan,
    11→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    14→
    15→
    16→_DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES = 10000
    17→
    18→
    19→def _get_log_cap() -> int:
    20→    """Read execution_log_max_entries from config. Returns 0 for unlimited."""
    21→    try:
    22→        from desloppify.core.config import load_config
    23→
    24→        config = load_config()
    25→        value = config.get("execution_log_max_entries", _DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES)
    26→        return max(0, int(value))
    27→    except (ImportError, OSError, ValueError, TypeError):
    28→        return _DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES
    29→
    30→
    31→def append_log_entry(
    32→    plan: PlanModel,
    33→    action: str,
    34→    *,
    35→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    36→    cluster_name: str | None = None,
    37→    actor: str = "user",
    38→    note: str | None = None,
    39→    detail: dict | None = None,
    40→) -> None:
    41→    """Append a structured entry to the plan's execution log."""
    42→    log = plan.get("execution_log", [])
    43→    entry: ExecutionLogEntry = {
    44→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    45→        "action": action,
    46→        "finding_ids": finding_ids or [],
    47→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
    48→        "actor": actor,
    49→        "note": note,
    50→        "detail": detail or {},
    51→    }
    52→    log.append(entry)
    53→    cap = _get_log_cap()
    54→    if cap > 0 and len(log) > cap:
    55→        plan["execution_log"] = log[-cap:]
    56→
    57→
    58→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    59→# Helpers
    60→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    61→
    62→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    63→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    64→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    65→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    66→    if finding_id in order:
    67→        order.remove(finding_id)
    68→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    69→
    70→
    71→def _resolve_position(
    72→    order: list[str],
    73→    position: str,
    74→    target: str | None = None,
    75→    offset: int | None = None,
    76→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    77→) -> int:
    78→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    79→
    80→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    81→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    82→    """
    83→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    84→
    85→    if position == "top":
    86→        return 0
    87→    if position == "bottom":
    88→        return len(order)
    89→
    90→    if position in {"before", "after"} and target:
    91→        return _resolve_relative_position(order, moving, position=position, target=target)
    92→
    93→    if position in {"up", "down"} and offset is not None:
    94→        return _resolve_offset_position(
    95→            order,
    96→            moving,
    97→            position=position,
    98→            offset=offset,
    99→            finding_ids=finding_ids,
   100→        )
```

```
100→        )
   101→
   102→    return len(order)
   103→
   104→
   105→def _resolve_relative_position(
   106→    order: list[str],
   107→    moving: set[str],
   108→    *,
   109→    position: str,
   110→    target: str,
   111→) -> int:
   112→    for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
   113→        if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
   114→            return i if position == "before" else i + 1
   115→    return 0 if position == "before" else len(order)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _find_index(items: list[str], target: str) -> int | None:
   119→    for i, item_id in enumerate(items):
   120→        if item_id == target:
   121→            return i
   122→    return None
   123→
   124→
   125→def _resolve_offset_position(
   126→    order: list[str],
   127→    moving: set[str],
   128→    *,
   129→    position: str,
   130→    offset: int,
   131→    finding_ids: list[str] | None,
   132→) -> int:
   133→    if not finding_ids:
   134→        return 0 if position == "up" else len(order)
   135→    first_id = finding_ids[0]
   136→    clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
   137→    current_idx = _find_index(clean_order, first_id)
   138→    if current_idx is None:
   139→        if position == "up":
   140→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
   141→        return len(clean_order)
   142→    if position == "up":
   143→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
   144→    return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
   145→
   146→
   147→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   148→# Move items
   149→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   150→
   151→def move_items(
   152→    plan: PlanModel,
   153→    finding_ids: list[str],
   154→    position: str,
   155→    target: str | None = None,
   156→    offset: int | None = None,
   157→) -> int:
   158→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   159→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   160→
   161→    # Triage stage IDs are workflow-managed and cannot be manually reordered
   162→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import TRIAGE_IDS
   163→    finding_ids = [fid for fid in finding_ids if fid not in TRIAGE_IDS]
   164→    if not finding_ids:
   165→        return 0
   166→
   167→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   168→
   169→    # Remove from skipped if present
   170→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   171→    for fid in finding_ids:
   172→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   173→
   174→    # Remove from current position in order
   175→    for fid in finding_ids:
   176→        if fid in order:
   177→            order.remove(fid)
   178→
   179→    # Resolve insertion point
   180→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   181→
   182→    # Insert in original order
   183→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   184→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   185→
   186→    # Track user-promoted IDs only when explicitly moved to the front.
   187→    # Moving to bottom/down/after should not create a promotion barrier.
   188→    if position == "top":
   189→        promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   190→        existing_promoted = set(promoted)
   191→        for fid in finding_ids:
   192→            if fid not in existing_promoted:
   193→                promoted.append(fid)
   194→                existing_promoted.add(fid)
   195→        plan["promoted_ids"] = promoted
   196→
   197→    return len(finding_ids)
   198→
   199→
   200→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   201→# Skip / unskip
   202→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   203→
   204→def skip_items(
   205→    plan: PlanModel,
   206→    finding_ids: list[str],
   207→    *,
   208→    kind: str = "temporary",
   209→    reason: str | None = None,
   210→    note: str | None = None,
   211→    attestation: str | None = None,
   212→    review_after: int | None = None,
   213→    scan_count: int = 0,
   214→) -> int:
   215→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   216→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   217→    now = utc_now()
   218→    count = 0
   219→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   220→    skip_set = set(finding_ids)
   221→    promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   222→    plan["promoted_ids"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in skip_set]
   223→    for fid in finding_ids:
   224→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   225→        skipped[fid] = {
   226→            "finding_id": fid,
   227→            "kind": kind,
   228→            "reason": reason,
   229→            "note": note,
   230→            "attestation": attestation,
   231→            "created_at": now,
   232→            "review_after": review_after,
   233→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
   234→        }
   235→        count += 1
   236→    return count
   237→
   238→
   239→def unskip_items(
   240→    plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]
   241→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   242→    """Bring finding IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   243→
   244→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
   245→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   246→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
   247→    """
   248→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   249→    count = 0
```

```
540→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   541→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   542→
   543→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   544→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   545→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   546→    """
   547→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   548→    found = 0
   549→
   550→    purge_set = set(finding_ids)
   551→    promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   552→    plan["promoted_ids"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in purge_set]
   553→
   554→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   555→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   556→    for fid in finding_ids:
   557→        was_present = False
   558→        if fid in order:
   559→            order.remove(fid)
   560→            was_present = True
   561→        if fid in skipped:
   562→            skipped.pop(fid)
   563→            was_present = True
   564→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   565→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   566→            if fid in ids:
   567→                ids.remove(fid)
   568→                was_present = True
   569→        # Clear stale cluster reference from override (preserve notes/descriptions)
   570→        override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   571→        if override and override.get("cluster"):
   572→            override["cluster"] = None
   573→            override["updated_at"] = utc_now()
   574→        if was_present:
   575→            found += 1
   576→
   577→    return found
   578→
   579→
   580→__all__ = [
   581→    "add_to_cluster",
   582→    "annotate_finding",
   583→    "append_log_entry",
   584→    "clear_focus",
   585→    "create_cluster",
   586→    "delete_cluster",
   587→    "describe_finding",
   588→    "merge_clusters",
   589→    "move_cluster",
   590→    "move_items",
   591→    "purge_ids",
   592→    "remove_from_cluster",
   593→    "reset_plan",
   594→    "resurface_stale_skips",
   595→    "set_focus",
   596→    "skip_items",
   597→    "unskip_items",
   598→]
   599→
```

```
    42→    chronic: bool = False
    43→    explain: bool = False
    44→    plan: dict | None = None
    45→    include_skipped: bool = False
    46→    cluster: str | None = None
    47→    collapse_clusters: bool = True
    48→
    49→
    50→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    51→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    52→
    53→    items: list[dict]
    54→    total: int
    55→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    56→    new_ids: set[str]
    57→
    58→
    59→def build_work_queue(
    60→    state: StateModel,
    61→    *,
    62→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    63→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    64→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
    65→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    66→
    67→    status = resolved_options.status
    68→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
    69→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
    70→    try:
    71→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
    72→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    73→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
    74→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
    75→
    76→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
    77→        state,
    78→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
    79→        status_filter=status,
    80→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
    81→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
    82→    )
    83→
    84→    all_items = list(finding_items)
    85→
    86→    # Count open objective findings for stale-subjective gating
    87→    objective_count = sum(
    88→        1 for item in finding_items
    89→        if item.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    90→    )
    91→
    92→    if (
    93→        resolved_options.include_subjective
    94→        and status in {"open", "all"}
    95→        and not resolved_options.chronic
    96→    ):
    97→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
    98→            state,
    99→            state.get("findings", {}),
   100→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
```

```
    30→    from desloppify.app.commands.viz_cmd import cmd_tree, cmd_viz
    31→    from desloppify.app.commands.zone_cmd import cmd_zone

    51→        "update-skill": cmd_update_skill,
    52→    }
    53→
    54→
    55→def get_command_handlers() -> dict[str, CommandHandler]:
    56→    """Return cached command handler dict, building on first access."""
    57→    global _COMMAND_HANDLERS
    58→    if _COMMAND_HANDLERS is None:
    59→        _COMMAND_HANDLERS = _build_handlers()
    60→    return _COMMAND_HANDLERS
```

```
1→"""Unified work-queue selection for next/show/plan views."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→from typing import TypedDict
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import (
    10→    ALL_STATUSES,
    11→    ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    12→    build_create_plan_item,
    13→    build_subjective_items,
    14→    build_triage_stage_items,
    15→    scope_matches,
    16→)
    17→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.ranking import (
    18→    build_finding_items,
    19→    enrich_with_impact,
    20→    group_queue_items,
    21→    item_explain,
    22→    item_sort_key,
    23→)
    24→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.plan_order import (
    25→    apply_plan_order as _apply_plan_order,
    26→    collapse_clusters as _collapse_clusters,
    27→    new_item_ids as _new_item_ids,
    28→)
    29→from desloppify.state import StateModel
    30→
    31→
    32→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    33→class QueueBuildOptions:
    34→    """Configuration for queue construction."""
    35→
    36→    count: int | None = 1
    37→    scan_path: str | None = None
    38→    scope: str | None = None
    39→    status: str = "open"
    40→    include_subjective: bool = True
    41→    subjective_threshold: float = 100.0
    42→    chronic: bool = False
    43→    explain: bool = False
    44→    plan: dict | None = None
    45→    include_skipped: bool = False
    46→    cluster: str | None = None
    47→    collapse_clusters: bool = True
    48→
    49→
    50→class WorkQueueResult(TypedDict):
    51→    """Typed shape of the dict returned by :func:`build_work_queue`."""
    52→
    53→    items: list[dict]
    54→    total: int
    55→    grouped: dict[str, list[dict]]
    56→    new_ids: set[str]
    57→
    58→
    59→def build_work_queue(
    60→    state: StateModel,
    61→    *,
    62→    options: QueueBuildOptions | None = None,
    63→) -> WorkQueueResult:
    64→    """Build ranked queue items + tier metadata."""
    65→    resolved_options = options or QueueBuildOptions()
    66→
    67→    status = resolved_options.status
    68→    if status not in ALL_STATUSES:
    69→        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported status filter: {status}")
    70→    try:
    71→        subjective_threshold_value = float(resolved_options.subjective_threshold)
    72→    except (TypeError, ValueError):
    73→        subjective_threshold_value = 100.0
    74→    subjective_threshold_value = max(0.0, min(100.0, subjective_threshold_value))
    75→
    76→    finding_items = build_finding_items(
    77→        state,
    78→        scan_path=resolved_options.scan_path,
    79→        status_filter=status,
    80→        scope=resolved_options.scope,
    81→        chronic=resolved_options.chronic,
    82→    )
    83→
    84→    all_items = list(finding_items)
    85→
    86→    # Count open objective findings for stale-subjective gating
    87→    objective_count = sum(
    88→        1 for item in finding_items
    89→        if item.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
    90→    )
    91→
    92→    if (
    93→        resolved_options.include_subjective
    94→        and status in {"open", "all"}
    95→        and not resolved_options.chronic
    96→    ):
    97→        subjective_items = build_subjective_items(
    98→            state,
    99→            state.get("findings", {}),
   100→            threshold=subjective_threshold_value,
   101→        )
   102→        for item in subjective_items:
   103→            if not scope_matches(item, resolved_options.scope):
   104→                continue
   105→            # Stale reruns only surface when objective backlog is zero
   106→            if item.get("stale_review") and objective_count > 0:
   107→                continue
   108→            all_items.append(item)
   109→
   110→    # Inject triage stage items and workflow items when plan requires it
   111→    if resolved_options.plan and status in {"open", "all"}:
   112→        synth_items = build_triage_stage_items(resolved_options.plan, state)
   113→        all_items.extend(synth_items)
   114→        plan_item = build_create_plan_item(resolved_options.plan)
   115→        if plan_item is not None:
   116→            all_items.append(plan_item)
   117→
   118→    enrich_with_impact(all_items, state.get("dimension_scores", {}))
   119→    all_items.sort(key=item_sort_key)
   120→
   121→    # Apply living plan ordering if provided
   122→    new_ids: set[str] = set()
   123→    if resolved_options.plan:
   124→        new_ids = _new_item_ids(state)
   125→        all_items = _apply_plan_order(
   126→            all_items,
   127→            resolved_options.plan,
   128→            include_skipped=resolved_options.include_skipped,
   129→            cluster=resolved_options.cluster,
   130→            new_ids=new_ids,
   131→        )
   132→
   133→    # Collapse auto-clusters into meta-items (unless drilling into a cluster)
   134→    should_collapse = (
   135→        resolved_options.collapse_clusters
   136→        and resolved_options.plan
   137→        and not resolved_options.cluster
   138→        and not resolved_options.plan.get("active_cluster")
   139→    )
   140→    if should_collapse:
   141→        all_items = _collapse_clusters(all_items, resolved_options.plan)
   142→
   143→    total = len(all_items)
   144→    if resolved_options.count is not None and resolved_options.count > 0:
   145→        all_items = all_items[: resolved_options.count]
   146→
   147→    if resolved_options.explain:
   148→        for item in all_items:
   149→            item["explain"] = item_explain(item)
   150→
   151→    return {
   152→        "items": all_items,
   153→        "total": total,
   154→        "grouped": group_queue_items(all_items, "item"),
   155→        "new_ids": new_ids,
   156→    }
   157→
   158→
   159→__all__ = [
   160→    "ATTEST_EXAMPLE",
   161→    "QueueBuildOptions",
   162→    "WorkQueueResult",
   163→    "build_work_queue",
   164→    "group_queue_items",
   165→]
   166→
```

```
1→"""Post-scan plan reconciliation — handle finding churn."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
     6→from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
     7→
     8→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import EPIC_PREFIX, PlanModel, SupersededEntry, ensure_plan_defaults
     9→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES
    10→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    11→
    12→SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS = 90
    13→
    14→
    15→@dataclass
    16→class ReconcileResult:
    17→    """Summary of changes made during reconciliation."""
    18→
    19→    superseded: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    20→    pruned: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    21→    resurfaced: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    22→    changes: int = 0
    23→
    24→
    25→def _find_candidates(
    26→    state: StateModel, detector: str, file: str
    27→) -> list[str]:
    28→    """Find open findings that could be remaps for a disappeared finding."""
    29→    candidates: list[str] = []
    30→    for fid, finding in state.get("findings", {}).items():
    31→        if finding.get("status") != "open":
    32→            continue
    33→        if finding.get("detector") == detector and finding.get("file") == file:
    34→            candidates.append(fid)
    35→    return candidates
    36→
    37→
    38→def _is_finding_alive(state: StateModel, finding_id: str) -> bool:
    39→    """Return True if the finding exists and is open."""
    40→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    41→    if finding is None:
    42→        return False
    43→    return finding.get("status") == "open"
    44→
    45→
    46→def _supersede_id(
    47→    plan: PlanModel,
    48→    state: StateModel,
    49→    finding_id: str,
    50→    now: str,
    51→) -> bool:
    52→    """Move a disappeared finding to superseded. Returns True if changed."""
    53→    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id)
    54→    detector = ""
    55→    file = ""
    56→    summary = ""
    57→    if finding:
    58→        detector = finding.get("detector", "")
    59→        file = finding.get("file", "")
    60→        summary = finding.get("summary", "")
    61→
    62→    candidates = _find_candidates(state, detector, file) if detector else []
    63→    # Don't include the original in candidates
    64→    candidates = [c for c in candidates if c != finding_id]
    65→
    66→    entry: SupersededEntry = {
    67→        "original_id": finding_id,
    68→        "original_detector": detector,
    69→        "original_file": file,
    70→        "original_summary": summary,
    71→        "status": "superseded",
    72→        "superseded_at": now,
    73→        "remapped_to": None,
    74→        "candidates": candidates[:5],
    75→    }
    76→
    77→    # Preserve any existing override note
    78→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
    79→    if override and override.get("note"):
    80→        entry["note"] = override["note"]
    81→
    82→    plan["superseded"][finding_id] = entry
    83→
    84→    # Remove from queue_order, skipped, promoted_ids, cluster finding_ids
    85→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    86→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
    87→    promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
    88→    if finding_id in order:
    89→        order.remove(finding_id)
    90→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    91→    if finding_id in promoted:
    92→        promoted.remove(finding_id)
    93→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    94→        ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
    95→        if finding_id in ids:
    96→            ids.remove(finding_id)
    97→
    98→    # Clear stale cluster reference from override
    99→    override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(finding_id)
   100→    if override and override.get("cluster"):
   101→        override["cluster"] = None
   102→        override["updated_at"] = now
   103→
   104→    return True
   105→
   106→
   107→def _prune_old_superseded(plan: PlanModel, now_dt: datetime) -> list[str]:
   108→    """Remove superseded entries older than TTL. Returns pruned IDs."""
   109→    superseded = plan.get("superseded", {})
   110→    cutoff = now_dt - timedelta(days=SUPERSEDED_TTL_DAYS)
   111→    to_prune: list[str] = []
   112→
   113→    for fid, entry in superseded.items():
   114→        ts = entry.get("superseded_at", "")
   115→        try:
   116→            entry_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(ts)
   117→            if entry_dt.tzinfo is None:
   118→                entry_dt = entry_dt.replace(tzinfo=UTC)
   119→            if entry_dt < cutoff:
   120→                to_prune.append(fid)
   121→        except (ValueError, TypeError):
   122→            to_prune.append(fid)
   123→
   124→    for fid in to_prune:
   125→        superseded.pop(fid, None)
   126→        # Also clean up stale overrides
   127→        plan.get("overrides", {}).pop(fid, None)
   128→
   129→    return to_prune
   130→
   131→
   132→def reconcile_plan_after_scan(
   133→    plan: PlanModel,
   134→    state: StateModel,
   135→) -> ReconcileResult:
   136→    """Reconcile plan against current state after a scan.
   137→
   138→    Finds IDs referenced in the plan that no longer exist or are no longer
   139→    open, moves them to superseded, and prunes old superseded entries.
   140→    """
   141→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   142→    result = ReconcileResult()
   143→    now = utc_now()
   144→    now_dt = datetime.now(UTC)
   145→
   146→    # Collect all finding IDs referenced by the plan
   147→    referenced_ids: set[str] = set()
   148→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   149→    referenced_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
   150→    for override_id in plan.get("overrides", {}):
   151→        referenced_ids.add(override_id)
   152→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   153→        referenced_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   154→
   155→    # Exclude already-superseded IDs and synthetic IDs (managed by stale_dimensions)
   156→    already_superseded = set(plan.get("superseded", {}).keys())
   157→    referenced_ids -= already_superseded
   158→    referenced_ids = {
   159→        fid for fid in referenced_ids
   160→        if not any(fid.startswith(prefix) for prefix in SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES)
   161→    }
   162→
   163→    # Check each referenced ID
   164→    for fid in sorted(referenced_ids):
   165→        if not _is_finding_alive(state, fid):
   166→            if _supersede_id(plan, state, fid, now):
   167→                result.superseded.append(fid)
   168→                result.changes += 1
   169→
   170→    # Reconcile epic clusters: remove dead findings, delete empty epics
   171→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   172→    epic_names_to_delete: list[str] = []
   173→    for name, cluster in list(clusters.items()):
   174→        if not name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX):
   175→            continue
   176→        finding_ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   177→        alive_ids = [fid for fid in finding_ids if _is_finding_alive(state, fid)]
   178→        if alive_ids != finding_ids:
   179→            cluster["finding_ids"] = alive_ids
   180→            result.changes += 1
   181→        if not alive_ids:
   182→            epic_names_to_delete.append(name)
   183→    for name in epic_names_to_delete:
   184→        clusters.pop(name, None)
   185→        result.changes += 1
   186→
   187→    # Resurface stale temporary skips
   188→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   189→    from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import resurface_stale_skips
   190→
   191→    resurfaced = resurface_stale_skips(plan, scan_count)
   192→    if resurfaced:
   193→        result.resurfaced = resurfaced
   194→        result.changes += len(resurfaced)
   195→
   196→    # Prune old superseded entries
   197→    pruned = _prune_old_superseded(plan, now_dt)
   198→    result.pruned = pruned
   199→    result.changes += len(pruned)
   200→
   201→    # Log reconciliation if any changes were made
   202→    if result.changes > 0:
   203→        from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import append_log_entry
   204→
   205→        append_log_entry(
   206→            plan,
   207→            "reconcile",
   208→            finding_ids=result.superseded,
   209→            actor="system",
   210→            detail={
   211→                "superseded_count": len(result.superseded),
   212→                "pruned_count": len(result.pruned),
   213→                "resurfaced_count": len(result.resurfaced),
   214→            },
   215→        )
   216→
   217→    return result
   218→
   219→
   220→@dataclass
   221→class ReviewImportSyncResult:
   222→    """Summary of plan changes after a review import."""
   223→
   224→    new_ids: set[str]
   225→    added_to_queue: list[str]
   226→    triage_injected: bool
   227→
   228→
   229→def sync_plan_after_review_import(
   230→    plan: PlanModel,
   231→    state: StateModel,
   232→) -> ReviewImportSyncResult | None:
   233→    """Sync plan queue after review import. Pure engine function — no I/O.
   234→
   235→    Appends new finding IDs to queue_order and injects triage stages
   236→    if needed. Returns None when there are no new findings to sync.
   237→    """
   238→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   239→        compute_new_finding_ids,
   240→        sync_triage_needed,
   241→    )
   242→
   243→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   244→    new_ids = compute_new_finding_ids(plan, state)
   245→    if not new_ids:
   246→        return None
   247→
   248→    # Add new finding IDs to end of queue_order so they have position
   249→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   250→    existing = set(order)
   251→    added: list[str] = []
   252→    for fid in sorted(new_ids):
   253→        if fid not in existing:
   254→            order.append(fid)
   255→            added.append(fid)
   256→
   257→    # Inject triage stages if needed
   258→    triage_result = sync_triage_needed(plan, state)
   259→    triage_injected = bool(
   260→        triage_result and getattr(triage_result, "injected", False)
   261→    )
   262→
   263→    return ReviewImportSyncResult(
   264→        new_ids=new_ids,
   265→        added_to_queue=added,
   266→        triage_injected=triage_injected,
   267→    )
   268→
   269→
   270→__all__ = [
   271→    "ReconcileResult",
   272→    "ReviewImportSyncResult",
   273→    "reconcile_plan_after_scan",
   274→    "sync_plan_after_review_import",
   275→]
   276→
```

```
1→"""Sync subjective dimensions into the plan queue.
     2→
     3→Two independent sync functions:

    47→    """What changed during a stale-dimension sync."""

    69→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

    72→
    73→def _current_stale_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    74→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently stale."""
    75→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
    76→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
    77→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
    78→    )
    79→
    80→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
    81→    if not dim_scores:
    82→        return set()
    83→
    84→    stale: set[str] = set()
    85→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
    86→        if not entry.get("stale"):
    87→            continue
    88→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
    89→        if dim_key:
    90→            stale.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
    91→    return stale
    92→
    93→
    94→def current_unscored_ids(state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
    95→    """Return the set of ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are currently unscored (placeholder).
    96→
    97→    Checks ``subjective_assessments`` first; when that dict is empty
    98→    (common before any reviews have been run), falls through to
    99→    ``dimension_scores`` which carries placeholder metadata from scan.
   100→    """
   101→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
   102→
   103→    # Primary source: subjective_assessments with placeholder=True
   104→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
   105→    if isinstance(assessments, dict) and assessments:
   106→        unscored: set[str] = set()
   107→        for dim_key, payload in assessments.items():
   108→            if not isinstance(payload, dict):
   109→                continue
   110→            if not payload.get("placeholder"):
   111→                continue
   112→            if dim_key:
   113→                unscored.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
   114→        return unscored
   115→
   116→    # Fallback: check dimension_scores directly for placeholder subjective
   117→    # dimensions.  This handles the common case where subjective_assessments
   118→    # hasn't been populated yet but dimension_scores already has placeholder
   119→    # entries from scan.  We can't use scorecard_subjective_entries() here
   120→    # because the scorecard pipeline intentionally hides placeholders.
   121→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   122→    if not dim_scores:
   123→        return set()
   124→
   125→    unscored = set()
   126→    for _name, data in dim_scores.items():
   127→        if not isinstance(data, dict):
   128→            continue
   129→        detectors = data.get("detectors", {})
   130→        meta = detectors.get("subjective_assessment")
   131→        if not isinstance(meta, dict):
   132→            continue
   133→        if not meta.get("placeholder"):
   134→            continue
   135→        dim_key = meta.get("dimension_key", "")
   136→        if dim_key:
   137→            unscored.add(f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}")
   138→    return unscored
   139→

   141→def current_under_target_ids(
   142→    state: StateModel,
   143→    *,
   144→    target_strict: float = 95.0,
   145→) -> set[str]:
   146→    """Return ``subjective::<slug>`` IDs that are under target but not stale or unscored.
   147→
   148→    These are dimensions whose assessment is still current (not needing refresh)
   149→    but whose score hasn't reached the target yet.
   150→    """
   151→    from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import slugify
   152→    from desloppify.engine.planning.scorecard_projection import (
   153→        scorecard_subjective_entries,
   154→    )
   155→
   156→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {}) or {}
   157→    if not dim_scores:
   158→        return set()
   159→
   160→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   161→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   162→
   163→    under_target: set[str] = set()
   164→    for entry in scorecard_subjective_entries(state, dim_scores=dim_scores):
   165→        if entry.get("placeholder") or entry.get("stale"):
   166→            continue
   167→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
   168→        if strict_val >= target_strict:
   169→            continue
   170→        dim_key = entry.get("dimension_key", "")
   171→        if not dim_key:
   172→            continue
   173→        fid = f"{SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX}{slugify(dim_key)}"
   174→        if fid not in stale_ids and fid not in unscored_ids:
   175→            under_target.add(fid)
   176→    return under_target

   178→
   179→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   180→# Promoted-aware insertion helper
   181→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   182→
   183→def _after_promoted(order: list[str], plan: PlanModel) -> int:
   184→    """Return the insertion index just after the last promoted item in *order*.
   185→
   186→    When no promoted items are present (or none are in *order*), returns 0
   187→    so callers fall back to existing front-of-queue behavior.
   188→    """
   189→    promoted = set(plan.get("promoted_ids", []))
   190→    if not promoted:
   191→        return 0
   192→    last_idx = -1
   193→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   194→        if fid in promoted:
   195→            last_idx = i
   196→    return last_idx + 1 if last_idx >= 0 else 0
   197→
   198→
   199→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   200→# Unscored dimension sync (front of queue, unconditional)
   201→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   202→
   203→def sync_unscored_dimensions(
   204→    plan: PlanModel,
   205→    state: StateModel,
   206→) -> UnscoredDimensionSyncResult:
   207→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions.
   208→
   209→    1. **Prune** — remove ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are
   210→       no longer unscored AND not stale (avoids pruning stale IDs — that is
   211→       ``sync_stale_dimensions``' responsibility).
   212→    2. **Inject** — unconditionally prepend currently-unscored IDs to the
   213→       *front* of ``queue_order`` so initial reviews are the first priority.
   214→    """
   215→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   216→    result = UnscoredDimensionSyncResult()
   217→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   218→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   219→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   220→
   221→    # --- Cleanup: prune subjective IDs that are no longer unscored --------
   222→    # Only prune IDs that are neither unscored nor stale (stale sync owns those).
   223→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   224→        fid for fid in order
   225→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   226→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   227→        and fid not in stale_ids
   228→    ]
   229→    for fid in to_remove:
   230→        order.remove(fid)
   231→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   232→
   233→    # --- Inject: prepend unscored IDs after any promoted items -------------
   234→    existing = set(order)
   235→    insert_at = _after_promoted(order, plan)
   236→    for uid in reversed(sorted(unscored_ids)):
   237→        if uid not in existing:
   238→            order.insert(insert_at, uid)
   239→            result.injected.append(uid)
   240→
   241→    return result
   242→
   243→
   244→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   245→# Stale dimension sync (back of queue, conditional)
   246→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   247→
   248→def sync_stale_dimensions(
   249→    plan: PlanModel,
   250→    state: StateModel,
   251→) -> StaleDimensionSyncResult:
   252→    """Keep the plan queue in sync with stale subjective dimensions.
   253→
   254→    1. Remove any ``subjective::*`` IDs from ``queue_order`` that are no
   255→       longer stale and not unscored (avoids pruning IDs owned by
   256→       ``sync_unscored_dimensions``).
   257→    2. If no objective items remain after cleanup, inject all currently-stale
   258→       dimension IDs so the plan surfaces them as actionable work.
   259→    """
   260→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   261→    result = StaleDimensionSyncResult()
   262→    stale_ids = _current_stale_ids(state)
   263→    unscored_ids = current_unscored_ids(state)
   264→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   265→
   266→    # --- Cleanup: prune resolved subjective IDs --------------------------
   267→    # Only prune IDs that are neither stale nor unscored.
   268→    to_remove: list[str] = [
   269→        fid for fid in order
   270→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX)
   271→        and fid not in stale_ids
   272→        and fid not in unscored_ids
   273→    ]
   274→    for fid in to_remove:
   275→        order.remove(fid)
   276→        result.pruned.append(fid)
   277→
   278→    # --- Inject: populate when no objective items remain -----------------
   279→    has_real_items = any(
   280→        f.get("status") == "open"
   281→        and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   282→        and not f.get("suppressed")
   283→        for f in state.get("findings", {}).values()
   284→    )
   285→    if not has_real_items and stale_ids:
   286→        existing = set(order)
   287→        for sid in sorted(stale_ids):
   288→            if sid not in existing:
   289→                order.append(sid)
   290→                result.injected.append(sid)
   291→
   292→    return result
   293→
   294→
   295→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   296→# Triage snapshot hash + sync
   297→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   298→
   299→def review_finding_snapshot_hash(state: StateModel) -> str:
   300→    """Hash open review finding IDs to detect changes.
   301→
   302→    Returns empty string when there are no open review findings.
   303→    """
   304→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   305→    review_ids = sorted(
   306→        fid for fid, f in findings.items()
   307→        if f.get("status") == "open"
   308→        and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   309→    )
   310→    if not review_ids:
   311→        return ""
   312→    return hashlib.sha256("|".join(review_ids).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
   313→
   314→
   315→@dataclass
   316→class TriageSyncResult:
   317→    """What changed during a triage sync."""
   318→
   319→    injected: bool = False
   320→    pruned: bool = False
   321→
   322→    @property
   323→    def changes(self) -> int:
   324→        return int(self.injected) + int(self.pruned)
   325→
   326→
   327→def sync_triage_needed(
   328→    plan: PlanModel,
   329→    state: StateModel,
   330→) -> TriageSyncResult:
   331→    """Inject 4 triage stage IDs at front of queue when review findings change.
   332→
   333→    Only injects stages not already confirmed in ``epic_triage_meta``.
   334→    Never auto-prunes — only explicit completion removes them.
   335→    """
   336→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   337→    result = TriageSyncResult()
   338→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   339→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   340→    confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   341→
   342→    # Check if any triage stage is already in queue
   343→    already_present = any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS)
   344→
   345→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   346→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   347→
   348→    # If hash changed and no stages present, inject unconfirmed stages
   349→    if current_hash and current_hash != last_hash and not already_present:
   350→        insert_at = _after_promoted(order, plan)
   351→        stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   352→        existing = set(order)
   353→        injected_count = 0
   354→        for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names):
   355→            if name not in confirmed and sid not in existing:
   356→                order.insert(insert_at + injected_count, sid)
   357→                injected_count += 1
   358→        if injected_count:
   359→            result.injected = True
   360→
   361→    return result
   362→
   363→
   364→@dataclass
   365→class CreatePlanSyncResult:
   366→    """What changed during a create-plan sync."""
   367→
   368→    injected: bool = False
   369→
   370→    @property
   371→    def changes(self) -> int:
   372→        return int(self.injected)
   373→
   374→
   375→def sync_create_plan_needed(
   376→    plan: PlanModel,
   377→    state: StateModel,
   378→) -> CreatePlanSyncResult:
   379→    """Inject ``workflow::create-plan`` when reviews complete + objective backlog exists.
   380→
   381→    Only injects when:
   382→    - No unscored (placeholder) subjective dimensions remain
   383→    - At least one objective finding exists
   384→    - ``workflow::create-plan`` is not already in the queue
   385→    - No triage stages are pending
   386→    """
   387→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   388→    result = CreatePlanSyncResult()
   389→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   390→
   391→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in order:
   392→        return result
   393→
   394→    # Don't inject if triage stages are pending
   395→    if any(sid in order for sid in TRIAGE_IDS):
   396→        return result
   397→
   398→    # Check that no unscored dimensions remain
   399→    unscored = current_unscored_ids(state)
   400→    if unscored:
   401→        return result
   402→
   403→    # Check that objective findings exist
   404→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   405→    has_objective = any(
   406→        f.get("status") == "open"
   407→        and f.get("detector") not in NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS
   408→        for f in findings.values()
   409→    )
   410→    if not has_objective:
   411→        return result
   412→
   413→    # Insert after any subjective items, before findings
   414→    insert_at = 0
   415→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   416→        if fid.startswith(SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX) or fid.startswith(TRIAGE_PREFIX):
   417→            insert_at = i + 1
   418→    order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   419→    result.injected = True
   420→    return result
   421→
   422→
   423→def compute_new_finding_ids(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> set[str]:
   424→    """Return the set of open review/concerns finding IDs added since last triage.
   425→
   426→    Returns an empty set when no prior triage has recorded ``triaged_ids``.
   427→    """
   428→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   429→    triaged = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", meta.get("synthesized_ids", [])))
   430→    current = {
   431→        fid for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
   432→        if f.get("status") == "open" and f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns")
   433→    }
   434→    return current - triaged if triaged else set()
   435→
   436→
   437→def is_triage_stale(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> bool:
   438→    """Side-effect-free check: is triage needed?
   439→
   440→    Returns True when any ``triage::*`` stage ID is in the queue OR the
   441→    review-finding snapshot hash has drifted from the last completed triage.
   442→    """
   443→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   444→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   445→    if order & TRIAGE_IDS:
   446→        return True
   447→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   448→    last_hash = meta.get("finding_snapshot_hash", "")
   449→    if not last_hash:
   450→        return False
   451→    current_hash = review_finding_snapshot_hash(state)
   452→    return bool(current_hash and current_hash != last_hash)
   453→
   454→
   455→
   456→__all__ = [
   457→    "NON_OBJECTIVE_DETECTORS",
   458→    "SUBJECTIVE_PREFIX",
   459→    "TRIAGE_ID",
   460→    "TRIAGE_IDS",
   461→    "TRIAGE_PREFIX",
   462→    "TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS",
   463→    "SYNTHETIC_PREFIXES",
   464→    "WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID",
   465→    "WORKFLOW_PREFIX",
   466→    "CreatePlanSyncResult",
   467→    "StaleDimensionSyncResult",
   468→    "TriageSyncResult",
   469→    "UnscoredDimensionSyncResult",
   470→    "current_under_target_ids",
   471→    "current_unscored_ids",
   472→    "compute_new_finding_ids",
   473→    "is_triage_stale",
   474→    "review_finding_snapshot_hash",
   475→    "sync_create_plan_needed",
   476→    "sync_stale_dimensions",
   477→    "sync_triage_needed",
   478→    "sync_unscored_dimensions",
   479→]
   480→
```

```
1→"""Handler for ``plan triage`` subcommand.
     2→
     3→Implements a staged workflow: OBSERVE → REFLECT → ORGANIZE → COMMIT
     4→with a fast-track CONFIRM-EXISTING skip path.
     5→
     6→Stage gates validate **plan data enrichment**, not text reports:
     7→- OBSERVE: lightweight — write an analysis of themes/root causes (100 char min)
     8→- REFLECT: compare current findings against completed work (recurring patterns)
     9→- ORGANIZE: structural — each manual cluster must have description + action_steps
    10→- COMMIT: strategy must be substantive (200 char min) or "same"
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→import argparse
    16→from collections import defaultdict
    17→
    18→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
    19→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import require_completed_scan
    20→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage_playbook import (
    21→    TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES,
    22→    TRIAGE_STAGE_LABELS,
    23→    TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ADD,
    24→    TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_CREATE,
    25→    TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_ENRICH_COMPACT,
    26→    TRIAGE_CMD_CLUSTER_STEPS,
    27→    TRIAGE_CMD_COMPLETE_VERBOSE,
    28→    TRIAGE_CMD_CONFIRM_EXISTING,
    29→    TRIAGE_CMD_OBSERVE,
    30→    TRIAGE_CMD_ORGANIZE,
    31→    TRIAGE_CMD_REFLECT,
    32→)
    33→from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage.shared import _unenriched_clusters
    34→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.display import short_finding_id
    35→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    36→from desloppify.engine._plan.epic_triage import (
    37→    build_triage_prompt,
    38→    collect_triage_input,
    39→    detect_recurring_patterns,
    40→    extract_finding_citations,
    41→)
    42→from desloppify.engine._plan.operations import append_log_entry
    43→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import review_finding_snapshot_hash
    44→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    45→from desloppify.engine.plan import (
    46→    TRIAGE_IDS,
    47→    TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
    48→    load_plan,
    49→    purge_ids,
    50→    save_plan,
    51→)
    52→
    53→
    54→_STAGE_ORDER = ["observe", "reflect", "organize"]
    55→
    56→
    57→def _has_triage_in_queue(plan: dict) -> bool:
    58→    """Check if any triage stage ID is in the queue."""
    59→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    60→    return bool(order & TRIAGE_IDS)
    61→
    62→
    63→def _inject_triage_stages(plan: dict) -> None:
    64→    """Inject all 4 triage stage IDs into the queue (fresh start)."""
    65→    order: list[str] = plan.setdefault("queue_order", [])
    66→    existing = set(order)
    67→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    68→        if sid not in existing:
    69→            order.insert(0 if sid == TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS[0] else len(order), sid)
    70→    # Re-insert in correct order at front
    71→    for sid in reversed(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS):
    72→        if sid in order:
    73→            order.remove(sid)
    74→    insert_at = 0
    75→    for sid in TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS:
    76→        order.insert(insert_at, sid)
    77→        insert_at += 1
    78→
    79→
    80→def _purge_triage_stage(plan: dict, stage_name: str) -> None:
    81→    """Purge a single triage stage ID from the queue."""
    82→    sid = f"triage::{stage_name}"
    83→    purge_ids(plan, [sid])
    84→
    85→
    86→def _cascade_clear_later_confirmations(stages: dict, from_stage: str) -> list[str]:
    87→    """Clear confirmed_at/confirmed_text on stages AFTER *from_stage*. Returns cleared names."""
    88→    try:
    89→        idx = _STAGE_ORDER.index(from_stage)
    90→    except ValueError:
    91→        return []
    92→    cleared: list[str] = []
    93→    for later in _STAGE_ORDER[idx + 1:]:
    94→        if later in stages and stages[later].get("confirmed_at"):
    95→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_at", None)
    96→            stages[later].pop("confirmed_text", None)
    97→            cleared.append(later)
    98→    return cleared
    99→
   100→
```

```
1→"""Plan persistence — load/save with atomic writes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import shutil
     8→import sys
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→
    11→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    13→    PLAN_VERSION,
    14→    PlanModel,
    15→    empty_plan,
    16→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    17→    validate_plan,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import STATE_DIR, json_default
    20→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    21→
    22→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    23→
    24→PLAN_FILE = STATE_DIR / "plan.json"
    25→
    26→
    27→def load_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> PlanModel:
    28→    """Load plan from disk, or return empty plan on missing/corruption."""
    29→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    30→    if not plan_path.exists():
    31→        return empty_plan()
    32→
    33→    try:
    34→        data = json.loads(plan_path.read_text())
    35→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as ex:
    36→        # Try backup before giving up
    37→        backup = plan_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
    38→        if backup.exists():
    39→            try:
    40→                data = json.loads(backup.read_text())
    41→                logger.warning("Plan file corrupted (%s), loaded from backup.", ex)
    42→                print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}), loaded from backup.", file=sys.stderr)
    43→                # Fall through to validation below
    44→            except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as backup_ex:
    45→                logger.warning("Plan file and backup both corrupted: %s / %s", ex, backup_ex)
    46→                print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    47→                return empty_plan()
    48→        else:
    49→            logger.warning("Plan file corrupted (%s). Starting fresh.", ex)
    50→            print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    51→            return empty_plan()
    52→
    53→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    54→        logger.warning("Plan file root is not a JSON object. Starting fresh.")
    55→        print("  Warning: Plan file root must be a JSON object. Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    56→        return empty_plan()
    57→
    58→    version = data.get("version", 1)
    59→    if version > PLAN_VERSION:
    60→        logger.warning("Plan file version %d > supported %d.", version, PLAN_VERSION)
    61→        print(
    62→            f"  Warning: Plan file version {version} is newer than supported "
    63→            f"({PLAN_VERSION}). Some features may not work correctly.",
    64→            file=sys.stderr,
    65→        )
    66→
    67→    ensure_plan_defaults(data)
    68→    try:
    69→        validate_plan(data)
    70→    except ValueError as ex:
    71→        logger.warning("Plan invariants invalid (%s). Starting fresh.", ex)
    72→        print(f"  Warning: Plan invariants invalid ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    73→        return empty_plan()
    74→
    75→    return data  # type: ignore[return-value]
    76→
    77→
    78→def save_plan(plan: PlanModel | dict, path: Path | None = None) -> None:
    79→    """Validate and save plan to disk atomically."""
    80→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    81→    plan["updated"] = utc_now()
    82→    validate_plan(plan)
    83→
    84→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    85→    plan_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    86→
    87→    content = json.dumps(plan, indent=2, default=json_default) + "\n"
    88→
    89→    if plan_path.exists():
    90→        backup = plan_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
    91→        try:
    92→            shutil.copy2(str(plan_path), str(backup))
    93→        except OSError as backup_ex:
    94→            logger.debug("Failed to create plan backup: %s", backup_ex)
    95→
    96→    try:
    97→        safe_write_text(plan_path, content)
    98→    except OSError as ex:
    99→        print(f"  Warning: Could not save plan: {ex}", file=sys.stderr)
   100→        raise
   101→
   102→
   103→def plan_path_for_state(state_path: Path) -> Path:
   104→    """Derive plan.json path from a state file path."""
   105→    return state_path.parent / "plan.json"
   106→
   107→
   108→def has_living_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> bool:
   109→    """Return True if a plan.json exists and has user intent."""
   110→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
   111→    if not plan_path.exists():
   112→        return False
   113→    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   114→    return bool(
   115→        plan.get("queue_order")
   116→        or plan.get("overrides")
   117→        or plan.get("clusters")
   118→    )
   119→
   120→
   121→__all__ = [
   122→    "PLAN_FILE",
   123→    "has_living_plan",
   124→    "load_plan",
   125→    "plan_path_for_state",
   126→    "save_plan",
   127→]
   128→
```

```
    74→    original_summary: str
    75→    status: str  # "superseded" | "remapped" | "dismissed"
    76→    superseded_at: str
    77→    remapped_to: str | None
    78→    candidates: list[str]
    79→    note: str | None
    80→
    81→
    82→class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    83→    version: Required[int]
    84→    created: Required[str]
    85→    updated: Required[str]
    86→    queue_order: list[str]
    87→    deferred: list[str]  # kept empty for migration compat
    88→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry]
    89→    active_cluster: str | None
    90→    overrides: dict[str, ItemOverride]
    91→    clusters: dict[str, Cluster]
    92→    superseded: dict[str, SupersededEntry]
    93→    promoted_ids: list[str]  # IDs user explicitly positioned via move_items()
    94→    plan_start_scores: dict  # frozen score snapshot from plan creation cycle
    95→    execution_log: list[ExecutionLogEntry]
    96→    epic_triage_meta: dict  # triage engine metadata
    97→
    98→
    99→def empty_plan() -> PlanModel:
   100→    """Return a new empty plan payload."""
   101→    now = utc_now()
   102→    return {
   103→        "version": PLAN_VERSION,
   104→        "created": now,
   105→        "updated": now,
   106→        "queue_order": [],
   107→        "deferred": [],
   108→        "skipped": {},
   109→        "active_cluster": None,
   110→        "overrides": {},
   111→        "clusters": {},
   112→        "superseded": {},
   113→        "promoted_ids": [],
   114→        "plan_start_scores": {},
   115→        "execution_log": [],
   116→        "epic_triage_meta": {},
   117→    }
   118→
   119→
   120→def ensure_plan_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   121→    """Normalize a loaded plan to ensure all keys exist.
   122→
   123→    Handles migration from v1 (deferred list) to v2 (skipped dict),
   124→    v3 (top-level epics) to v4 (epics unified into clusters),
   125→    v5 (gates) to v6 (unified queue),
   126→    and v6 (synthesis naming) to v7 (triage naming).
   127→    """
   128→    defaults = empty_plan()
   129→    for key, value in defaults.items():
   130→        plan.setdefault(key, value)
   131→
   132→    _ensure_container_types(plan)
   133→    _migrate_deferred_to_skipped(plan)
   134→    _migrate_epics_to_clusters(plan)
   135→    _normalize_cluster_defaults(plan)
   136→    _migrate_v5_to_v6(plan)
   137→    _migrate_synthesis_to_triage(plan)
   138→
   139→
   140→def triage_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Cluster]:
   141→    """Return clusters whose name starts with ``EPIC_PREFIX``."""
   142→    return {
   143→        name: cluster
   144→        for name, cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).items()
   145→        if name.startswith(EPIC_PREFIX)
   146→    }
   147→
   148→
   149→def validate_plan(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   150→    """Raise ValueError when plan invariants are violated."""
```

```
    59→    if snapshot is None:

    62→        except FileNotFoundError:
    63→            return
    64→        return

    68→def _save_plan_state_transactional(

    75→    """Persist plan+state together; rollback both files on partial write failure."""
    76→    effective_plan_path = _resolve_plan_file(plan_path)
    77→    effective_state_path = _resolve_state_file(state_path_value)
    78→    plan_snapshot = _snapshot_file(effective_plan_path)
```

```
1→"""Epic triage engine — LLM-driven meta-plan for review findings.
     2→
     3→Clusters review findings by root cause, dismisses false positives,
     4→resolves contradictions, and orders by dependency. The triage is incremental:
     5→it updates existing triage-clusters rather than recreating from scratch.
     6→"""
     7→
     8→from __future__ import annotations
     9→
    10→import json
    11→import logging
    12→import re
    13→from dataclasses import dataclass, field
    14→from typing import Any
    15→
    16→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    17→    EPIC_PREFIX,
    18→    VALID_EPIC_DIRECTIONS,
    19→    Cluster,
    20→    PlanModel,
    21→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    22→    triage_clusters,
    23→)
    24→from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import review_finding_snapshot_hash
    25→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import StateModel, utc_now
    26→
    27→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    28→
    29→
    30→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    31→# Finding-ID citation extraction
    32→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    33→
    34→FINDING_ID_RE = re.compile(r"[a-z_]+::[a-f0-9]{8,}")
    35→
    36→
    37→def extract_finding_citations(text: str, valid_ids: set[str]) -> set[str]:
    38→    """Extract finding IDs cited in free text.
    39→
    40→    Matches full finding IDs (e.g. ``review::abcdef12``) or bare 8+ char
    41→    hex suffixes that correspond to a known finding.
    42→    """
    43→    cited: set[str] = set()
    44→    # Match full finding IDs
    45→    for match in FINDING_ID_RE.finditer(text):
    46→        candidate = match.group()
    47→        if candidate in valid_ids:
    48→            cited.add(candidate)
    49→    # Match 8+ char hex suffixes
    50→    for token in re.findall(r"[0-9a-f]{8,}", text):
    51→        for valid_id in valid_ids:
    52→            if valid_id.endswith("::" + token):
    53→                cited.add(valid_id)
    54→                break
    55→    return cited
    56→
    57→
    58→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    59→# Completed-work helpers
    60→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    61→
    62→def last_real_review_timestamp(state: dict) -> str | None:
    63→    """ISO timestamp of most recent genuine review import (not manual override/scan reset)."""
    64→    REAL_MODES = {"holistic", "per_file", "trusted_internal", "attested_external"}
    65→    audit = state.get("assessment_import_audit", [])
    66→    if isinstance(audit, list):
    67→        for entry in reversed(audit):
    68→            if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get("mode") in REAL_MODES:
    69→                ts = entry.get("timestamp")
    70→                if ts:
    71→                    return str(ts)
    72→    holistic = (state.get("review_cache") or {}).get("holistic")
    73→    if isinstance(holistic, dict):
    74→        return holistic.get("reviewed_at")
    75→    return None
    76→
    77→
    78→def detect_recurring_patterns(
    79→    open_findings: dict[str, dict],
    80→    resolved_findings: dict[str, dict],
    81→) -> dict[str, dict]:
    82→    """Detect dimensions with both resolved AND current open findings.
    83→
    84→    Returns ``{dimension: {"open": [ids], "resolved": [ids]}}``.
    85→    A dimension with both resolved and open findings signals a potential
    86→    loop — similar issues recur after previous fixes.
    87→    """
    88→    def _dimension(f: dict) -> str:
    89→        detail = f.get("detail", {})
    90→        if isinstance(detail, dict):
    91→            return detail.get("dimension", "")
    92→        return ""
    93→
    94→    open_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
    95→    for fid, f in open_findings.items():
    96→        dim = _dimension(f)
    97→        if dim:
    98→            open_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
    99→
   100→    resolved_by_dim: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   101→    for fid, f in resolved_findings.items():
   102→        dim = _dimension(f)
   103→        if dim:
   104→            resolved_by_dim.setdefault(dim, []).append(fid)
   105→
   106→    recurring: dict[str, dict] = {}
   107→    for dim in set(open_by_dim) & set(resolved_by_dim):
   108→        recurring[dim] = {
   109→            "open": open_by_dim[dim],
   110→            "resolved": resolved_by_dim[dim],
   111→        }
   112→    return recurring
   113→
   114→
   115→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   116→# Data types
   117→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   118→
   119→@dataclass
   120→class TriageInput:
   121→    """All data the LLM needs to produce/update epics."""
   122→
   123→    open_findings: dict[str, dict]       # id -> finding (review + concerns)
   124→    mechanical_findings: dict[str, dict]  # id -> finding (non-review, for context)
   125→    existing_epics: dict[str, Cluster]    # current triage-clusters to update
   126→    dimension_scores: dict[str, Any]      # for context
   127→    new_since_last: set[str]             # finding IDs new since last triage
   128→    resolved_since_last: set[str]        # finding IDs resolved since last
   129→    previously_dismissed: list[str]      # IDs dismissed in prior triage
   130→    triage_version: int                  # next version number
   131→    resolved_findings: dict[str, dict]   # full finding objects for resolved IDs
   132→    completed_clusters: list[dict]       # clusters completed since last triage
   133→
   134→
   135→@dataclass
   136→class DismissedFinding:
   137→    """A finding the LLM says doesn't make sense."""
   138→
   139→    finding_id: str
   140→    reason: str
   141→
   142→
   143→@dataclass
   144→class ContradictionNote:
   145→    """Record of a resolved contradiction."""
   146→
   147→    kept: str
   148→    dismissed: str
   149→    reason: str
   150→
   151→
   152→@dataclass
   153→class TriageResult:
   154→    """Parsed and validated LLM triage output."""
   155→
   156→    strategy_summary: str
   157→    epics: list[dict]
   158→    dismissed_findings: list[DismissedFinding] = field(default_factory=list)
   159→    contradiction_notes: list[ContradictionNote] = field(default_factory=list)
   160→    priority_rationale: str = ""
   161→
   162→
   163→@dataclass
   164→class TriageMutationResult:
   165→    """What changed when triage was applied to the plan."""
   166→
   167→    epics_created: int = 0
   168→    epics_updated: int = 0
   169→    epics_completed: int = 0
   170→    findings_dismissed: int = 0
   171→    findings_reassigned: int = 0
   172→    strategy_summary: str = ""
   173→    triage_version: int = 0
   174→    dry_run: bool = False
   175→
   176→
   177→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   178→# Input collection
   179→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   180→
   181→def collect_triage_input(plan: PlanModel, state: StateModel) -> TriageInput:
   182→    """Gather all data needed for the triage LLM prompt."""
   183→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   184→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   185→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   186→    epics = triage_clusters(plan)
   187→
   188→    open_review: dict[str, dict] = {}
   189→    open_mechanical: dict[str, dict] = {}
   190→    for fid, f in findings.items():
   191→        if f.get("status") != "open":
   192→            continue
   193→        if f.get("detector") in ("review", "concerns"):
   194→            open_review[fid] = f
   195→        else:
   196→            open_mechanical[fid] = f
   197→
   198→    triaged_ids = set(meta.get("triaged_ids", []))
   199→    current_review_ids = set(open_review.keys())
```

```
1→"""CLI entry point: parse args, load shared context, dispatch command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→import sys
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.app.cli_support.parser import create_parser as _create_parser
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.registry import get_command_handlers
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import LangResolutionError, resolve_lang
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import CommandRuntime
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    14→from desloppify.core.registry import detector_names, on_detector_registered
    15→from desloppify.core.config import load_config
    16→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import set_exclusions
    17→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    18→from desloppify.core.paths_api import get_default_path, get_project_root
    19→from desloppify.core.runtime_state import runtime_scope
    20→from desloppify.languages import available_langs
    21→from desloppify.state import load_state
    22→
    23→_DETECTOR_NAMES: list[str] | None = None
    24→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    25→# Backward-compatible test patch hook; runtime path resolution uses get_project_root().
    26→PROJECT_ROOT = get_project_root()
    27→_PROJECT_ROOT_SENTINEL = PROJECT_ROOT
    28→
    29→
    30→def _get_detector_names() -> list[str]:
    31→    """Return cached detector names, computing on first access."""
    32→    global _DETECTOR_NAMES
    33→    if _DETECTOR_NAMES is None:
    34→        _DETECTOR_NAMES = detector_names()
    35→    return _DETECTOR_NAMES
    36→
    37→
    38→def _invalidate_detector_names_cache() -> None:
    39→    """Invalidate detector-name cache when runtime registrations change."""
    40→    global _DETECTOR_NAMES
    41→    _DETECTOR_NAMES = None
    42→
    43→
    44→on_detector_registered(_invalidate_detector_names_cache)
    45→
    46→def create_parser():
    47→    """Return the top-level argparse parser."""
    48→    return _create_parser(langs=available_langs(), detector_names=_get_detector_names())
    49→
    50→
    51→def _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config: dict):
    52→    """Merge CLI --exclude with persisted config.exclude and apply globally."""
    53→    cli_exclusions = getattr(args, "exclude", None) or []
    54→    persisted = config.get("exclude", [])
    55→    combined = list(cli_exclusions) + [e for e in persisted if e not in cli_exclusions]
    56→    if not combined:
    57→        return
    58→    set_exclusions(combined)
    59→    if cli_exclusions:
    60→        print(
    61→            colorize(f"  Excluding: {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"),
    62→            file=sys.stderr,
    63→        )
    64→        return
    65→    print(
    66→        colorize(
    67→            f"  Excluding (from config): {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"
    68→        ),
    69→        file=sys.stderr,
    70→    )
    71→
    72→
    73→def _resolve_default_path(args) -> None:
    74→    """Fill args.path from detected language or default source path.
    75→
    76→    For the review command, the last scan path (stored in state) is used as the
    77→    default so that ``desloppify review --prepare`` works on the same scope as
    78→    the preceding scan even when the project files are not under ``src/``.
    79→    """
    80→    if getattr(args, "path", None) is not None:
    81→        return
    82→    runtime_root = (
    83→        PROJECT_ROOT.resolve()
    84→        if PROJECT_ROOT is not _PROJECT_ROOT_SENTINEL and isinstance(PROJECT_ROOT, Path)
    85→        else get_project_root()
    86→    )
    87→    if getattr(args, "command", None) == "review":
    88→        try:
    89→            state_file = state_path(args)
    90→            if state_file:
    91→                saved = load_state(state_file)
    92→                saved_path = saved.get("scan_path")
    93→                if saved_path:
    94→                    args.path = str((runtime_root / saved_path).resolve())
    95→                    return
    96→        except (OSError, KeyError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as exc:
    97→            logger.debug("Failed to resolve default path from saved state: %s", exc)
    98→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    99→    if lang:
   100→        args.path = str(runtime_root / lang.default_src)
```

```
    46→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    47→    state: dict,
    48→    dim_scores: dict,
    49→    *,
    50→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    51→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    52→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    53→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    54→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    55→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    56→            continue
    57→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    58→        if strict_val < threshold:
    59→            low.append(
    60→                (
    61→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    62→                    strict_val,
    63→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    64→                )
    65→            )
    66→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    67→    return low
```

```
# Desloppify - an agent harness to make your codebase 🤌

[![PyPI version](https://img.shields.io/pypi/v/desloppify)](https://pypi.org/project/desloppify/) ![Python 3.11+](https://img.shields.io/badge/python-3.11%2B-blue)

Desloppify gives your AI coding agent the tools to identify, understand, and systematically improve codebase quality. It combines mechanical detection (dead code, duplication, complexity) with subjective LLM review (naming, abstractions, module boundaries), then works through a prioritized fix loop. State persists across scans so it chips away over multiple sessions, and the scoring is designed to resist gaming.

<img src="assets/explained.png" width="100%">

The score gives your agent a north-star, and the tooling helps it plan, execute, and resolve issues until it hits your target — with a lot of tricks to keep it on track. A score above 98 should correlate with a codebase a seasoned engineer would call beautiful.

That score generates a scorecard badge for your GitHub profile or README:

<img src="assets/scorecard.png" width="100%">

Currently supports 28 languages — full plugin depth for TypeScript, Python, C#, Dart, GDScript, and Go; generic linter + tree-sitter support for Rust, Ruby, Java, Kotlin, and 17 more.

## For your agent's consideration...

Paste this prompt into your agent:

```
I want you to improve the quality of this codebase. To do this, install and run desloppify.
Run ALL of the following (requires Python 3.11+):

pip install --upgrade "desloppify[full]"
desloppify update-skill claude    # installs the full workflow guide — pick yours: claude, cursor, codex, copilot, windsurf, gemini

Before scanning, check for directories that should be excluded (vendor, build output,
generated code, worktrees, etc.) and exclude obvious ones with `desloppify exclude <path>`.
Share any questionable candidates with me before excluding.

desloppify scan --path .
desloppify next

--path is the directory to scan (use "." for the whole project, or "src/" etc).

Your goal is to get the strict score as high as possible. The scoring resists gaming — the
only way to improve it is to actually make the code better.

THE LOOP: run `next`. It tells you what to fix, which file, and the resolve command to run
when done. Fix it, resolve it, run `next` again. Over and over. This is your main job.

Don't be lazy. Large refactors and small detailed fixes — do both with equal energy. No task
is too big or too small. Fix things properly, not minimally.

Use `plan` to reorder priorities or cluster related issues. Rescan periodically. The scan
output includes agent instructions — follow them, don't substitute your own analysis.
```

## From Vibe Coding to Vibe Engineering
```
