1→"""Pure mutation operations on the plan model."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
     6→    Cluster,
     7→    ExecutionLogEntry,
     8→    PlanModel,
     9→    SkipEntry,
    10→    empty_plan,
    11→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    14→
    15→
    16→_DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES = 10000
    17→
    18→
    19→def _get_log_cap() -> int:
    20→    """Read execution_log_max_entries from config. Returns 0 for unlimited."""
    21→    try:
    22→        from desloppify.core.config import load_config
    23→
    24→        config = load_config()
    25→        value = config.get("execution_log_max_entries", _DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES)
    26→        return max(0, int(value))
    27→    except (ImportError, OSError, ValueError, TypeError):
    28→        return _DEFAULT_MAX_LOG_ENTRIES
    29→
    30→
    31→def append_log_entry(
    32→    plan: PlanModel,
    33→    action: str,
    34→    *,
    35→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    36→    cluster_name: str | None = None,
    37→    actor: str = "user",
    38→    note: str | None = None,
    39→    detail: dict | None = None,
    40→) -> None:
    41→    """Append a structured entry to the plan's execution log."""
    42→    log = plan.get("execution_log", [])
    43→    entry: ExecutionLogEntry = {
    44→        "timestamp": utc_now(),
    45→        "action": action,
    46→        "finding_ids": finding_ids or [],
    47→        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
    48→        "actor": actor,
    49→        "note": note,
    50→        "detail": detail or {},
    51→    }
    52→    log.append(entry)
    53→    cap = _get_log_cap()
    54→    if cap > 0 and len(log) > cap:
    55→        plan["execution_log"] = log[-cap:]
    56→
    57→
    58→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    59→# Helpers
    60→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    61→
    62→def _remove_id_from_lists(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> None:
    63→    """Remove a finding ID from queue_order and skipped."""
    64→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
    65→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
    66→    if finding_id in order:
    67→        order.remove(finding_id)
    68→    skipped.pop(finding_id, None)
    69→
    70→
    71→def _resolve_position(
    72→    order: list[str],
    73→    position: str,
    74→    target: str | None = None,
    75→    offset: int | None = None,
    76→    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    77→) -> int:
    78→    """Resolve a position specifier to an insertion index.
    79→
    80→    *finding_ids* are the IDs being moved — used to calculate relative
    81→    positions when they already exist in the list.
    82→    """
    83→    moving = set(finding_ids or [])
    84→
    85→    if position == "top":
    86→        return 0
    87→    if position == "bottom":
    88→        return len(order)
    89→
    90→    if position in {"before", "after"} and target:
    91→        return _resolve_relative_position(order, moving, position=position, target=target)
    92→
    93→    if position in {"up", "down"} and offset is not None:
    94→        return _resolve_offset_position(
    95→            order,
    96→            moving,
    97→            position=position,
    98→            offset=offset,
    99→            finding_ids=finding_ids,
   100→        )
   101→
   102→    return len(order)
   103→
   104→
   105→def _resolve_relative_position(
   106→    order: list[str],
   107→    moving: set[str],
   108→    *,
   109→    position: str,
   110→    target: str,
   111→) -> int:
   112→    for i, item_id in enumerate(order):
   113→        if item_id == target and item_id not in moving:
   114→            return i if position == "before" else i + 1
   115→    return 0 if position == "before" else len(order)
   116→
   117→
   118→def _find_index(items: list[str], target: str) -> int | None:
   119→    for i, item_id in enumerate(items):
   120→        if item_id == target:
   121→            return i
   122→    return None
   123→
   124→
   125→def _resolve_offset_position(
   126→    order: list[str],
   127→    moving: set[str],
   128→    *,
   129→    position: str,
   130→    offset: int,
   131→    finding_ids: list[str] | None,
   132→) -> int:
   133→    if not finding_ids:
   134→        return 0 if position == "up" else len(order)
   135→    first_id = finding_ids[0]
   136→    clean_order = [x for x in order if x not in moving]
   137→    current_idx = _find_index(clean_order, first_id)
   138→    if current_idx is None:
   139→        if position == "up":
   140→            return max(0, len(clean_order) - offset)
   141→        return len(clean_order)
   142→    if position == "up":
   143→        return max(0, current_idx - offset)
   144→    return min(len(clean_order), current_idx + offset)
   145→
   146→
   147→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   148→# Move items
   149→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   150→
   151→def move_items(
   152→    plan: PlanModel,
   153→    finding_ids: list[str],
   154→    position: str,
   155→    target: str | None = None,
   156→    offset: int | None = None,
   157→) -> int:
   158→    """Move finding IDs to a position in queue_order. Returns count moved."""
   159→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   160→
   161→    # Triage stage IDs are workflow-managed and cannot be manually reordered
   162→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import TRIAGE_IDS
   163→    finding_ids = [fid for fid in finding_ids if fid not in TRIAGE_IDS]
   164→    if not finding_ids:
   165→        return 0
   166→
   167→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   168→
   169→    # Remove from skipped if present
   170→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan.get("skipped", {})
   171→    for fid in finding_ids:
   172→        skipped.pop(fid, None)
   173→
   174→    # Remove from current position in order
   175→    for fid in finding_ids:
   176→        if fid in order:
   177→            order.remove(fid)
   178→
   179→    # Resolve insertion point
   180→    idx = _resolve_position(order, position, target, offset, finding_ids)
   181→
   182→    # Insert in original order
   183→    for i, fid in enumerate(finding_ids):
   184→        order.insert(idx + i, fid)
   185→
   186→    # Track user-promoted IDs only when explicitly moved to the front.
   187→    # Moving to bottom/down/after should not create a promotion barrier.
   188→    if position == "top":
   189→        promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   190→        existing_promoted = set(promoted)
   191→        for fid in finding_ids:
   192→            if fid not in existing_promoted:
   193→                promoted.append(fid)
   194→                existing_promoted.add(fid)
   195→        plan["promoted_ids"] = promoted
   196→
   197→    return len(finding_ids)
   198→
   199→
   200→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   201→# Skip / unskip
   202→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   203→
   204→def skip_items(
   205→    plan: PlanModel,
   206→    finding_ids: list[str],
   207→    *,
   208→    kind: str = "temporary",
   209→    reason: str | None = None,
   210→    note: str | None = None,
   211→    attestation: str | None = None,
   212→    review_after: int | None = None,
   213→    scan_count: int = 0,
   214→) -> int:
   215→    """Move finding IDs to the skipped dict. Returns count skipped."""
   216→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   217→    now = utc_now()
   218→    count = 0
   219→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   220→    skip_set = set(finding_ids)
   221→    promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   222→    plan["promoted_ids"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in skip_set]
   223→    for fid in finding_ids:
   224→        _remove_id_from_lists(plan, fid)
   225→        skipped[fid] = {
   226→            "finding_id": fid,
   227→            "kind": kind,
   228→            "reason": reason,
   229→            "note": note,
   230→            "attestation": attestation,
   231→            "created_at": now,
   232→            "review_after": review_after,
   233→            "skipped_at_scan": scan_count,
   234→        }
   235→        count += 1
   236→    return count
   237→
   238→
   239→def unskip_items(
   240→    plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]
   241→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   242→    """Bring finding IDs back from skipped to the end of queue_order.
   243→
   244→    Returns ``(count_unskipped, permanent_ids_needing_state_reopen)``
   245→    where the second list contains IDs that were permanent or false_positive
   246→    and need their state-layer status reopened by the caller.
   247→    """
   248→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   249→    count = 0
   250→    need_reopen: list[str] = []
   251→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   252→    for fid in finding_ids:
   253→        entry = skipped.pop(fid, None)
   254→        if entry is not None:
   255→            if entry.get("kind") in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   256→                need_reopen.append(fid)
   257→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   258→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   259→            count += 1
   260→    return count, need_reopen
   261→
   262→
   263→def resurface_stale_skips(
   264→    plan: PlanModel, current_scan_count: int
   265→) -> list[str]:
   266→    """Move temporary skips past their review_after threshold back to queue.
   267→
   268→    Returns list of resurfaced finding IDs.
   269→    """
   270→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   271→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   272→    resurfaced: list[str] = []
   273→    for fid in list(skipped):
   274→        entry = skipped[fid]
   275→        if entry.get("kind") != "temporary":
   276→            continue
   277→        review_after = entry.get("review_after")
   278→        if review_after is None:
   279→            continue
   280→        skipped_at = entry.get("skipped_at_scan", 0)
   281→        if current_scan_count >= skipped_at + review_after:
   282→            skipped.pop(fid)
   283→            if fid not in plan["queue_order"]:
   284→                plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   285→            resurfaced.append(fid)
   286→    return resurfaced
   287→
   288→
   289→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   290→# Describe / annotate
   291→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   292→
   293→def describe_finding(
   294→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, description: str | None
   295→) -> None:
   296→    """Set or clear an augmented description on a finding."""
   297→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   298→    now = utc_now()
   299→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   300→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   301→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   302→    overrides[finding_id]["description"] = description
   303→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   304→
   305→
   306→def annotate_finding(
   307→    plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str, note: str | None
   308→) -> None:
   309→    """Set or clear a note on a finding."""
   310→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   311→    now = utc_now()
   312→    overrides = plan["overrides"]
   313→    if finding_id not in overrides:
   314→        overrides[finding_id] = {"finding_id": finding_id, "created_at": now}
   315→    overrides[finding_id]["note"] = note
   316→    overrides[finding_id]["updated_at"] = now
   317→
   318→
   319→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   320→# Clusters
   321→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   322→
   323→def create_cluster(
   324→    plan: PlanModel,
   325→    name: str,
   326→    description: str | None = None,
   327→    action: str | None = None,
   328→) -> Cluster:
   329→    """Create a named cluster. Raises ValueError if it already exists."""
   330→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   331→    if name.startswith("auto/"):
   332→        raise ValueError(
   333→            f"Cluster names starting with 'auto/' are reserved for auto-clusters: {name!r}"
   334→        )
   335→    if name.startswith("epic/"):
   336→        raise ValueError(
   337→            f"Cluster names starting with 'epic/' are reserved for triage epics: {name!r}"
   338→        )
   339→    if name in plan["clusters"]:
   340→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} already exists")
   341→    now = utc_now()
   342→    cluster: Cluster = {
   343→        "name": name,
   344→        "description": description,
   345→        "finding_ids": [],
   346→        "created_at": now,
   347→        "updated_at": now,
   348→        "auto": False,
   349→        "cluster_key": "",
   350→        "action": action,
   351→        "user_modified": False,
   352→    }
   353→    plan["clusters"][name] = cluster
   354→    return cluster
   355→
   356→
   357→def add_to_cluster(
   358→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   359→) -> int:
   360→    """Add finding IDs to a cluster. Returns count added."""
   361→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   362→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   363→    if cluster is None:
   364→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   365→
   366→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   367→    count = 0
   368→    now = utc_now()
   369→    for fid in finding_ids:
   370→        if fid not in member_ids:
   371→            member_ids.append(fid)
   372→            count += 1
   373→        # Update override to track cluster membership
   374→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   375→        if fid not in overrides:
   376→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   377→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = cluster_name
   378→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   379→
   380→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   381→    return count
   382→
   383→
   384→def remove_from_cluster(
   385→    plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str]
   386→) -> int:
   387→    """Remove finding IDs from a cluster. Returns count removed."""
   388→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   389→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   390→    if cluster is None:
   391→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   392→
   393→    member_ids: list[str] = cluster["finding_ids"]
   394→    now = utc_now()
   395→    count = 0
   396→    for fid in finding_ids:
   397→        if fid in member_ids:
   398→            member_ids.remove(fid)
   399→            count += 1
   400→        # Clear cluster from override
   401→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   402→        if override and override.get("cluster") == cluster_name:
   403→            override["cluster"] = None
   404→            override["updated_at"] = now
   405→
   406→    # Mark auto-clusters as user_modified when items are manually removed
   407→    if count > 0 and cluster.get("auto"):
   408→        cluster["user_modified"] = True
   409→
   410→    cluster["updated_at"] = now
   411→    return count
   412→
   413→
   414→def delete_cluster(plan: PlanModel, name: str) -> list[str]:
   415→    """Delete a cluster and clear cluster refs from overrides. Returns orphaned IDs."""
   416→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   417→    cluster = plan["clusters"].pop(name, None)
   418→    if cluster is None:
   419→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {name!r} does not exist")
   420→
   421→    orphaned = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   422→    now = utc_now()
   423→    for fid in orphaned:
   424→        override = plan["overrides"].get(fid)
   425→        if override and override.get("cluster") == name:
   426→            override["cluster"] = None
   427→            override["updated_at"] = now
   428→
   429→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == name:
   430→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   431→
   432→    return orphaned
   433→
   434→
   435→def merge_clusters(
   436→    plan: PlanModel, source_name: str, target_name: str
   437→) -> tuple[int, list[str]]:
   438→    """Move all source findings to target, copy missing metadata, delete source.
   439→
   440→    Returns ``(added_count, source_finding_ids)``.
   441→    """
   442→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   443→    if source_name == target_name:
   444→        raise ValueError("Cannot merge a cluster into itself")
   445→    source = plan["clusters"].get(source_name)
   446→    if source is None:
   447→        raise ValueError(f"Source cluster {source_name!r} does not exist")
   448→    target = plan["clusters"].get(target_name)
   449→    if target is None:
   450→        raise ValueError(f"Target cluster {target_name!r} does not exist")
   451→
   452→    source_ids = list(source.get("finding_ids", []))
   453→    target_ids: list[str] = target["finding_ids"]
   454→    now = utc_now()
   455→
   456→    # Add source findings to target (deduplicate)
   457→    existing = set(target_ids)
   458→    added = 0
   459→    for fid in source_ids:
   460→        if fid not in existing:
   461→            target_ids.append(fid)
   462→            existing.add(fid)
   463→            added += 1
   464→        # Update override to point to target cluster
   465→        overrides = plan["overrides"]
   466→        if fid not in overrides:
   467→            overrides[fid] = {"finding_id": fid, "created_at": now}
   468→        overrides[fid]["cluster"] = target_name
   469→        overrides[fid]["updated_at"] = now
   470→
   471→    # Copy metadata from source if target is missing them
   472→    if not target.get("description") and source.get("description"):
   473→        target["description"] = source["description"]
   474→    if not target.get("action_steps") and source.get("action_steps"):
   475→        target["action_steps"] = list(source["action_steps"])
   476→    if not target.get("action") and source.get("action"):
   477→        target["action"] = source["action"]
   478→
   479→    target["updated_at"] = now
   480→
   481→    # Delete source cluster
   482→    plan["clusters"].pop(source_name, None)
   483→    if plan.get("active_cluster") == source_name:
   484→        plan["active_cluster"] = None
   485→
   486→    return added, source_ids
   487→
   488→
   489→def move_cluster(
   490→    plan: PlanModel,
   491→    cluster_name: str,
   492→    position: str,
   493→    target: str | None = None,
   494→    offset: int | None = None,
   495→) -> int:
   496→    """Move all cluster members as a contiguous block. Returns count moved."""
   497→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   498→    cluster = plan["clusters"].get(cluster_name)
   499→    if cluster is None:
   500→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   501→
   502→    member_ids = list(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
   503→    if not member_ids:
   504→        return 0
   505→
   506→    return move_items(plan, member_ids, position, target, offset)
   507→
   508→
   509→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   510→# Focus
   511→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   512→
   513→def set_focus(plan: PlanModel, cluster_name: str) -> None:
   514→    """Set the active cluster focus."""
   515→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   516→    if cluster_name not in plan["clusters"]:
   517→        raise ValueError(f"Cluster {cluster_name!r} does not exist")
   518→    plan["active_cluster"] = cluster_name
   519→
   520→
   521→def clear_focus(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   522→    """Clear the active cluster focus."""
   523→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   524→    plan["active_cluster"] = None
   525→
   526→
   527→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   528→# Reset
   529→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   530→
   531→def reset_plan(plan: PlanModel) -> None:
   532→    """Reset plan to empty state, preserving version and created timestamp."""
   533→    created = plan.get("created", utc_now())
   534→    plan.clear()
   535→    for k, v in empty_plan().items():
   536→        plan[k] = v
   537→    plan["created"] = created
   538→
   539→
   540→def purge_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
   541→    """Remove finding IDs from the plan entirely.
   542→
   543→    Cleans queue_order, skipped, and all cluster memberships.
   544→    Does NOT touch overrides (descriptions/notes are kept for history).
   545→    Returns count of IDs that were actually present somewhere.
   546→    """
   547→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
   548→    found = 0
   549→
   550→    purge_set = set(finding_ids)
   551→    promoted: list[str] = plan.get("promoted_ids", [])
   552→    plan["promoted_ids"] = [p for p in promoted if p not in purge_set]
   553→
   554→    order: list[str] = plan["queue_order"]
   555→    skipped: dict[str, SkipEntry] = plan["skipped"]
   556→    for fid in finding_ids:
   557→        was_present = False
   558→        if fid in order:
   559→            order.remove(fid)
   560→            was_present = True
   561→        if fid in skipped:
   562→            skipped.pop(fid)
   563→            was_present = True
   564→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   565→            ids = cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   566→            if fid in ids:
   567→                ids.remove(fid)
   568→                was_present = True
   569→        # Clear stale cluster reference from override (preserve notes/descriptions)
   570→        override = plan.get("overrides", {}).get(fid)
   571→        if override and override.get("cluster"):
   572→            override["cluster"] = None
   573→            override["updated_at"] = utc_now()
   574→        if was_present:
   575→            found += 1
   576→
   577→    return found
   578→
   579→
   580→__all__ = [
   581→    "add_to_cluster",
   582→    "annotate_finding",
   583→    "append_log_entry",
   584→    "clear_focus",
   585→    "create_cluster",
   586→    "delete_cluster",
   587→    "describe_finding",
   588→    "merge_clusters",
   589→    "move_cluster",
   590→    "move_items",
   591→    "purge_ids",
   592→    "remove_from_cluster",
   593→    "reset_plan",
   594→    "resurface_stale_skips",
   595→    "set_focus",
   596→    "skip_items",
   597→    "unskip_items",
   598→]
   599→

1→"""Terminal rendering helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import _print_strict_target_nudge
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.render_support import (
     9→    print_post_resolve_guidance,
    10→    print_strict_gap_note,
    11→    score_snapshot_or_warn,
    12→)
    13→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    14→
    15→
    16→def _print_resolve_summary(*, status: str, all_resolved: list[str]) -> None:
    17→    verb = "Reopened" if status == "open" else "Resolved"
    18→    print(colorize(f"\n{verb} {len(all_resolved)} finding(s) as {status}:", "green"))
    19→    for fid in all_resolved[:20]:
    20→        print(f"  {fid}")
    21→    if len(all_resolved) > 20:
    22→        print(f"  ... and {len(all_resolved) - 20} more")
    23→
    24→
    25→def _print_wontfix_batch_warning(
    26→    state: dict,
    27→    *,
    28→    status: str,
    29→    resolved_count: int,
    30→) -> None:
    31→    if status != "wontfix" or resolved_count <= 10:
    32→        return
    33→    wontfix_count = sum(
    34→        1 for finding in state["findings"].values() if finding["status"] == "wontfix"
    35→    )
    36→    actionable = sum(
    37→        1
    38→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
    39→        if finding["status"]
    40→        in ("open", "wontfix", "fixed", "auto_resolved", "false_positive")
    41→    )
    42→    wontfix_pct = round(wontfix_count / actionable * 100) if actionable else 0
    43→    print(
    44→        colorize(
    45→            f"\n  ⚠ Wontfix debt is now {wontfix_count} findings ({wontfix_pct}% of actionable).",
    46→            "yellow",
    47→        )
    48→    )
    49→    print(
    50→        colorize(
    51→            '    The strict score reflects this. Run `desloppify show "*" --status wontfix` to review.',
    52→            "dim",
    53→        )
    54→    )
    55→
    56→
    57→def _delta_suffix(delta: float) -> str:
    58→    if abs(delta) < 0.05:
    59→        return ""
    60→    return f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})"
    61→
    62→
    63→def _print_score_movement(
    64→    *,
    65→    status: str,
    66→    prev_overall: float | None,
    67→    prev_objective: float | None,
    68→    prev_strict: float | None,
    69→    prev_verified: float | None,
    70→    state: dict,
    71→    has_review_findings: bool = False,
    72→    target_strict: float | None = None,
    73→) -> None:
    74→    new = score_snapshot_or_warn(state)
    75→    if new is None:
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    overall_delta = new.overall - (prev_overall or 0)
    79→    objective_delta = new.objective - (prev_objective or 0)
    80→    strict_delta = new.strict - (prev_strict or 0)
    81→    verified_delta = new.verified - (prev_verified or 0)
    82→    print(
    83→        f"\n  Scores: overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(overall_delta)}"
    84→        + colorize(
    85→            f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(objective_delta)}",
    86→            "dim",
    87→        )
    88→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(strict_delta)}", "dim")
    89→        + colorize(
    90→            f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{_delta_suffix(verified_delta)}", "dim"
    91→        )
    92→    )
    93→    if target_strict is not None:
    94→        _print_strict_target_nudge(new.strict, target_strict, show_next=False)
    95→    print_strict_gap_note(status, overall=new.overall, strict=new.strict)
    96→    print_post_resolve_guidance(
    97→        status=status,
    98→        has_review_findings=has_review_findings,
    99→        overall_delta=overall_delta,
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→def _print_subjective_reset_hint(
   104→    *,
   105→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   106→    state: dict,
   107→    all_resolved: list[str],
   108→    prev_subjective_scores: dict[str, float],
   109→) -> None:
   110→    has_review = any(
   111→        state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   112→        for fid in all_resolved
   113→    )
   114→    if not has_review or not state.get("subjective_assessments"):
   115→        return
   116→
   117→    stale_dims = sorted(
   118→        dim
   119→        for dim in {
   120→            str(
   121→                state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")
   122→            ).strip()
   123→            for fid in all_resolved
   124→            if state["findings"].get(fid, {}).get("detector") == "review"
   125→        }
   126→        if dim and dim in (state.get("subjective_assessments") or {})
   127→    )
   128→    if not stale_dims:
   129→        return
   130→
   131→    shown = ", ".join(stale_dims[:3])
   132→    if len(stale_dims) > 3:
   133→        shown = f"{shown}, +{len(stale_dims) - 3} more"
   134→    print(
   135→        colorize(
   136→            f"  Subjective scores unchanged — re-run review for updated scores: {shown}",
   137→            "yellow",
   138→        )
   139→    )
   140→    print(
   141→        colorize(
   142→            "  Next subjective step: "
   143→            + (
   144→                "`desloppify review --prepare --dimensions "
   145→                f"{','.join(stale_dims)} --force-review-rerun`"
   146→            ),
   147→            "dim",
   148→        )
   149→    )
   150→
   151→
   152→def _print_next_command(state: dict) -> str:
   153→    remaining = sum(
   154→        1
   155→        for finding in state["findings"].values()
   156→        if finding["status"] == "open"
   157→        and not finding.get("suppressed")
   158→    )
   159→    next_command = "desloppify scan"
   160→    if remaining > 0:
   161→        suffix = "s" if remaining != 1 else ""
   162→        print(
   163→            colorize(
   164→                f"\n  {remaining} finding{suffix} remaining — run `desloppify next`",
   165→                "dim",
   166→            )
   167→        )
   168→        next_command = "desloppify next"
   169→    print(colorize(f"  Next command: `{next_command}`", "dim"))
   170→    print()
   171→    return next_command
   172→

1→"""Validation and preflight helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→import copy
     7→import sys
     8→from dataclasses import dataclass
     9→
    10→from desloppify import state as state_mod
    11→from desloppify.engine.work_queue import ATTEST_EXAMPLE
    12→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    13→
    14→_REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES = ("i have actually", "not gaming")
    15→_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT = ("I have actually", "not gaming")
    16→_MIN_NOTE_LENGTH = 50
    17→
    18→
    19→def _emit_warning(message: str) -> None:
    20→    """Write resolve preflight warnings to stderr consistently."""
    21→    print(colorize(message, "yellow"), file=sys.stderr)
    22→
    23→
    24→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    25→class ResolveQueryContext:
    26→    patterns: list[str]
    27→    status: str
    28→    resolved: list[str]
    29→    next_command: str
    30→    prev_overall: float | None
    31→    prev_objective: float | None
    32→    prev_strict: float | None
    33→    prev_verified: float | None
    34→    attestation: str | None
    35→    narrative: dict
    36→    state: dict
    37→
    38→
    39→def _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation: str | None) -> list[str]:
    40→    normalized = " ".join((attestation or "").strip().lower().split())
    41→    return [
    42→        phrase for phrase in _REQUIRED_ATTESTATION_PHRASES if phrase not in normalized
    43→    ]
    44→
    45→
    46→def validate_attestation(attestation: str | None) -> bool:
    47→    return not _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation)
    48→
    49→
    50→def show_attestation_requirement(
    51→    label: str,
    52→    attestation: str | None,
    53→    example: str,
    54→) -> None:
    55→    missing = _missing_attestation_keywords(attestation)
    56→    if not attestation:
    57→        _emit_warning(f"{label} requires --attest.")
    58→    elif missing:
    59→        missing_str = ", ".join(f"'{keyword}'" for keyword in missing)
    60→        _emit_warning(
    61→            f"{label} attestation is missing required keyword(s): {missing_str}."
    62→        )
    63→    _emit_warning(
    64→        f"Required keywords: '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[0]}' and '{_ATTESTATION_KEYWORD_HINT[1]}'."
    65→    )
    66→    print(colorize(f'Example: --attest "{example}"', "dim"), file=sys.stderr)
    67→
    68→
    69→def validate_note_length(note: str | None) -> bool:
    70→    """Return True if the note meets the minimum length requirement."""
    71→    return note is not None and len(note.strip()) >= _MIN_NOTE_LENGTH
    72→
    73→
    74→def show_note_length_requirement(note: str | None) -> None:
    75→    """Emit a warning about minimum note length."""
    76→    current = len((note or "").strip())
    77→    _emit_warning(
    78→        f"Note must be at least {_MIN_NOTE_LENGTH} characters (got {current}). "
    79→        f"Describe what you actually did."
    80→    )
    81→
    82→
    83→def _validate_resolve_inputs(args: argparse.Namespace, attestation: str | None) -> None:
    84→    if args.status == "wontfix" and not args.note:
    85→        _emit_warning(
    86→            "Wontfix items become technical debt. Add --note to record your reasoning for future review."
    87→        )
    88→        sys.exit(1)
    89→    if args.status == "open":
    90→        return
    91→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
    92→        show_attestation_requirement(
    93→            "Manual resolve",
    94→            attestation,
    95→            ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
    96→        )
    97→        sys.exit(1)
    98→
    99→
   100→def _previous_score_snapshot(state: dict) -> state_mod.ScoreSnapshot:
   101→    """Load a score snapshot for comparison after resolve operations."""
   102→    return state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
   103→
   104→
   105→def _preview_resolve_count(state: dict, patterns: list[str]) -> int:
   106→    """Count unique open findings matching the provided patterns."""
   107→    matched_ids: set[str] = set()
   108→    for pattern in patterns:
   109→        for finding in state_mod.match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter="open"):
   110→            finding_id = finding.get("id")
   111→            if finding_id:
   112→                matched_ids.add(finding_id)
   113→    return len(matched_ids)
   114→
   115→
   116→def _estimate_wontfix_strict_delta(
   117→    state: dict,
   118→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   119→    *,
   120→    attestation: str | None,
   121→    resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   122→) -> float:
   123→    """Estimate strict score drop if this resolve command is applied as wontfix."""
   124→    before = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   125→    if before is None:
   126→        return 0.0
   127→
   128→    preview_state = copy.deepcopy(state)
   129→    resolve_all_patterns_fn(preview_state, args, attestation=attestation)
   130→    after = state_mod.get_strict_score(preview_state)
   131→    if after is None:
   132→        return 0.0
   133→    return max(0.0, before - after)
   134→
   135→
   136→def _enforce_batch_wontfix_confirmation(
   137→    state: dict,
   138→    args: argparse.Namespace,
   139→    *,
   140→    attestation: str | None,
   141→    resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   142→) -> None:
   143→    if args.status != "wontfix":
   144→        return
   145→
   146→    preview_count = _preview_resolve_count(state, args.patterns)
   147→    if preview_count <= 10:
   148→        return
   149→    if getattr(args, "confirm_batch_wontfix", False):
   150→        return
   151→
   152→    strict_delta = _estimate_wontfix_strict_delta(
   153→        state,
   154→        args,
   155→        attestation=attestation,
   156→        resolve_all_patterns_fn=resolve_all_patterns_fn,
   157→    )
   158→    _emit_warning(f"Large wontfix batch detected ({preview_count} findings).")
   159→    if strict_delta > 0:
   160→        _emit_warning(f"Estimated strict-score debt added now: {strict_delta:.1f} points.")
   161→    _emit_warning(
   162→        "Re-run with --confirm-batch-wontfix if this debt is intentional."
   163→    )
   164→    sys.exit(1)
   165→
```

    37→
    38→def _scorecard_subjective(
    39→    state: dict,
    40→    dim_scores: dict,
    41→) -> list[dict]:
    42→    """Return scorecard-aligned subjective entries for current dimension scores."""
    43→    return next_render_mod.scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores)
    44→
    45→
    46→def _low_subjective_dimensions(
    47→    state: dict,
    48→    dim_scores: dict,
    49→    *,
    50→    threshold: float = 95.0,
    51→) -> list[tuple[str, float, int]]:
    52→    """Return assessed scorecard-subjective entries below the threshold."""
    53→    low: list[tuple[str, float, int]] = []
    54→    for entry in _scorecard_subjective(state, dim_scores):
    55→        if entry.get("placeholder"):
    56→            continue
    57→        strict_val = float(entry.get("strict", entry.get("score", 100.0)))
    58→        if strict_val < threshold:
    59→            low.append(
    60→                (
    61→                    str(entry.get("name", "Subjective")),
    62→                    strict_val,
    63→                    int(entry.get("issues", 0)),
    64→                )
    65→            )
    66→    low.sort(key=lambda item: item[1])
    67→    return low
    68→
    69→
    70→def cmd_next(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    71→    """Show next highest-priority queue items."""
    72→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    73→    state = runtime.state
    74→    config = runtime.config
    75→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
    76→        return
    77→
    78→    skill_warning = check_skill_version()
    79→    if skill_warning:
    80→        print(colorize(f"  {skill_warning}", "yellow"))
    81→    config_warning = check_config_staleness(config)
    82→    if config_warning:
    83→        print(colorize(f"  {config_warning}", "yellow"))
    84→
    85→    print_triage_guardrail_info(state=state)
    86→    _get_items(args, state, config)
    87→
    88→
    89→def _resolve_cluster_focus(
    90→    plan_data: dict | None,
    91→    *,
    92→    cluster_arg: str | None,
    93→    scope: str | None,
    94→) -> str | None:
    95→    effective_cluster = cluster_arg
    96→    if plan_data and not cluster_arg and not scope:
    97→        active_cluster = plan_data.get("active_cluster")
    98→        if active_cluster:
    99→            effective_cluster = active_cluster
   100→    return effective_cluster
   101→
   102→
   103→def _build_next_payload(
   104→    *,
   105→    queue: dict,
   106→    items: list[dict],
   107→    state: dict,
   108→    narrative: dict,
   109→    plan_data: dict | None,
   110→) -> dict:
   111→    payload = next_output_mod.build_query_payload(
   112→        queue, items, command="next", narrative=narrative, plan=plan_data
   113→    )
   114→    payload["overall_score"] = state_mod.get_overall_score(state)
   115→    payload["objective_score"] = state_mod.get_objective_score(state)
   116→    payload["strict_score"] = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   117→    payload["scorecard_dimensions"] = scorecard_dimensions_payload(
   118→        state,
   119→        dim_scores=state.get("dimension_scores", {}),
   120→    )
   121→    payload["subjective_measures"] = [
   122→        row for row in payload["scorecard_dimensions"] if row.get("subjective")
   123→    ]
   124→    return payload
   125→
   126→
   127→def _emit_requested_output(
   128→    args,
   129→    payload: dict,
   130→    items: list[dict],
   131→) -> bool:
   132→    output_file = getattr(args, "output", None)
   133→    if output_file:
   134→        if next_output_mod.write_output_file(
   135→            output_file,
   136→            payload,
   137→            len(items),
   138→            safe_write_text_fn=safe_write_text,
   139→            colorize_fn=colorize,
   140→        ):
   141→            return True
   142→        raise SystemExit(1)
   143→
   144→    output_format = getattr(args, "format", "terminal")
   145→    if next_output_mod.emit_non_terminal_output(output_format, payload, items):
   146→        return True
   147→    return False
   148→
   149→
   150→def _plan_queue_context(
   151→    *,
   152→    state: dict,
   153→    plan_data: dict | None,
   154→) -> tuple[float | None, QueueBreakdown | None]:
   155→    plan_start_strict = get_plan_start_strict(plan_data)
   156→    try:
   157→        breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan_data)
   158→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   159→        breakdown = None
   160→    return plan_start_strict, breakdown
   161→
   162→
   163→def _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials: dict | None) -> dict | None:
   164→    try:
   165→        return merge_potentials(raw_potentials) or None
   166→    except (ImportError, TypeError, ValueError):
   167→        return raw_potentials or None
   168→
   169→
   170→def _get_items(args, state: dict, config: dict) -> None:
   171→    count = getattr(args, "count", 1) or 1
   172→    scope = getattr(args, "scope", None)
   173→    status = getattr(args, "status", "open")
   174→    group = getattr(args, "group", "item")
   175→    explain = bool(getattr(args, "explain", False))
   176→    cluster_arg = getattr(args, "cluster", None)
   177→    include_skipped = bool(getattr(args, "include_skipped", False))
   178→
   179→    target_strict = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
   180→
   181→    # Load the living plan
   182→    plan = load_plan()
   183→    plan_data: dict | None = None
   184→    if (
   185→        plan.get("queue_order")
   186→        or plan.get("overrides")
   187→        or plan.get("clusters")
   188→    ):
   189→        plan_data = plan
   190→
   191→    # Auto-scope to focus cluster if set and no explicit scope/cluster
   192→    effective_cluster = _resolve_cluster_focus(
   193→        plan_data,
   194→        cluster_arg=cluster_arg,
   195→        scope=scope,
   196→    )
   197→
   198→    queue = build_work_queue(
   199→        state,
   200→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   201→            count=count,
   202→            scan_path=state.get("scan_path"),
   203→            scope=scope,
   204→            status=status,
   205→            include_subjective=True,
   206→            subjective_threshold=target_strict,
   207→            explain=explain,
   208→            plan=plan_data,
   209→            include_skipped=include_skipped,
   210→            cluster=effective_cluster,
   211→        ),
   212→    )
   213→    items = queue.get("items", [])
   214→
   215→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
   216→    lang_name = lang.name if lang else None
   217→    narrative = compute_narrative(
   218→        state,
   219→        context=NarrativeContext(lang=lang_name, command="next", plan=plan_data),
   220→    )
   221→
   222→    payload = _build_next_payload(
   223→        queue=queue,
   224→        items=items,
   225→        state=state,
   226→        narrative=narrative,
   227→        plan_data=plan_data,
   228→    )
   229→    write_query(payload)
   230→
   231→    if _emit_requested_output(args, payload, items):
   232→        return
   233→
   234→    dim_scores = state.get("dimension_scores", {})
   235→    findings_scoped = state_mod.path_scoped_findings(
   236→        state.get("findings", {}),
   237→        state.get("scan_path"),
   238→    )
   239→
   240→    # Extract frozen plan-start score and queue breakdown for lifecycle display
   241→    plan_start_strict, breakdown = _plan_queue_context(
   242→        state=state,
   243→        plan_data=plan_data,
   244→    )
   245→    queue_total = breakdown.queue_total if breakdown else 0
   246→
   247→    next_render_mod.render_queue_header(queue, explain)
   248→    strict_score = state_mod.get_strict_score(state)
   249→    if next_render_mod.show_empty_queue(
   250→        queue,
   251→        strict_score,
   252→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   253→        target_strict=target_strict,
   254→    ):
   255→        return
   256→
   257→    raw_potentials = state.get("potentials", {})
   258→    potentials = _merge_potentials_safe(raw_potentials)
   259→    next_render_mod.render_terminal_items(
   260→        items, dim_scores, findings_scoped, group=group, explain=explain,
   261→        potentials=potentials, plan=plan_data,
   262→        cluster_filter=effective_cluster,
   263→    )
   264→    next_render_mod.render_single_item_resolution_hint(items)
   265→    next_render_mod.render_followup_nudges(
   266→        state,

   268→        findings_scoped,

   271→        queue_total=queue_total,
   272→        plan_start_strict=plan_start_strict,
   273→        breakdown=breakdown,
   274→    )
   275→    print()

   277→
   278→__all__ = ["_low_subjective_dimensions", "cmd_next"]

    92→def triage_phase_banner(plan: PlanModel) -> str:
    93→    """Return a banner string when triage stage IDs are in the queue."""
    94→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    95→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))

    97→    if not has_triage:
    98→        return ""
    99→    meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {})
   100→    stages = meta.get("triage_stages", {})
   101→    completed = [s for s in ("observe", "reflect", "organize") if s in stages]
   102→    if completed:
   103→        return (
   104→            f"TRIAGE MODE ({len(completed)}/4 stages complete) — "
   105→            "complete all stages to exit. Run: desloppify plan triage"
   106→        )
   107→    return (
   108→        "TRIAGE MODE — review findings need analysis before fixing. "
   109→        "Run: desloppify plan triage"

1→"""Central command registry for CLI command handler resolution."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from collections.abc import Callable
     6→from typing import Any
     7→
     8→CommandHandler = Callable[[Any], None]
     9→
    10→_COMMAND_HANDLERS: dict[str, CommandHandler] | None = None
    11→
    12→
    13→def _build_handlers() -> dict[str, CommandHandler]:
    14→    """Import all command modules and build the handler dict on first access."""
    15→    from desloppify.app.commands.config_cmd import cmd_config
    16→    from desloppify.app.commands.detect import cmd_detect
    17→    from desloppify.app.commands.dev_cmd import cmd_dev
    18→    from desloppify.app.commands.exclude_cmd import cmd_exclude
    19→    from desloppify.app.commands.fix.cmd import cmd_fix
    20→    from desloppify.app.commands.langs import cmd_langs
    21→    from desloppify.app.commands.move.move import cmd_move
    22→    from desloppify.app.commands.next import cmd_next
    23→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan.cmd import cmd_plan
    24→    from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.ignore_cmd import cmd_ignore_pattern
    25→    from desloppify.app.commands.review import cmd_review
    26→    from desloppify.app.commands.scan import cmd_scan
    27→    from desloppify.app.commands.show.cmd import cmd_show
    28→    from desloppify.app.commands.status_cmd import cmd_status
    29→    from desloppify.app.commands.update_skill import cmd_update_skill
    30→    from desloppify.app.commands.viz_cmd import cmd_tree, cmd_viz
    31→    from desloppify.app.commands.zone_cmd import cmd_zone
    32→
    33→    return {
    34→        "scan": cmd_scan,
    35→        "status": cmd_status,
    36→        "show": cmd_show,
    37→        "next": cmd_next,
    38→        "ignore": cmd_ignore_pattern,
    39→        "exclude": cmd_exclude,
    40→        "fix": cmd_fix,
    41→        "plan": cmd_plan,
    42→        "detect": cmd_detect,
    43→        "tree": cmd_tree,
    44→        "viz": cmd_viz,
    45→        "move": cmd_move,
    46→        "zone": cmd_zone,
    47→        "review": cmd_review,
    48→        "config": cmd_config,
    49→        "dev": cmd_dev,
    50→        "langs": cmd_langs,
    51→        "update-skill": cmd_update_skill,
    52→    }
    53→
    54→
    55→def get_command_handlers() -> dict[str, CommandHandler]:
    56→    """Return cached command handler dict, building on first access."""
    57→    global _COMMAND_HANDLERS
    58→    if _COMMAND_HANDLERS is None:
    59→        _COMMAND_HANDLERS = _build_handlers()
    60→    return _COMMAND_HANDLERS
    61→
    62→
    63→__all__ = ["CommandHandler", "get_command_handlers"]
    64→

1→"""Plan persistence — load/save with atomic writes."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import json
     6→import logging
     7→import shutil
     8→import sys
     9→from pathlib import Path
    10→
    11→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import safe_write_text
    12→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    13→    PLAN_VERSION,
    14→    PlanModel,
    15→    empty_plan,
    16→    ensure_plan_defaults,
    17→    validate_plan,
    18→)
    19→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import STATE_DIR, json_default
    20→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
    21→
    22→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    23→
    24→PLAN_FILE = STATE_DIR / "plan.json"
    25→
    26→
    27→def load_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> PlanModel:
    28→    """Load plan from disk, or return empty plan on missing/corruption."""
    29→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    30→    if not plan_path.exists():
    31→        return empty_plan()
    32→
    33→    try:
    34→        data = json.loads(plan_path.read_text())
    35→    except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as ex:
    36→        # Try backup before giving up
    37→        backup = plan_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
    38→        if backup.exists():
    39→            try:
    40→                data = json.loads(backup.read_text())
    41→                logger.warning("Plan file corrupted (%s), loaded from backup.", ex)
    42→                print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}), loaded from backup.", file=sys.stderr)
    43→                # Fall through to validation below
    44→            except (json.JSONDecodeError, UnicodeDecodeError, OSError) as backup_ex:
    45→                logger.warning("Plan file and backup both corrupted: %s / %s", ex, backup_ex)
    46→                print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    47→                return empty_plan()
    48→        else:
    49→            logger.warning("Plan file corrupted (%s). Starting fresh.", ex)
    50→            print(f"  Warning: Plan file corrupted ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    51→            return empty_plan()
    52→
    53→    if not isinstance(data, dict):
    54→        logger.warning("Plan file root is not a JSON object. Starting fresh.")
    55→        print("  Warning: Plan file root must be a JSON object. Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    56→        return empty_plan()
    57→
    58→    version = data.get("version", 1)
    59→    if version > PLAN_VERSION:
    60→        logger.warning("Plan file version %d > supported %d.", version, PLAN_VERSION)
    61→        print(
    62→            f"  Warning: Plan file version {version} is newer than supported "
    63→            f"({PLAN_VERSION}). Some features may not work correctly.",
    64→            file=sys.stderr,
    65→        )
    66→
    67→    ensure_plan_defaults(data)
    68→    try:
    69→        validate_plan(data)
    70→    except ValueError as ex:
    71→        logger.warning("Plan invariants invalid (%s). Starting fresh.", ex)
    72→        print(f"  Warning: Plan invariants invalid ({ex}). Starting fresh.", file=sys.stderr)
    73→        return empty_plan()
    74→
    75→    return data  # type: ignore[return-value]
    76→
    77→
    78→def save_plan(plan: PlanModel | dict, path: Path | None = None) -> None:
    79→    """Validate and save plan to disk atomically."""
    80→    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    81→    plan["updated"] = utc_now()
    82→    validate_plan(plan)
    83→
    84→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
    85→    plan_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    86→
    87→    content = json.dumps(plan, indent=2, default=json_default) + "\n"
    88→
    89→    if plan_path.exists():
    90→        backup = plan_path.with_suffix(".json.bak")
    91→        try:
    92→            shutil.copy2(str(plan_path), str(backup))
    93→        except OSError as backup_ex:
    94→            logger.debug("Failed to create plan backup: %s", backup_ex)
    95→
    96→    try:
    97→        safe_write_text(plan_path, content)
    98→    except OSError as ex:
    99→        print(f"  Warning: Could not save plan: {ex}", file=sys.stderr)
   100→        raise
   101→
   102→
   103→def plan_path_for_state(state_path: Path) -> Path:
   104→    """Derive plan.json path from a state file path."""
   105→    return state_path.parent / "plan.json"
   106→
   107→
   108→def has_living_plan(path: Path | None = None) -> bool:
   109→    """Return True if a plan.json exists and has user intent."""
   110→    plan_path = path or PLAN_FILE
   111→    if not plan_path.exists():
   112→        return False
   113→    plan = load_plan(plan_path)
   114→    return bool(
   115→        plan.get("queue_order")
   116→        or plan.get("overrides")
   117→        or plan.get("clusters")
   118→    )
   119→
   120→
   121→__all__ = [
   122→    "PLAN_FILE",
   123→    "has_living_plan",
   124→    "load_plan",
   125→    "plan_path_for_state",
   126→    "save_plan",
   127→]
   128→
```

1→"""Helper routines shared by resolve render output."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     7→
     8→
     9→def score_snapshot_or_warn(state: dict):
    10→    snapshot = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    11→    if (
    12→        snapshot.overall is None
    13→        or snapshot.objective is None
    14→        or snapshot.strict is None
    15→        or snapshot.verified is None
    16→    ):
    17→        print(colorize("\n  Scores unavailable — run `desloppify scan`.", "yellow"))
    18→        return None
    19→    return snapshot
    20→
    21→
    22→def print_strict_gap_note(status: str, *, overall: float, strict: float) -> None:
    23→    if status != "wontfix":
    24→        return
    25→    strict_gap = round(overall - strict, 1)
    26→    if strict_gap <= 0:
    27→        return
    28→    print(
    29→        colorize(
    30→            f"  Note: wontfix items still count against strict score. "
    31→            f"Current gap: overall {overall:.1f} vs strict {strict:.1f} ({strict_gap:.1f} pts of hidden debt).",
    32→            "yellow",
    33→        )
    34→    )
    35→
    36→
    37→def print_post_resolve_guidance(
    38→    *,
    39→    status: str,
    40→    has_review_findings: bool,
    41→    overall_delta: float,
    42→) -> None:
    43→    if has_review_findings and abs(overall_delta) < 0.05:
    44→        print(
    45→            colorize(
    46→                "  Scores unchanged (review findings don't affect scores directly).",
    47→                "yellow",
    48→            )
    49→        )
    50→        print(
    51→            colorize(
    52→                "  Run `desloppify review --prepare` to get updated assessment scores.",
    53→                "dim",
    54→            )
    55→        )
    56→        return
    57→    if status == "fixed":
    58→        print(
    59→            colorize(
    60→                "  Verified score updates after a scan confirms the finding disappeared.",
    61→                "yellow",
    62→            )
    63→        )
    64→
    65→
    66→__all__ = [
    67→    "print_post_resolve_guidance",
    68→    "print_strict_gap_note",
    69→    "score_snapshot_or_warn",
    70→]
    71→

1→"""Plan-aware queue progress and frozen score display helpers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from dataclasses import dataclass
     6→
     7→from desloppify.core.exception_sets import PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→from desloppify.engine._work_queue.helpers import is_subjective_queue_item
    10→
    11→
    12→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    13→# QueueBreakdown — single source of truth for queue numbers
    14→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    15→
    16→@dataclass(frozen=True)
    17→class QueueBreakdown:
    18→    """All numbers needed to render the standardized queue display."""
    19→
    20→    queue_total: int = 0
    21→    plan_ordered: int = 0
    22→    skipped: int = 0
    23→    subjective: int = 0
    24→    workflow: int = 0
    25→    focus_cluster: str | None = None
    26→    focus_cluster_count: int = 0
    27→    focus_cluster_total: int = 0
    28→
    29→
    30→def plan_aware_queue_breakdown(
    31→    state: dict,
    32→    plan: dict | None = None,
    33→) -> QueueBreakdown:
    34→    """Build a full :class:`QueueBreakdown` from a single ``build_work_queue`` call."""
    35→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
    36→
    37→    result = build_work_queue(
    38→        state,
    39→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
    40→            status="open",
    41→            count=None,
    42→            plan=plan,
    43→            collapse_clusters=True,
    44→            include_skipped=False,
    45→        ),
    46→    )
    47→
    48→    queue_total = result["total"]
    49→
    50→    # Count subjective and workflow items in the queue.
    51→    # Collapsed clusters whose members are all subjective count as subjective.
    52→    items = result.get("items", [])
    53→    subjective = sum(
    54→        1 for item in items
    55→        if is_subjective_queue_item(item)
    56→    )
    57→    workflow = sum(
    58→        1 for item in items
    59→        if item.get("kind") in ("workflow_stage", "workflow_action")
    60→    )
    61→
    62→    # Plan-derived counts
    63→    plan_ordered = 0
    64→    skipped = 0
    65→    if plan:
    66→        skipped = len(plan.get("skipped", {}))
    67→        # plan_ordered = items that are in queue_order minus skipped
    68→        queue_order = plan.get("queue_order", [])
    69→        skipped_ids = set(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    70→        plan_ordered = sum(1 for fid in queue_order if fid not in skipped_ids)
    71→
    72→    # Focus cluster info
    73→    focus_cluster = None
    74→    focus_cluster_count = 0
    75→    focus_cluster_total = 0
    76→    if plan:
    77→        active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    78→        if active:
    79→            focus_cluster = active
    80→            cluster_data = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(active, {})
    81→            focus_cluster_total = len(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
    82→            # Count how many cluster members are still in the queue
    83→            cluster_member_ids = set(cluster_data.get("finding_ids", []))
    84→            open_findings = {
    85→                fid
    86→                for fid, f in state.get("findings", {}).items()
    87→                if f.get("status") == "open"
    88→            }
    89→            focus_cluster_count = len(cluster_member_ids & open_findings)
    90→
    91→    return QueueBreakdown(
    92→        queue_total=queue_total,
    93→        plan_ordered=plan_ordered,
    94→        skipped=skipped,
    95→        subjective=subjective,
    96→        workflow=workflow,
    97→        focus_cluster=focus_cluster,
    98→        focus_cluster_count=focus_cluster_count,
    99→        focus_cluster_total=focus_cluster_total,
   100→    )
   101→
   102→
   103→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   104→# Formatting helpers — single source of truth for queue display
   105→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   106→
   107→def format_queue_headline(breakdown: QueueBreakdown) -> str:
   108→    """The one-line Queue summary. Same format everywhere.
   109→
   110→    Examples::
   111→
   112→        Queue: 1934 items (292 planned · 23 skipped)
   113→        Queue: 1934 items
   114→    """
   115→    n = breakdown.queue_total
   116→    label = f"Queue: {n} item{'s' if n != 1 else ''}"
   117→
   118→    # Parenthesized segments
   119→    segments: list[str] = []
   120→    if breakdown.workflow > 0:
   121→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.workflow} planning step{'s' if breakdown.workflow != 1 else ''}")
   122→    if breakdown.plan_ordered > 0:
   123→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.plan_ordered} planned")
   124→    if breakdown.skipped > 0:
   125→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.skipped} skipped")
   126→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   127→        segments.append(f"{breakdown.subjective} subjective")
   128→    if segments:
   129→        sep = " \u00b7 "
   130→        detail = sep.join(segments)
   131→        return f"{label} ({detail})"
   132→    return label
   133→
   134→
   135→def format_queue_block(
   136→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   137→    *,
   138→    frozen_score: float | None = None,
   139→) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
   140→    """Full queue block: focus banner + queue line + contextual hints.
   141→
   142→    Returns a list of ``(text, style)`` pairs ready for ``colorize()``.
   143→    """
   144→    lines: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
   145→
   146→    # Focus banner (prominent, separate)
   147→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   148→        focus_line = (
   149→            f"  Focus: `{breakdown.focus_cluster}` "
   150→            f"\u2014 {breakdown.focus_cluster_count}/{breakdown.focus_cluster_total}"
   151→            f" items remaining"
   152→        )
   153→        lines.append((focus_line, "cyan"))
   154→
   155→    # Frozen score line
   156→    if frozen_score is not None:
   157→        lines.append((
   158→            f"  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {frozen_score:.1f}/100",
   159→            "cyan",
   160→        ))
   161→
   162→    # Queue headline — always the same
   163→    lines.append((f"  {format_queue_headline(breakdown)}", "bold"))
   164→
   165→    # Contextual hints (dim)
   166→    if breakdown.focus_cluster:
   167→        lines.append((
   168→            f"  Unfocus: `desloppify plan focus --clear`"
   169→            f" \u00b7 Cluster details: `desloppify next --cluster {breakdown.focus_cluster} --count 10`",
   170→            "dim",
   171→        ))
   172→    elif breakdown.plan_ordered > 0 or breakdown.skipped > 0:
   173→        lines.append((
   174→            "  Details: `desloppify plan queue`"
   175→            " \u00b7 Skip: `desloppify plan skip <id>`",
   176→            "dim",
   177→        ))
   178→    else:
   179→        lines.append((
   180→            "  Start planning: `desloppify plan`",
   181→            "dim",
   182→        ))
   183→
   184→    return lines
   185→
   186→
   187→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   188→# Legacy / backward-compatible helpers
   189→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   190→
   191→def plan_aware_queue_count(state: dict, plan: dict | None = None) -> int:
   192→    """Count remaining plan-aware queue items (skips excluded, clusters collapsed)."""
   193→    from desloppify.engine.work_queue import QueueBuildOptions, build_work_queue
   194→
   195→    result = build_work_queue(
   196→        state,
   197→        options=QueueBuildOptions(
   198→            status="open",
   199→            count=None,
   200→            plan=plan,
   201→            collapse_clusters=True,
   202→            include_skipped=False,
   203→        ),
   204→    )
   205→    return result["total"]
   206→
   207→
   208→def get_plan_start_strict(plan: dict | None) -> float | None:
   209→    """Extract the frozen plan-start strict score, or None if unset."""
   210→    if not plan:
   211→        return None
   212→    return plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict")
   213→
   214→
   215→def print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   216→    plan: dict,
   217→    queue_remaining: int,
   218→    *,
   219→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown | None = None,
   220→) -> None:
   221→    """Show frozen plan-start score + queue progress.
   222→
   223→    When *breakdown* is provided, uses the standardized queue block.
   224→    Otherwise falls back to the legacy two-line format.
   225→    """
   226→    scores = plan.get("plan_start_scores", {})
   227→    strict = scores.get("strict")
   228→    if strict is None:
   229→        return
   230→
   231→    if breakdown is not None:
   232→        block = format_queue_block(breakdown, frozen_score=strict)
   233→        print()
   234→        for text, style in block:
   235→            print(colorize(text, style))
   236→        if queue_remaining > 0:
   237→            print(colorize(
   238→                "  Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   239→                "dim",
   240→            ))
   241→        return
   242→
   243→    # Legacy fallback
   244→    print(
   245→        colorize(
   246→            f"\n  Score (frozen at plan start): strict {strict:.1f}/100",
   247→            "cyan",
   248→        )
   249→    )
   250→    print(
   251→        colorize(
   252→            f"  Queue: {queue_remaining} item{'s' if queue_remaining != 1 else ''}"
   253→            " remaining. Score will not update until the queue is clear and you run `desloppify scan`.",
   254→            "dim",
   255→        )
   256→    )
   257→
   258→
   259→def _print_objective_drained_banner(
   260→    frozen_strict: float,
   261→    remaining: int,
   262→    breakdown: QueueBreakdown,
   263→) -> None:
   264→    """Show a phase-transition banner when objective work is drained."""
   265→    kind_labels: list[str] = []
   266→    if breakdown.subjective > 0:
   267→        kind_labels.append("subjective")
   268→    if breakdown.workflow > 0:
   269→        kind_labels.append("workflow")
   270→    kind_desc = " + ".join(kind_labels) if kind_labels else "non-objective"
   271→    print(colorize(
   272→        f"\n  Objective queue complete (plan-start was {frozen_strict:.1f})."
   273→        f" {remaining} {kind_desc} item{'s' if remaining != 1 else ''} remain.",
   274→        "cyan",
   275→    ))
   276→    print(colorize(
   277→        "  Run `desloppify next` for remaining work,"
   278→        " then `desloppify scan` to finalize.",
   279→        "dim",
   280→    ))
   281→
   282→
   283→def print_execution_or_reveal(
   284→    state: dict,
   285→    prev,
   286→    plan: dict | None,
   287→) -> None:
   288→    """Context-aware score display: frozen plan-start score or live scores.
   289→
   290→    Three display modes:
   291→
   292→    1. **Frozen** — objective queue items remain → show frozen plan-start score.
   293→    2. **Phase transition** — objective drained, subjective/workflow remains
   294→       → show live scores + transition banner.
   295→    3. **Live** — no active plan cycle or queue is clear → show live scores.
   296→    """
   297→    if plan and plan.get("plan_start_scores", {}).get("strict") is not None:
   298→        try:
   299→            breakdown = plan_aware_queue_breakdown(state, plan)
   300→            remaining = breakdown.queue_total
   301→        except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   302→            breakdown = None
   303→            remaining = 0
   304→        if remaining > 0 and breakdown is not None:
   305→            objective_remaining = max(
   306→                0, remaining - breakdown.subjective - breakdown.workflow
   307→            )
   308→            if objective_remaining == 0:
   309→                # Phase transition — live scores + banner
   310→                from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import (
   311→                    print_score_update,
   312→                )
   313→
   314→                print_score_update(state, prev)
   315→                _print_objective_drained_banner(
   316→                    plan["plan_start_scores"]["strict"],
   317→                    remaining,
   318→                    breakdown,
   319→                )
   320→                return
   321→            # Frozen — objective work remains
   322→            print_frozen_score_with_queue_context(
   323→                plan, remaining, breakdown=breakdown,
   324→            )
   325→            return
   326→
   327→    # Live — no active plan cycle or queue is clear
   328→    from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score_update import print_score_update
   329→
   330→    print_score_update(state, prev)
   331→
   332→
   333→def show_score_with_plan_context(state: dict, prev) -> None:
   334→    """Load plan (best-effort) and show frozen or live score context.
   335→
   336→    Encapsulates the common load_plan + PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS + reveal
   337→    choreography so command modules don't each repeat it.
   338→    """
   339→    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
   340→
   341→    try:
   342→        plan = load_plan()
   343→    except PLAN_LOAD_EXCEPTIONS:
   344→        plan = None
   345→    print_execution_or_reveal(state, prev, plan)
   346→
   347→
   348→__all__ = [
   349→    "QueueBreakdown",
   350→    "format_queue_block",
   351→    "format_queue_headline",
   352→    "get_plan_start_strict",
   353→    "plan_aware_queue_breakdown",
   354→    "plan_aware_queue_count",
   355→    "print_execution_or_reveal",
   356→    "print_frozen_score_with_queue_context",
   357→    "show_score_with_plan_context",
   358→]
   359→

1→"""Migration/default helpers for living plan schema payloads."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from typing import Any
     6→
     7→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now
     8→
     9→
    10→def ensure_container_types(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    11→    if not isinstance(plan.get("queue_order"), list):
    12→        plan["queue_order"] = []
    13→    if not isinstance(plan.get("deferred"), list):
    14→        plan["deferred"] = []
    15→    if not isinstance(plan.get("skipped"), dict):
    16→        plan["skipped"] = {}
    17→    if not isinstance(plan.get("overrides"), dict):
    18→        plan["overrides"] = {}
    19→    if not isinstance(plan.get("clusters"), dict):
    20→        plan["clusters"] = {}
    21→    if not isinstance(plan.get("superseded"), dict):
    22→        plan["superseded"] = {}
    23→    if not isinstance(plan.get("promoted_ids"), list):
    24→        plan["promoted_ids"] = []
    25→    if not isinstance(plan.get("plan_start_scores"), dict):
    26→        plan["plan_start_scores"] = {}
    27→    if not isinstance(plan.get("execution_log"), list):
    28→        plan["execution_log"] = []
    29→    if not isinstance(plan.get("epic_triage_meta"), dict):
    30→        plan["epic_triage_meta"] = {}
    31→
    32→
    33→def migrate_deferred_to_skipped(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    34→    deferred: list[str] = plan["deferred"]
    35→    skipped: dict[str, dict[str, Any]] = plan["skipped"]
    36→    if not deferred:
    37→        return
    38→
    39→    now = utc_now()
    40→    for finding_id in list(deferred):
    41→        if finding_id in skipped:
    42→            continue
    43→        skipped[finding_id] = {
    44→            "finding_id": finding_id,
    45→            "kind": "temporary",
    46→            "reason": None,
    47→            "note": None,
    48→            "attestation": None,
    49→            "created_at": now,
    50→            "review_after": None,
    51→            "skipped_at_scan": 0,
    52→        }
    53→    deferred.clear()
    54→
    55→
    56→def normalize_cluster_defaults(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    57→    for cluster in plan["clusters"].values():
    58→        if not isinstance(cluster, dict):
    59→            continue
    60→        if not isinstance(cluster.get("finding_ids"), list):
    61→            cluster["finding_ids"] = []
    62→        cluster.setdefault("auto", False)
    63→        cluster.setdefault("cluster_key", "")
    64→        cluster.setdefault("action", None)
    65→        cluster.setdefault("user_modified", False)
    66→
    67→
    68→def migrate_epics_to_clusters(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    69→    """Migrate v3 top-level ``epics`` dict into ``clusters`` (v4 unification)."""
    70→    epics = plan.pop("epics", None)
    71→    if not isinstance(epics, dict) or not epics:
    72→        return
    73→    clusters = plan["clusters"]
    74→    now = utc_now()
    75→    for name, epic in epics.items():
    76→        if not isinstance(epic, dict):
    77→            continue
    78→        if name in clusters:
    79→            continue
    80→        clusters[name] = {
    81→            "name": name,
    82→            "description": epic.get("thesis", ""),
    83→            "finding_ids": epic.get("finding_ids", []),
    84→            "auto": True,
    85→            "cluster_key": f"epic::{name}",
    86→            "action": f"desloppify plan focus {name}",
    87→            "user_modified": False,
    88→            "created_at": epic.get("created_at", now),
    89→            "updated_at": epic.get("updated_at", now),
    90→            "thesis": epic.get("thesis", ""),
    91→            "direction": epic.get("direction", "simplify"),
    92→            "root_cause": epic.get("root_cause", ""),
    93→            "supersedes": epic.get("supersedes", []),
    94→            "dismissed": epic.get("dismissed", []),
    95→            "agent_safe": epic.get("agent_safe", False),
    96→            "dependency_order": epic.get("dependency_order", 999),
    97→            "action_steps": epic.get("action_steps", []),
    98→            "source_clusters": epic.get("source_clusters", []),
    99→            "status": epic.get("status", "pending"),
   100→            "triage_version": epic.get("triage_version", epic.get("synthesis_version", 0)),
   101→        }
   102→
   103→
   104→def migrate_v5_to_v6(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   105→    """Migrate v5 → v6: unified queue system."""
   106→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import (
   107→        TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS,
   108→        WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID,
   109→    )
   110→
   111→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   112→
   113→    # Handle legacy synthesis::pending or triage::pending
   114→    for legacy_pending in ("synthesis::pending", "triage::pending"):
   115→        if legacy_pending in order:
   116→            idx = order.index(legacy_pending)
   117→            order.remove(legacy_pending)
   118→            meta = plan.get("epic_triage_meta", plan.get("epic_synthesis_meta", {}))
   119→            confirmed = set(meta.get("triage_stages", meta.get("synthesis_stages", {})).keys())
   120→            stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   121→            to_inject = [
   122→                stage_id
   123→                for stage_id, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names)
   124→                if name not in confirmed and stage_id not in order
   125→            ]
   126→            for offset, stage_id in enumerate(to_inject):
   127→                order.insert(idx + offset, stage_id)
   128→            break
   129→
   130→    if plan.pop("pending_plan_gate", False):
   131→        if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID not in order:
   132→            insert_at = 0
   133→            for idx, finding_id in enumerate(order):
   134→                if finding_id.startswith("triage::") or finding_id.startswith("synthesis::"):
   135→                    insert_at = idx + 1
   136→            order.insert(insert_at, WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID)
   137→    else:
   138→        plan.pop("pending_plan_gate", None)
   139→
   140→
   141→def migrate_synthesis_to_triage(plan: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   142→    """Migrate synthesis::* → triage::* naming throughout the plan.
   143→
   144→    - Renames ``synthesis::*`` IDs to ``triage::*`` in ``queue_order`` and ``skipped``
   145→    - Renames ``epic_synthesis_meta`` key to ``epic_triage_meta``
   146→    - Renames ``synthesis_stages`` to ``triage_stages`` inside that meta dict
   147→    - Renames ``synthesized_ids`` to ``triaged_ids`` inside that meta dict
   148→    - Renames ``synthesis_version`` to ``triage_version`` in cluster dicts
   149→    """
   150→    # Rename synthesis::* IDs in queue_order
   151→    order: list[str] = plan.get("queue_order", [])
   152→    for i, fid in enumerate(order):
   153→        if fid.startswith("synthesis::"):
   154→            order[i] = "triage::" + fid[len("synthesis::"):]
   155→
   156→    # Rename synthesis::* IDs in skipped
   157→    skipped: dict = plan.get("skipped", {})
   158→    synth_keys = [k for k in skipped if k.startswith("synthesis::")]
   159→    for old_key in synth_keys:
   160→        new_key = "triage::" + old_key[len("synthesis::"):]
   161→        entry = skipped.pop(old_key)
   162→        if isinstance(entry, dict):
   163→            entry["finding_id"] = new_key
   164→        skipped[new_key] = entry
   165→
   166→    # Rename epic_synthesis_meta → epic_triage_meta
   167→    if "epic_synthesis_meta" in plan:
   168→        meta = plan.pop("epic_synthesis_meta")
   169→        if isinstance(meta, dict):
   170→            # Rename synthesis_stages → triage_stages
   171→            if "synthesis_stages" in meta:
   172→                meta["triage_stages"] = meta.pop("synthesis_stages")
   173→            # Rename synthesized_ids → triaged_ids
   174→            if "synthesized_ids" in meta:
   175→                meta["triaged_ids"] = meta.pop("synthesized_ids")
   176→        plan["epic_triage_meta"] = meta
   177→
   178→    # Rename synthesized_out → triaged_out skip kind
   179→    for entry in skipped.values():
   180→        if isinstance(entry, dict) and entry.get("kind") == "synthesized_out":
   181→            entry["kind"] = "triaged_out"
   182→
   183→    # Rename synthesis_version → triage_version in clusters
   184→    for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
   185→        if not isinstance(cluster, dict):
   186→            continue
   187→        if "synthesis_version" in cluster:
   188→            cluster["triage_version"] = cluster.pop("synthesis_version")
   189→
