1→"""State-application helpers for resolve command flows."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import argparse
     6→
     7→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     8→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.query import write_query_best_effort as _write_query_best_effort
     9→from desloppify.core.output_contract import OutputResult
    10→from desloppify.engine.plan import has_living_plan, load_plan
    11→
    12→from .selection import ResolveQueryContext
    13→
    14→
    15→def _try_expand_cluster(pattern: str) -> list[str] | None:
    16→    """If pattern matches a cluster name, return its finding IDs."""
    17→    try:
    18→        if not has_living_plan():
    19→            return None
    20→        plan = load_plan()
    21→        cluster = plan.get("clusters", {}).get(pattern)
    22→        if cluster and cluster.get("finding_ids"):
    23→            return list(cluster["finding_ids"])
    24→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as exc:
    25→        _ = exc
    26→    return None
    27→
    28→
    29→def _resolve_all_patterns(
    30→    state: dict,
    31→    args: argparse.Namespace,
    32→    *,
    33→    attestation: str | None,
    34→) -> list[str]:
    35→    all_resolved: list[str] = []
    36→    for pattern in args.patterns:
    37→        # Check if pattern is a cluster name — expand to member IDs
    38→        cluster_ids = _try_expand_cluster(pattern)
    39→        if cluster_ids:
    40→            for fid in cluster_ids:
    41→                resolved = state_mod.resolve_findings(
    42→                    state,
    43→                    fid,
    44→                    args.status,
    45→                    args.note,
    46→                    attestation=attestation,
    47→                )
    48→                all_resolved.extend(resolved)
    49→            continue
    50→
    51→        resolved = state_mod.resolve_findings(
    52→            state,
    53→            pattern,
    54→            args.status,
    55→            args.note,
    56→            attestation=attestation,
    57→        )
    58→        all_resolved.extend(resolved)
    59→    return all_resolved
    60→
    61→
    62→def write_query(payload: dict) -> OutputResult:
    63→    """Backward-compatible resolve query writer seam."""
    64→    return _write_query_best_effort(
    65→        payload,
    66→        context="resolve query payload update",
    67→    )
    68→
    69→
    70→def _write_resolve_query_entry(context: ResolveQueryContext) -> None:
    71→    scores = state_mod.score_snapshot(context.state)
    72→    write_query(
    73→        {
    74→            "command": "resolve",
    75→            "patterns": context.patterns,
    76→            "status": context.status,
    77→            "resolved": context.resolved,
    78→            "count": len(context.resolved),
    79→            "next_command": context.next_command,
    80→            "overall_score": scores.overall,
    81→            "objective_score": scores.objective,
    82→            "strict_score": scores.strict,
    83→            "verified_strict_score": scores.verified,
    84→            "prev_overall_score": context.prev_overall,
    85→            "prev_objective_score": context.prev_objective,
    86→            "prev_strict_score": context.prev_strict,
    87→            "prev_verified_strict_score": context.prev_verified,
    88→            "attestation": context.attestation,
    89→            "narrative": context.narrative,
    90→        }
    91→    )
    92→

     8→from desloppify.core import config as config_mod

    10→
    11→
    12→def _save_state_or_exit(state: dict, state_file: str) -> None:
    13→    """Persist state with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    14→    try:
    15→        state_mod.save_state(state, state_file)
    16→    except OSError as exc:
    17→        print_error(f"could not save state: {exc}")
    18→        sys.exit(1)
    19→
    20→
    21→def _save_config_or_exit(config: dict) -> None:
    22→    """Persist config with a consistent CLI error boundary."""
    23→    try:
    24→        config_mod.save_config(config)
    25→    except OSError as exc:
    26→        print_error(f"could not save config: {exc}")
    27→        sys.exit(1)
    28→
    29→
    30→__all__ = ["_save_config_or_exit", "_save_state_or_exit"]

1→"""CLI entry point: parse args, load shared context, dispatch command handlers."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import logging
     6→import sys
     7→from pathlib import Path
     8→
     9→from desloppify.app.cli_support.parser import create_parser as _create_parser
    10→from desloppify.app.commands.registry import get_command_handlers
    11→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.lang import LangResolutionError, resolve_lang
    12→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import CommandRuntime
    13→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.state import state_path
    14→from desloppify.core.registry import detector_names, on_detector_registered
    15→from desloppify.core.config import load_config
    16→from desloppify.core.discovery_api import set_exclusions
    17→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
    18→from desloppify.core.paths_api import get_default_path, get_project_root
    19→from desloppify.core.runtime_state import runtime_scope
    20→from desloppify.languages import available_langs
    21→from desloppify.state import load_state
    22→
    23→_DETECTOR_NAMES: list[str] | None = None
    24→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    25→# Backward-compatible test patch hook; runtime path resolution uses get_project_root().
    26→PROJECT_ROOT = get_project_root()
    27→_PROJECT_ROOT_SENTINEL = PROJECT_ROOT
    28→
    29→
    30→def _get_detector_names() -> list[str]:
    31→    """Return cached detector names, computing on first access."""
    32→    global _DETECTOR_NAMES
    33→    if _DETECTOR_NAMES is None:
    34→        _DETECTOR_NAMES = detector_names()
    35→    return _DETECTOR_NAMES
    36→
    37→
    38→def _invalidate_detector_names_cache() -> None:
    39→    """Invalidate detector-name cache when runtime registrations change."""
    40→    global _DETECTOR_NAMES
    41→    _DETECTOR_NAMES = None
    42→
    43→
    44→on_detector_registered(_invalidate_detector_names_cache)
    45→
    46→def create_parser():
    47→    """Return the top-level argparse parser."""
    48→    return _create_parser(langs=available_langs(), detector_names=_get_detector_names())
    49→
    50→
    51→def _apply_persisted_exclusions(args, config: dict):
    52→    """Merge CLI --exclude with persisted config.exclude and apply globally."""
    53→    cli_exclusions = getattr(args, "exclude", None) or []
    54→    persisted = config.get("exclude", [])
    55→    combined = list(cli_exclusions) + [e for e in persisted if e not in cli_exclusions]
    56→    if not combined:
    57→        return
    58→    set_exclusions(combined)
    59→    if cli_exclusions:
    60→        print(
    61→            colorize(f"  Excluding: {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"),
    62→            file=sys.stderr,
    63→        )
    64→        return
    65→    print(
    66→        colorize(
    67→            f"  Excluding (from config): {', '.join(combined)}", "dim"
    68→        ),
    69→        file=sys.stderr,
    70→    )
    71→
    72→
    73→def _resolve_default_path(args) -> None:
    74→    """Fill args.path from detected language or default source path.
    75→
    76→    For the review command, the last scan path (stored in state) is used as the
    77→    default so that ``desloppify review --prepare`` works on the same scope as
    78→    the preceding scan even when the project files are not under ``src/``.
    79→    """
    80→    if getattr(args, "path", None) is not None:
    81→        return
    82→    runtime_root = (
    83→        PROJECT_ROOT.resolve()
    84→        if PROJECT_ROOT is not _PROJECT_ROOT_SENTINEL and isinstance(PROJECT_ROOT, Path)
    85→        else get_project_root()
    86→    )
    87→    if getattr(args, "command", None) == "review":
    88→        try:
    89→            state_file = state_path(args)
    90→            if state_file:
    91→                saved = load_state(state_file)
    92→                saved_path = saved.get("scan_path")
    93→                if saved_path:
    94→                    args.path = str((runtime_root / saved_path).resolve())
    95→                    return
    96→        except (OSError, KeyError, ValueError, TypeError, AttributeError) as exc:
    97→            logger.debug("Failed to resolve default path from saved state: %s", exc)
    98→    lang = resolve_lang(args)
    99→    if lang:
   100→        args.path = str(runtime_root / lang.default_src)

    28→
    29→USAGE_EXAMPLES = """
    30→core workflow:
    31→  scan       Run detectors, update state, show diff
    32→  status     Score dashboard with dimension health
    33→  next       Show next highest-priority item to work on
    34→  plan       Living plan: prioritize, cluster, skip, done, annotate
    35→
    36→investigation:
    37→  show       Dig into findings by file/dir/detector/ID
    38→  tree       Annotated codebase tree (zoom with --focus)
    39→  detect     Run a single detector directly (bypass state)
    40→
    41→maintenance:
    42→  fix        Auto-fix mechanical issues
    43→  ignore     Suppress findings matching a pattern
    44→  exclude    Exclude path pattern from scanning
    45→  move       Move file/dir and update import references
    46→  review     Holistic subjective review (LLM-based)
    47→
    48→setup & admin:
    49→  zone       Show/set zone classifications
    50→  config     Project configuration
    51→  viz        Interactive HTML treemap
    52→  langs      List language plugins
    53→  dev        Developer utilities
    54→  update-skill  Install/update agent skill document
    55→
    56→examples:
    57→  desloppify scan
    58→  desloppify scan --skip-slow --profile ci
    59→  desloppify plan                        # full prioritized markdown
    60→  desloppify plan queue                  # compact table of all items
    61→  desloppify next --count 10             # top 10 queue items
    62→  desloppify show src/components/Modal.tsx
    63→  desloppify plan done "unused::src/foo.tsx::React" \\
    64→    --note "removed import" --attest "I have actually ..."
    65→  desloppify review --run-batches --parallel --scan-after-import
    66→"""
    67→
    68→
    69→class _NoAbbrevArgumentParser(argparse.ArgumentParser):
    70→    """Argparse parser variant that disables long-option abbreviation."""
    71→
    72→    def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
    73→        kwargs.setdefault("allow_abbrev", False)
    74→        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
    75→
    76→
    77→def _cli_version_string() -> str:
    78→    """Return the best available CLI version label."""
    79→    try:
    80→        return f"desloppify {get_version('desloppify')}"
    81→    except PackageNotFoundError:
    82→        return "desloppify (version unknown)"
    83→
    84→
    85→def create_parser(*, langs: list[str], detector_names: list[str]) -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
    86→    """Build top-level CLI parser with all subcommands."""
    87→    lang_help = ", ".join(langs) if langs else "registered languages"
    88→
    89→    parser = _NoAbbrevArgumentParser(
    90→        prog="desloppify",
    91→        description="Desloppify — codebase health tracker",
    92→        epilog=USAGE_EXAMPLES,
    93→        formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,
    94→    )
    95→    parser.add_argument(
    96→        "--lang",
    97→        type=str,
    98→        default=None,
    99→        help=f"Language to scan ({lang_help}). Auto-detected if omitted.",
   100→    )
   101→    parser.add_argument(
   102→        "--exclude",
   103→        action="append",
   104→        default=None,
   105→        metavar="PATTERN",
   106→        help="Path pattern to exclude (component/prefix match; repeatable)",
   107→    )
   108→    parser.add_argument(
   109→        "--version",
   110→        "-V",
   111→        action="version",
   112→        version=_cli_version_string(),
   113→    )
   114→    sub = parser.add_subparsers(
   115→        dest="command",
   116→        required=True,
   117→        parser_class=_NoAbbrevArgumentParser,
   118→    )
   119→    _add_scan_parser(sub)
   120→    _add_status_parser(sub)
   121→    _add_tree_parser(sub)
   122→    _add_show_parser(sub)
   123→    _add_next_parser(sub)
   124→    _add_ignore_parser(sub)
   125→    _add_exclude_parser(sub)
   126→    _add_fix_parser(sub, langs)
   127→    _add_plan_parser(sub)
   128→    _add_viz_parser(sub)
   129→    _add_detect_parser(sub, detector_names)
   130→    _add_move_parser(sub)
   131→    _add_review_parser(sub)
   132→    _add_zone_parser(sub)
   133→    _add_config_parser(sub)
   134→    _add_dev_parser(sub)
   135→    _add_langs_parser(sub)
   136→    _add_update_skill_parser(sub)
   137→    return parser
   138→
   139→
   140→__all__ = ["USAGE_EXAMPLES", "create_parser"]
   141→

1→"""State resolution operations (match + resolve)."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→import copy
     6→
     7→__all__ = [
     8→    "coerce_assessment_score",
     9→    "match_findings",
    10→    "resolve_findings",
    11→]
    12→
    13→from desloppify.core._internal.text_utils import is_numeric
    14→from desloppify.engine._state.filtering import _matches_pattern
    15→from desloppify.engine._state.schema import (
    16→    StateModel,
    17→    ensure_state_defaults,
    18→    utc_now,
    19→    validate_state_invariants,
    20→)
    21→from desloppify.engine._state.scoring import _recompute_stats
    22→
    23→
    24→def coerce_assessment_score(value: object) -> float | None:
    25→    """Normalize a subjective assessment score payload to a 0-100 float.
    26→
    27→    Returns ``None`` when the value cannot be interpreted as a numeric score
    28→    (e.g. bools, non-numeric strings, missing keys).
    29→    """
    30→    if is_numeric(value):
    31→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(value))), 1)
    32→    if isinstance(value, dict):
    33→        raw = value.get("score")
    34→        if not is_numeric(raw):
    35→            return None
    36→        return round(max(0.0, min(100.0, float(raw))), 1)
    37→    return None
    38→
    39→
    40→def _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
    41→    state: StateModel,
    42→    *,
    43→    status: str,
    44→    resolved_findings: list[dict],
    45→    now: str,
    46→) -> None:
    47→    """Mark subjective assessments as stale when review findings are resolved.
    48→
    49→    The assessment score is preserved (not zeroed) — only a fresh review import
    50→    should change dimension scores.  The stale marker tells the UI to prompt
    51→    for a re-review.
    52→    """
    53→    assessments = state.get("subjective_assessments")
    54→    if not isinstance(assessments, dict) or not assessments:
    55→        return
    56→
    57→    touched_dimensions: set[str] = set()
    58→    for finding in resolved_findings:
    59→        if finding.get("detector") != "review":
    60→            continue
    61→        dimension = str(finding.get("detail", {}).get("dimension", "")).strip()
    62→        if dimension:
    63→            touched_dimensions.add(dimension)
    64→
    65→    for dimension in sorted(touched_dimensions):
    66→        if dimension not in assessments:
    67→            continue
    68→
    69→        payload = assessments.get(dimension)
    70→        if isinstance(payload, dict):
    71→            payload["needs_review_refresh"] = True
    72→            payload["refresh_reason"] = f"review_finding_{status}"
    73→            payload["stale_since"] = now
    74→        else:
    75→            assessments[dimension] = {
    76→                "score": coerce_assessment_score(payload) or 0.0,
    77→                "needs_review_refresh": True,
    78→                "refresh_reason": f"review_finding_{status}",
    79→                "stale_since": now,
    80→            }
    81→
    82→
    83→def match_findings(
    84→    state: StateModel, pattern: str, status_filter: str = "open"
    85→) -> list[dict]:
    86→    """Return findings matching *pattern* with the given status."""
    87→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
    88→    return [
    89→        finding
    90→        for finding_id, finding in state["findings"].items()
    91→        if not finding.get("suppressed")
    92→        if (status_filter == "all" or finding["status"] == status_filter)
    93→        and _matches_pattern(finding_id, finding, pattern)
    94→    ]
    95→
    96→
    97→def resolve_findings(
    98→    state: StateModel,
    99→    pattern: str,
   100→    status: str,
   101→    note: str | None = None,
   102→    attestation: str | None = None,
   103→) -> list[str]:
   104→    """Set finding status for matches and return affected finding IDs."""
   105→    ensure_state_defaults(state)
   106→    now = utc_now()
   107→    resolved: list[str] = []
   108→    resolved_findings: list[dict] = []
   109→    status_filter = "all" if status == "open" else "open"
   110→    for finding in match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter):
   111→        previous_status = str(finding.get("status", "open")).strip() or "open"
   112→        if status == "open" and previous_status == "open":
   113→            continue
   114→
   115→        extra_updates: dict[str, object] = {}
   116→        if status == "wontfix":
   117→            snapshot_scan_count = int(state.get("scan_count", 0) or 0)
   118→            extra_updates["wontfix_scan_count"] = snapshot_scan_count
   119→            extra_updates["wontfix_snapshot"] = {
   120→                "captured_at": now,
   121→                "scan_count": snapshot_scan_count,
   122→                "tier": finding.get("tier"),
   123→                "confidence": finding.get("confidence"),
   124→                "detail": copy.deepcopy(finding.get("detail", {})),
   125→            }
   126→        if status == "open":
   127→            finding["reopen_count"] = int(finding.get("reopen_count", 0) or 0) + 1
   128→            finding.pop("wontfix_scan_count", None)
   129→            finding.pop("wontfix_snapshot", None)
   130→            previous_note = finding.get("note")
   131→            next_note = note if note is not None else previous_note
   132→            extra_updates["resolved_at"] = None
   133→            extra_updates["note"] = next_note
   134→            reopen_attestation = {
   135→                "kind": "manual_reopen",
   136→                "text": attestation or note,
   137→                "attested_at": now,
   138→                "scan_verified": False,
   139→            }
   140→            reopen_attestation["previous_status"] = previous_status
   141→            extra_updates["resolution_attestation"] = reopen_attestation
   142→
   143→        updates: dict[str, object] = {
   144→            "status": status,
   145→            "note": note,
   146→            "resolved_at": now,
   147→            "suppressed": False,
   148→            "suppressed_at": None,
   149→            "suppression_pattern": None,
   150→            "resolution_attestation": {
   151→                "kind": "manual",
   152→                "text": attestation,
   153→                "attested_at": now,
   154→                "scan_verified": False,
   155→            },
   156→        }
   157→        updates.update(extra_updates)
   158→        finding.update(updates)
   159→        resolved.append(finding["id"])
   160→        resolved_findings.append(finding)
   161→
   162→    _mark_stale_assessments_on_review_resolve(
   163→        state,
   164→        status=status,
   165→        resolved_findings=resolved_findings,
   166→        now=now,
   167→    )
   168→
   169→    _recompute_stats(state, scan_path=state.get("scan_path"))
   170→    validate_state_invariants(state)
   171→    return resolved
   172→
```

1→"""Pattern → finding-ID resolution for plan commands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import PlanModel
     6→from desloppify.state import StateModel, match_findings
     7→
     8→
     9→def resolve_ids_from_patterns(
    10→    state: StateModel,
    11→    patterns: list[str],
    12→    *,
    13→    plan: PlanModel | None = None,
    14→    status_filter: str = "open",
    15→) -> list[str]:
    16→    """Resolve one or more patterns to a deduplicated list of finding IDs.
    17→
    18→    When *plan* is provided, literal IDs that exist only in the plan
    19→    (e.g. ``subjective::*`` synthetic items) are included even if they
    20→    have no corresponding entry in ``state["findings"]``.
    21→    """
    22→    seen: set[str] = set()
    23→    result: list[str] = []
    24→
    25→    # Collect all plan IDs for literal-match fallback
    26→    plan_ids: set[str] = set()
    27→    if plan is not None:
    28→        plan_ids.update(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    29→        plan_ids.update(plan.get("skipped", {}).keys())
    30→        for cluster in plan.get("clusters", {}).values():
    31→            plan_ids.update(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    32→
    33→    for pattern in patterns:
    34→        matches = match_findings(state, pattern, status_filter=status_filter)
    35→        if matches:
    36→            for finding in matches:
    37→                fid = finding["id"]
    38→                if fid not in seen:
    39→                    seen.add(fid)
    40→                    result.append(fid)
    41→        elif pattern in plan_ids and pattern not in seen:
    42→            # Literal plan ID (e.g. subjective::foo) not in state findings
    43→            seen.add(pattern)
    44→            result.append(pattern)
    45→        elif plan is not None and pattern in plan.get("clusters", {}):
    46→            # Cluster name → expand to member IDs
    47→            for fid in plan["clusters"][pattern].get("finding_ids", []):
    48→                if fid not in seen:
    49→                    seen.add(fid)
    50→                    result.append(fid)
    51→    return result
    52→
    53→
    54→__all__ = ["resolve_ids_from_patterns"]
    55→
```

    31→def _cmd_plan_generate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    32→    """Generate the prioritized markdown plan (existing behavior)."""
    33→    # Auto-resolve the create-plan workflow item when plan runs
    34→    plan = load_plan()
    35→    if WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID in plan.get("queue_order", []):
    36→        purge_ids(plan, [WORKFLOW_CREATE_PLAN_ID])
    37→        save_plan(plan)
    38→    _plan_output_impl(args)
    39→
    40→
    41→def _cmd_plan_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    42→    """Show plan metadata summary."""
    43→    plan = load_plan()
    44→    ordered = len(plan.get("queue_order", []))
    45→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
    46→    total_skipped = len(skipped)
    47→    temp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "temporary")
    48→    perm_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "permanent")
    49→    fp_count = sum(1 for e in skipped.values() if e.get("kind") == "false_positive")
    50→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
    51→    overrides = plan.get("overrides", {})
    52→    active = plan.get("active_cluster")
    53→    superseded = len(plan.get("superseded", {}))
    54→
    55→    # Count meaningful annotations (descriptions and notes)
    56→    described = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("description"))
    57→    noted = sum(1 for o in overrides.values() if o.get("note"))
    58→
    59→    print(colorize("  Living Plan Status", "bold"))
    60→    print(colorize("  " + "─" * 40, "dim"))
    61→    print(f"  Queue:            {ordered} items prioritized")
    62→    if total_skipped:
    63→        print(f"  Skipped:          {total_skipped} (temp: {temp_count}, wontfix: {perm_count}, fp: {fp_count})")
    64→    else:
    65→        print(f"  Skipped:          0")
    66→    print(f"  Clusters:         {len(clusters)}")
    67→    if clusters:
    68→        for name, cluster in clusters.items():
    69→            desc = cluster.get("description") or ""
    70→            member_count = len(cluster.get("finding_ids", []))
    71→            marker = " (focused)" if name == active else ""
    72→            desc_str = f" — {desc}" if desc else ""
    73→            print(f"    {name}: {member_count} items{desc_str}{marker}")
    74→    if described or noted:
    75→        print(f"  Annotations:      {described} described, {noted} noted")
    76→    if active:
    77→        print(f"  Focus:            {active}")
    78→    if superseded:
    79→        print(f"  Disappeared:      {superseded} (resolved or removed since last scan)")
    80→

    37→
    38→def _resolve_state_file(path: Path | None) -> Path:
    39→    return path if path is not None else state_mod.STATE_FILE
    40→
    41→
    42→def _resolve_plan_file(path: Path | None) -> Path:
    43→    return path if path is not None else PLAN_FILE
    44→
    45→
    46→def _plan_file_for_state(state_file: Path | None) -> Path | None:
    47→    if state_file is None:
    48→        return None
    49→    return plan_path_for_state(state_file)
    50→
    51→
    52→def _snapshot_file(path: Path) -> str | None:
    53→    if not path.exists():
    54→        return None
    55→    return path.read_text()
    56→
    57→
    58→def _restore_file_snapshot(path: Path, snapshot: str | None) -> None:
    59→    if snapshot is None:
    60→        try:
    61→            path.unlink()
    62→        except FileNotFoundError:
    63→            return
    64→        return
    65→    safe_write_text(path, snapshot)
    66→
    67→
    68→def _save_plan_state_transactional(
    69→    *,
    70→    plan: dict,
    71→    plan_path: Path | None,
    72→    state_data: dict,
    73→    state_path_value: Path | None,
    74→) -> None:
    75→    """Persist plan+state together; rollback both files on partial write failure."""
    76→    effective_plan_path = _resolve_plan_file(plan_path)
    77→    effective_state_path = _resolve_state_file(state_path_value)
    78→    plan_snapshot = _snapshot_file(effective_plan_path)
    79→    state_snapshot = _snapshot_file(effective_state_path)
    80→
    81→    try:
    82→        state_mod.save_state(state_data, effective_state_path)
    83→        save_plan(plan, effective_plan_path)
    84→    except Exception:
    85→        _restore_file_snapshot(effective_state_path, state_snapshot)
    86→        _restore_file_snapshot(effective_plan_path, plan_snapshot)
    87→        raise
    88→

    96→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
    97→    text: str = getattr(args, "text", "")
    98→
    99→    plan = load_plan()
   100→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   101→    if not finding_ids:

   144→def cmd_plan_skip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   145→    """Skip findings — unified command for temporary/permanent/false-positive."""
   146→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   147→    state = runtime.state
   148→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   149→        return
   150→
   151→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   152→    reason: str | None = getattr(args, "reason", None)
   153→    review_after: int | None = getattr(args, "review_after", None)
   154→    permanent: bool = getattr(args, "permanent", False)
   155→    false_positive: bool = getattr(args, "false_positive", False)
   156→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   157→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   158→
   159→    # Determine skip kind
   160→    if false_positive:
   161→        kind = "false_positive"
   162→    elif permanent:
   163→        kind = "permanent"
   164→    else:
   165→        kind = "temporary"
   166→
   167→    # Validate requirements for permanent/false_positive
   168→    if kind in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   169→        if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   170→            show_attestation_requirement(
   171→                "Permanent skip" if kind == "permanent" else "False positive",
   172→                attestation,
   173→                ATTEST_EXAMPLE,
   174→            )
   175→            return
   176→        if kind == "permanent" and not note:
   177→            print(
   178→                colorize("  --permanent requires --note to explain the decision.", "yellow"),
   179→                file=sys.stderr,
   180→            )
   181→            return
   182→
   183→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   184→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   185→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   186→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan)
   187→    if not finding_ids:
   188→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
   189→        return
   190→
   191→    # For permanent/false_positive: delegate to state layer for score impact
   192→    state_data: dict | None = None
   193→    if kind in ("permanent", "false_positive"):
   194→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   195→        status = "wontfix" if kind == "permanent" else "false_positive"
   196→        for fid in finding_ids:
   197→            state_mod.resolve_findings(
   198→                state_data, fid, status, note or "", attestation=attestation
   199→            )
   200→
   201→    scan_count = state.get("scan_count", 0)
   202→    count = skip_items(
   203→        plan,
   204→        finding_ids,
   205→        kind=kind,
   206→        reason=reason,
   207→        note=note,
   208→        attestation=attestation,
   209→        review_after=review_after,
   210→        scan_count=scan_count,
   211→    )
   212→
   213→    # Log the skip action
   214→    append_log_entry(
   215→        plan,
   216→        "skip",
   217→        finding_ids=finding_ids,
   218→        actor="user",
   219→        note=note,
   220→        detail={"kind": kind, "reason": reason},
   221→    )
   222→    if state_data is not None:
   223→        _save_plan_state_transactional(
   224→            plan=plan,
   225→            plan_path=plan_file,
   226→            state_data=state_data,
   227→            state_path_value=state_file,
   228→        )
   229→    else:
   230→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   231→
   232→    label = {"temporary": "Skipped", "permanent": "Wontfixed", "false_positive": "Marked false positive"}
   233→    print(colorize(f"  {label[kind]} {count} item(s).", "green"))
   234→    if review_after:
   235→        print(colorize(f"  Will re-surface after {review_after} scan(s).", "dim"))
   236→

   238→def cmd_plan_unskip(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   239→    """Unskip findings — bring back to queue."""
   240→    runtime = command_runtime(args)
   241→    state = runtime.state
   242→    if not require_completed_scan(state):
   243→        return
   244→
   245→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   246→
   247→    state_file = runtime.state_path
   248→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   249→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   250→    # For unskip we need to match against all statuses (skipped items may be wontfix/fp)
   251→    finding_ids = resolve_ids_from_patterns(state, patterns, plan=plan, status_filter="all")
   252→    if not finding_ids:
   253→        print(colorize("  No matching findings found.", "yellow"))
   254→        return
   255→
   256→    count, need_reopen = unskip_items(plan, finding_ids)
   257→    append_log_entry(
   258→        plan, "unskip", finding_ids=finding_ids, actor="user",
   259→        detail={"need_reopen": need_reopen},
   260→    )
   261→
   262→    # Reopen permanent/false_positive items in state
   263→    reopened: list[str] = []
   264→    if need_reopen:
   265→        state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   266→        for fid in need_reopen:
   267→            reopened.extend(state_mod.resolve_findings(state_data, fid, "open"))
   268→        _save_plan_state_transactional(
   269→            plan=plan,
   270→            plan_path=plan_file,
   271→            state_data=state_data,
   272→            state_path_value=state_file,
   273→        )
   274→        print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} finding(s) in state.", "dim"))
   275→    else:
   276→        save_plan(plan, plan_file)
   277→
   278→    print(colorize(f"  Unskipped {count} item(s) — back in queue.", "green"))
   279→

   281→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   282→# Reopen
   283→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   284→
   285→def cmd_plan_reopen(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   286→    """Reopen resolved findings from plan context."""
   287→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   288→
   289→    raw_state_path = state_path(args)
   290→    state_file = raw_state_path if isinstance(raw_state_path, Path) else Path(raw_state_path) if raw_state_path else None
   291→    state_data = state_mod.load_state(state_file)
   292→    plan_file = _plan_file_for_state(state_file)
   293→
   294→    reopened: list[str] = []
   295→    for pattern in patterns:
   296→        reopened.extend(
   297→            state_mod.resolve_findings(state_data, pattern, "open")
   298→        )
   299→
   300→    if not reopened:
   301→        print(colorize("  No resolved findings matching: " + " ".join(patterns), "yellow"))
   302→        return
   303→
   304→    # Remove from skipped if present, and ensure all reopened IDs are in queue
   305→    plan = load_plan(plan_file)
   306→    skipped = plan.get("skipped", {})
   307→    count = 0
   308→    order = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   309→    for fid in reopened:
   310→        if fid in skipped:
   311→            skipped.pop(fid)
   312→            count += 1
   313→        if fid not in order:
   314→            plan["queue_order"].append(fid)
   315→            order.add(fid)
   316→            count += 1
   317→    append_log_entry(
   318→        plan, "reopen", finding_ids=reopened, actor="user",
   319→    )
   320→    _save_plan_state_transactional(
   321→        plan=plan,
   322→        plan_path=plan_file,
   323→        state_data=state_data,
   324→        state_path_value=state_file,
   325→    )
   326→
   327→    print(colorize(f"  Reopened {len(reopened)} finding(s).", "green"))
   328→    if count:
   329→        print(colorize(f"  Plan updated: items moved back to queue.", "dim"))
   330→
   331→
   332→_CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD = 10
   333→
   334→
   335→def _check_cluster_guard(patterns: list[str], plan: dict, state: dict) -> bool:
   336→    """Return True if blocked by cluster guard, False if OK to proceed."""
   337→    clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   338→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   339→    for pattern in patterns:
   340→        if pattern in clusters:
   341→            cluster = clusters[pattern]
   342→            # Filter to alive findings only — stale IDs should not count
   343→            ids = [
   344→                fid for fid in cluster.get("finding_ids", [])
   345→                if fid in findings and findings[fid].get("status") == "open"
   346→            ]
   347→            if len(ids) == 0:
   348→                print(colorize(
   349→                    f"\n  Cluster '{pattern}' is empty — add items before marking it done.\n",
   350→                    "yellow",
   351→                ))
   352→                print(colorize(
   353→                    f"  Use: desloppify plan cluster add {pattern} <finding-id>",
   354→                    "dim",
   355→                ))
   356→                return True  # blocked
   357→            if len(ids) <= _CLUSTER_INDIVIDUAL_THRESHOLD:
   358→                _print_cluster_guard(pattern, ids, state)
   359→                return True  # blocked
   360→    return False  # OK
   361→
   362→
   363→def _print_cluster_guard(cluster_name: str, finding_ids: list[str], state: dict) -> None:
   364→    findings = state.get("findings", {})
   365→    print(colorize(
   366→        f"\n  Cluster '{cluster_name}' has {len(finding_ids)} item(s) — mark them done individually first:\n",
   367→        "yellow",
   368→    ))
   369→    for fid in finding_ids:
   370→        f = findings.get(fid, {})
   371→        summary = f.get("summary", "(no summary)")[:80]
   372→        detector = f.get("detector", "?")
   373→        print(f"    {fid}  [{detector}]  {summary}")
   374→    print(colorize(
   375→        f"\n  Use: desloppify resolve <id> --status fixed --note '...' --attest '...'",
   376→        "dim",
   377→    ))
   378→    print(colorize(
   379→        f"  Or mark each done: desloppify plan done <id> --note '...' --confirm\n",
   380→        "dim",
   381→    ))
   382→
   383→
   384→def _is_synthetic_id(fid: str) -> bool:
   385→    """Return True if the ID is a synthetic workflow/triage item, not a real finding."""
   386→    return fid.startswith("triage::") or fid.startswith("workflow::") or fid.startswith("subjective::")
   387→
   388→
   389→def _resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns: list[str]) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
   390→    """Separate synthetic IDs from real finding patterns.
   391→
   392→    Returns (synthetic_ids, remaining_patterns).
   393→    """
   394→    synthetic = [p for p in patterns if _is_synthetic_id(p)]
   395→    remaining = [p for p in patterns if not _is_synthetic_id(p)]
   396→    return synthetic, remaining
   397→
   398→
   399→def _blocked_triage_stages(plan: dict) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
   400→    """Return ``{stage_id: [blocked_by_ids]}`` for triage stages that can't run yet.
   401→
   402→    Uses the dependency graph and confirmed-stage metadata directly —
   403→    no state needed, no queue item construction.
   404→    """
   405→    from desloppify.app.commands.plan.triage_playbook import TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES
   406→    from desloppify.engine._plan.stale_dimensions import TRIAGE_IDS, TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS
   407→
   408→    order_set = set(plan.get("queue_order", []))
   409→    present = order_set & TRIAGE_IDS
   410→    if not present:
   411→        return {}
   412→
   413→    confirmed = set(plan.get("epic_triage_meta", {}).get("triage_stages", {}).keys())
   414→    stage_names = ("observe", "reflect", "organize", "commit")
   415→
   416→    blocked: dict[str, list[str]] = {}
   417→    for sid, name in zip(TRIAGE_STAGE_IDS, stage_names):
   418→        if sid not in present or name in confirmed:
   419→            continue
   420→        deps = TRIAGE_STAGE_DEPENDENCIES.get(name, set())
   421→        unmet = sorted(
   422→            f"triage::{dep}" for dep in deps
   423→            if f"triage::{dep}" in present and dep not in confirmed
   424→        )
   425→        if unmet:
   426→            blocked[sid] = unmet
   427→    return blocked
   428→
   429→
   430→def cmd_plan_done(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   431→    """Mark findings as fixed — delegates to cmd_resolve for rich UX."""
   432→    patterns: list[str] = getattr(args, "patterns", [])
   433→    attestation: str | None = getattr(args, "attest", None)
   434→    note: str | None = getattr(args, "note", None)
   435→
   436→    # --confirm: auto-generate attestation from --note
   437→    if getattr(args, "confirm", False):
   438→        if not note:
   439→            print(colorize("  --confirm requires --note to describe what you did.", "red"))
   440→            return
   441→        attestation = f"I have actually {note} and I am not gaming the score."
   442→        args.attest = attestation
   443→
   444→    # Handle synthetic IDs (triage::*, workflow::*, subjective::*) directly
   445→    synthetic_ids, real_patterns = _resolve_synthetic_ids(patterns)
   446→    if synthetic_ids:
   447→        plan = load_plan()
   448→        # Validate triage dependency chain
   449→        blocked_map = _blocked_triage_stages(plan)
   450→        for sid in synthetic_ids:
   451→            if sid in blocked_map:
   452→                deps = ", ".join(b.replace("triage::", "") for b in blocked_map[sid])
   453→                print(colorize(f"  Cannot resolve {sid} — blocked by: {deps}", "red"))
   454→                print(colorize("  Complete those stages first, or use --force-resolve to override.", "dim"))
   455→                if not getattr(args, "force_resolve", False):
   456→                    return
   457→        purge_ids(plan, synthetic_ids)
   458→        append_log_entry(
   459→            plan, "done", finding_ids=synthetic_ids, actor="user", note=note,
   460→        )
   461→        save_plan(plan)
   462→        for sid in synthetic_ids:
   463→            print(colorize(f"  Resolved: {sid}", "green"))
   464→        if not real_patterns:
   465→            return
   466→        # Continue with remaining real patterns
   467→        patterns = real_patterns
   468→        args.patterns = patterns
   469→
   470→    # Validate note length
   471→    if not validate_note_length(note):
   472→        show_note_length_requirement(note)
   473→        return
   474→
   475→    # Pre-validate attestation before delegating (avoids stale hint in resolve)
   476→    if not validate_attestation(attestation):
   477→        show_attestation_requirement("Plan done", attestation, ATTEST_EXAMPLE)
   478→        return
   479→
   480→    # Cluster completion guard: block bulk-completing small clusters
   481→    try:
   482→        runtime = command_runtime(args)
   483→        state = runtime.state
   484→        plan = load_plan()
   485→        if _check_cluster_guard(patterns, plan, state):
   486→            return
   487→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
   488→        plan = None
   489→
   490→    # Log the done action (best-effort)
   491→    try:
   492→        if plan is None:
   493→            plan = load_plan()
   494→        clusters = plan.get("clusters", {})
   495→        cluster_name = None
   496→        for p in patterns:
   497→            if p in clusters:
   498→                cluster_name = p
   499→                break
   500→        append_log_entry(
   501→            plan,
   502→            "done",
   503→            finding_ids=patterns,
   504→            cluster_name=cluster_name,
   505→            actor="user",
   506→            note=note,
   507→        )
   508→        save_plan(plan)
   509→    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as exc:
   510→        print(colorize(f"  Note: unable to append plan done log entry ({exc}).", "dim"))
   511→
   512→    # Deferred import: cmd_resolve has a heavy import chain that isn't needed
   513→    # for synthetic-ID handling above.
   514→    from desloppify.app.commands.resolve.cmd import cmd_resolve
   515→
   516→    # Build a Namespace that cmd_resolve expects
   517→    resolve_args = argparse.Namespace(
   518→        status="fixed",
   519→        patterns=patterns,
   520→        note=note,
   521→        attest=attestation,
   522→        confirm_batch_wontfix=False,
   523→        force_resolve=bool(getattr(args, "force_resolve", False)),
   524→        state=getattr(args, "state", None),
   525→        lang=getattr(args, "lang", None),
   526→        path=getattr(args, "path", None),
   527→        exclude=getattr(args, "exclude", None),
   528→    )
   529→
   530→    cmd_resolve(resolve_args)

   532→
   533→def cmd_plan_focus(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
   534→    """Set or clear the active cluster focus."""
   535→    clear_flag = getattr(args, "clear", False)
   536→    cluster_name: str | None = getattr(args, "cluster_name", None)
   537→
   538→    plan = load_plan()
   539→    if clear_flag:
   540→        prev = plan.get("active_cluster")
   541→        clear_focus(plan)
   542→        append_log_entry(
   543→            plan, "focus", actor="user",
   544→            detail={"action": "clear", "previous": prev},
   545→        )
   546→        save_plan(plan)
   547→        print(colorize("  Focus cleared.", "green"))
   548→        return
   549→
   550→    if not cluster_name:
   551→        active = plan.get("active_cluster")
   552→        if active:
   553→            print(f"  Focused on: {active}")
   554→        else:
   555→            print("  No active focus.")
   556→        return
   557→
   558→    try:
   559→        set_focus(plan, cluster_name)

   567→    save_plan(plan)
   568→    print(colorize(f"  Focused on: {cluster_name}", "green"))
   569→

1→"""Centralized score update display for all state-changing commands."""
     2→
     3→from __future__ import annotations
     4→
     5→from desloppify import state as state_mod
     6→from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.score import target_strict_score_from_config
     7→from desloppify.core import config as config_mod
     8→from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
     9→
    10→
    11→def _format_delta(value: float, prev: float | None) -> tuple[str, str]:
    12→    """Return (delta_str, color) for a score change."""
    13→    delta = value - prev if prev is not None else 0
    14→    delta_str = f" ({'+' if delta > 0 else ''}{delta:.1f})" if delta != 0 else ""
    15→    color = "green" if delta > 0 else ("red" if delta < 0 else "dim")
    16→    return delta_str, color
    17→
    18→
    19→def print_score_update(
    20→    state: dict,
    21→    prev: state_mod.ScoreSnapshot,
    22→    *,
    23→    config: dict | None = None,
    24→    label: str = "Scores",
    25→) -> None:
    26→    """Print score quartet with deltas and strict target progress.
    27→
    28→    Args:
    29→        state: Current state dict (scores already recomputed by save_state).
    30→        prev: ScoreSnapshot taken before the operation.
    31→        config: Project config dict (loaded from disk if not provided).
    32→        label: Prefix label (default "Scores").
    33→    """
    34→    new = state_mod.score_snapshot(state)
    35→    if (
    36→        new.overall is None
    37→        or new.objective is None
    38→        or new.strict is None
    39→        or new.verified is None
    40→    ):
    41→        print(colorize(f"\n  {label} unavailable — run `desloppify scan`.", "yellow"))
    42→        return
    43→
    44→    overall_s, overall_c = _format_delta(new.overall, prev.overall)
    45→    objective_s, objective_c = _format_delta(new.objective, prev.objective)
    46→    strict_s, strict_c = _format_delta(new.strict, prev.strict)
    47→    verified_s, verified_c = _format_delta(new.verified, prev.verified)
    48→
    49→    print(
    50→        f"\n  {label}: "
    51→        + colorize(f"overall {new.overall:.1f}/100{overall_s}", overall_c)
    52→        + colorize(f"  objective {new.objective:.1f}/100{objective_s}", objective_c)
    53→        + colorize(f"  strict {new.strict:.1f}/100{strict_s}", strict_c)
    54→        + colorize(f"  verified {new.verified:.1f}/100{verified_s}", verified_c)
    55→    )
    56→
    57→    # Score-drop reassurance after structural fixes
    58→    if new.strict is not None and prev.strict is not None and new.strict < prev.strict:
    59→        print(colorize(
    60→            "  Score dropped — this is normal after structural fixes. "
    61→            "New findings may surface; keep working the queue.",
    62→            "yellow",
    63→        ))
    64→
    65→    # Always show strict target + next-command nudge
    66→    if config is None:
    67→        config = config_mod.load_config()
    68→
    69→    target = target_strict_score_from_config(config, fallback=95.0)
    70→    _print_strict_target_nudge(new.strict, target)
    71→
    72→
    73→def _print_strict_target_nudge(
    74→    strict: float, target: float, *, show_next: bool = True,
    75→) -> None:
    76→    """Print a one-liner with strict→target and optional next-command nudge."""
    77→    gap = round(target - strict, 1)
    78→    if gap > 0:
    79→        suffix = " — run `desloppify next` to find the next improvement" if show_next else ""
    80→        print(colorize(f"  Strict {strict:.1f} (target: {target:.1f}){suffix}", "dim"))
    81→    else:
    82→        print(colorize(f"  Strict {strict:.1f} — target {target:.1f} reached!", "green"))
    83→
    84→
    85→__all__ = ["print_score_update", "_print_strict_target_nudge"]
    86→

I have now thoroughly explored the codebase. Let me compile the full implementation plan.

---

## Commit Integration Feature -- Implementation Plan

### 1. Design Philosophy and Key Decisions

After thorough exploration of the codebase, here are the recommended approaches for each design question:

**A. Data Model: Hybrid approach -- new top-level `commit_log` on PlanModel + back-references on execution_log entries**

The commit_log is the right primary home because:
- It needs to be independently queryable ("which findings are uncommitted?")
- Execution log entries are append-only and capped at 10,000; they are not designed for reverse lookups
- Findings in state already have `resolved_at` and `resolution_attestation` -- adding git metadata there would couple state (per-language) to plan (cross-language), breaking the clean separation

The execution_log `detail` dict gets a `commit_sha` back-reference when a commit is recorded, linking the two systems.

**B. CLI Interface: No new subcommand. Post-resolve output only, plus a `plan commit-status` query subcommand.**

Rationale: The tool should NOT perform `git add/commit` operations. It is a health tracker, not a git workflow manager. Instead:
- After resolve, print a commit guidance block showing uncommitted findings
- Add `desloppify plan commit-log` to mark findings as committed (recording the SHA) or to view uncommitted findings
- The information output keeps the tool non-interactive (matching the existing pattern of all input via CLI args)

**C. Git Operations: Observation only, never mutation.**

The tool should:
- Detect the current git branch (for recording in commit_log)
- Detect the current HEAD SHA (after user commits externally)
- Check for uncommitted changes via `git status --porcelain`
- NEVER run `git add`, `git commit`, `git checkout`, or any mutation

This is consistent with the tool's philosophy (advisor/tracker, not actor) and avoids the enormous complexity of managing git state.

**D. The "bundle" concept: Implicit bundling via uncommitted-findings accumulation**

Findings accumulate in an "uncommitted" state after resolve. The user sees them listed together in the post-resolve guidance. When they run `desloppify plan commit-log record`, all uncommitted findings (or a subset) get tagged with the current HEAD SHA. This naturally handles:
- "Commit just this task" -- resolve one, then record
- "Bundle with previous" -- resolve several, then record once
- "Wait and bundle with next" -- just keep resolving; the uncommitted list grows

### 2. Detailed Data Model Changes

#### 2a. New TypedDict: `CommitRecord` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/schema.py`

```python
class CommitRecord(TypedDict, total=False):
    sha: Required[str]          # git commit SHA (short or full)
    branch: str | None          # branch name at commit time
    finding_ids: list[str]      # findings included in this commit
    recorded_at: str            # ISO timestamp when record was created
    note: str | None            # user-provided commit rationale
    cluster_name: str | None    # if these findings were from a focused cluster
```

#### 2b. New field on `PlanModel`

```python
class PlanModel(TypedDict, total=False):
    # ... existing fields ...
    commit_log: list[CommitRecord]        # ordered list of commit records
    uncommitted_findings: list[str]       # finding IDs resolved but not yet committed
```

#### 2c. `empty_plan()` update

Add defaults:
```python
"commit_log": [],
"uncommitted_findings": [],
```

#### 2d. `ensure_container_types()` in schema_migrations.py

Add:
```python
if not isinstance(plan.get("commit_log"), list):
    plan["commit_log"] = []
if not isinstance(plan.get("uncommitted_findings"), list):
    plan["uncommitted_findings"] = []
```

#### 2e. No PLAN_VERSION bump needed

These are additive fields with list defaults. `ensure_plan_defaults` already handles missing keys via `setdefault`. A version bump would only be needed if we changed the semantics of existing fields.

### 3. Config Changes

#### New keys in `CONFIG_SCHEMA` in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/core/config.py`:

```python
"commit_tracking_enabled": ConfigKey(
    bool, True, "Show commit guidance after resolve and track uncommitted findings"
),
"commit_default_branch": ConfigKey(
    str, "", "Default branch name for commit context (empty = auto-detect from git)"
),
"commit_message_template": ConfigKey(
    str,
    "desloppify: {status} {count} finding(s) — {summary}",
    "Template for suggested commit messages ({status}, {count}, {summary}, {ids} available)",
),
```

The `commit_tracking_enabled` key allows users to opt out entirely (e.g., in CI pipelines or when not using git). The `commit_default_branch` stores the target branch -- empty means auto-detect. The `commit_message_template` provides a customizable commit message suggestion.

### 4. New Module: Git Observation Layer

#### New file: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/core/git_context.py`

This module provides read-only git introspection with graceful degradation. It is the single place where `subprocess` is invoked for git operations.

```python
"""Read-only git context detection with graceful degradation."""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import shutil
import subprocess
from dataclasses import dataclass

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class GitContext:
    """Snapshot of the current git state. All fields None when git is unavailable."""
    available: bool
    branch: str | None           # current branch name
    head_sha: str | None         # current HEAD short SHA
    has_uncommitted: bool | None  # True if working tree has changes
    root: str | None             # git repo root path


def detect_git_context() -> GitContext:
    """Detect current git context. Returns a GitContext with available=False
    when git is not installed or not in a repo."""
    if not shutil.which("git"):
        return GitContext(available=False, branch=None, head_sha=None,
                         has_uncommitted=None, root=None)
    try:
        root = _run_git("rev-parse", "--show-toplevel")
        branch = _run_git("rev-parse", "--abbrev-ref", "HEAD")
        head_sha = _run_git("rev-parse", "--short", "HEAD")
        status_output = _run_git("status", "--porcelain")
        has_uncommitted = bool(status_output.strip())
        return GitContext(
            available=True,
            branch=branch.strip() if branch else None,
            head_sha=head_sha.strip() if head_sha else None,
            has_uncommitted=has_uncommitted,
            root=root.strip() if root else None,
        )
    except (subprocess.SubprocessError, OSError, FileNotFoundError) as exc:
        logger.debug("Git context detection failed: %s", exc)
        return GitContext(available=False, branch=None, head_sha=None,
                         has_uncommitted=None, root=None)


def _run_git(*args: str) -> str:
    """Run a git command and return stdout. Raises on failure."""
    result = subprocess.run(
        ["git", *args],
        capture_output=True,
        text=True,
        timeout=5,
    )
    if result.returncode != 0:
        raise subprocess.SubprocessError(
            f"git {' '.join(args)} failed: {result.stderr.strip()}"
        )
    return result.stdout
```

### 5. New Module: Commit Tracking Operations

#### New file: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/engine/_plan/commit_tracking.py`

```python
"""Commit tracking operations on the plan model."""

from __future__ import annotations

from desloppify.engine._plan.schema import (
    CommitRecord,
    PlanModel,
    ensure_plan_defaults,
)
from desloppify.engine._state.schema import utc_now


def add_uncommitted_findings(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
    """Mark finding IDs as resolved-but-uncommitted. Returns count added."""
    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    uncommitted: list[str] = plan.get("uncommitted_findings", [])
    existing = set(uncommitted)
    added = 0
    for fid in finding_ids:
        if fid not in existing:
            uncommitted.append(fid)
            existing.add(fid)
            added += 1
    plan["uncommitted_findings"] = uncommitted
    return added


def record_commit(
    plan: PlanModel,
    *,
    sha: str,
    branch: str | None = None,
    finding_ids: list[str] | None = None,
    note: str | None = None,
    cluster_name: str | None = None,
) -> CommitRecord:
    """Record a commit against uncommitted findings.

    If *finding_ids* is None, records all uncommitted findings.
    Removes recorded IDs from uncommitted_findings.
    """
    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    uncommitted: list[str] = plan.get("uncommitted_findings", [])

    if finding_ids is None:
        # Record all uncommitted
        ids_to_record = list(uncommitted)
    else:
        ids_to_record = [fid for fid in finding_ids if fid in set(uncommitted)]

    record: CommitRecord = {
        "sha": sha,
        "branch": branch,
        "finding_ids": ids_to_record,
        "recorded_at": utc_now(),
        "note": note,
        "cluster_name": cluster_name,
    }
    plan.setdefault("commit_log", []).append(record)

    # Remove from uncommitted
    recorded_set = set(ids_to_record)
    plan["uncommitted_findings"] = [
        fid for fid in uncommitted if fid not in recorded_set
    ]
    return record


def get_uncommitted_findings(plan: PlanModel) -> list[str]:
    """Return list of resolved-but-uncommitted finding IDs."""
    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    return list(plan.get("uncommitted_findings", []))


def find_commit_for_finding(plan: PlanModel, finding_id: str) -> CommitRecord | None:
    """Find the commit record that includes a given finding ID."""
    for record in plan.get("commit_log", []):
        if finding_id in record.get("finding_ids", []):
            return record
    return None


def purge_uncommitted_ids(plan: PlanModel, finding_ids: list[str]) -> int:
    """Remove finding IDs from uncommitted list (e.g., when reopened). Returns count removed."""
    ensure_plan_defaults(plan)
    uncommitted: list[str] = plan.get("uncommitted_findings", [])
    purge_set = set(finding_ids)
    before = len(uncommitted)
    plan["uncommitted_findings"] = [fid for fid in uncommitted if fid not in purge_set]
    return before - len(plan["uncommitted_findings"])
```

The new `_print_commit_guidance` function goes in `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/resolve/render.py`:

```python
def _print_commit_guidance(
    state: dict,
    just_resolved: list[str],
    status: str,
) -> None:
    """Show post-resolve commit guidance block."""
    if status != "fixed":
        return  # Only prompt for fixed status, not wontfix/reopen

    from desloppify.core.config import load_config
    config = load_config()
    if not config.get("commit_tracking_enabled", True):
        return

    from desloppify.core.git_context import detect_git_context
    git = detect_git_context()
    if not git.available:
        return  # Graceful degradation: no git, no guidance

    from desloppify.engine.plan import load_plan
    from desloppify.engine._plan.commit_tracking import get_uncommitted_findings

    try:
        plan = load_plan()
        uncommitted = get_uncommitted_findings(plan)
    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
        return

    if not uncommitted:
        return

    # Render the commit guidance block
    print(colorize("\n  Commit Guidance", "bold"))
    print(colorize("  " + "-" * 40, "dim"))

    if git.has_uncommitted:
        print(colorize("  Working tree has uncommitted changes.", "yellow"))
    if git.branch:
        print(colorize(f"  Branch: {git.branch}", "dim"))

    just_set = set(just_resolved)
    previous = [fid for fid in uncommitted if fid not in just_set]

    if previous:
        print(colorize(f"  {len(previous)} previously resolved finding(s) also uncommitted:", "yellow"))
        for fid in previous[:5]:
            summary = _finding_summary(state, fid)
            print(f"    {fid}  {summary}")
        if len(previous) > 5:
            print(colorize(f"    ... and {len(previous) - 5} more", "dim"))

    total = len(uncommitted)
    print(colorize(f"\n  Total uncommitted: {total} finding(s)", "cyan"))

    # Suggest commit message
    template = config.get("commit_message_template", "desloppify: fixed {count} finding(s)")
    suggested = template.format(
        status="fixed",
        count=total,
        summary=_brief_summary(state, uncommitted[:3]),
        ids=", ".join(uncommitted[:5]),
    )
    print(colorize(f"  Suggested commit message:", "dim"))
    print(colorize(f'    "{suggested}"', "dim"))
    print(colorize(
        f"\n  After committing, record it: "
        f"`desloppify plan commit-log record`",
        "dim",
    ))


def _finding_summary(state: dict, finding_id: str) -> str:
    """Get a short summary for a finding ID."""
    finding = state.get("findings", {}).get(finding_id, {})
    return finding.get("summary", "(resolved)")[:60]


def _brief_summary(state: dict, finding_ids: list[str]) -> str:
    """Build a brief summary string from a list of finding IDs."""
    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    detectors = set()
    for fid in finding_ids:
        f = findings.get(fid, {})
        d = f.get("detector", "")
        if d:
            detectors.add(d)
    if detectors:
        return ", ".join(sorted(detectors)[:3])
    return "resolved findings"
```

examples:

  desloppify plan commit-log record               # record current HEAD for all uncommitted
  desloppify plan commit-log record --sha abc123  # record specific SHA
  desloppify plan commit-log record --note "..."  # add rationale
  desloppify plan commit-log history              # show commit history""",
    formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter,

    help="Commit rationale or description",
)

    help="Record only specific finding IDs (default: all uncommitted)",
)

)

#### 6d. New handler: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/desloppify/desloppify/app/commands/plan/commit_log_handlers.py`

```python
"""Plan commit-log subcommand handlers."""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse

from desloppify.app.commands.helpers.runtime import command_runtime
from desloppify.core.git_context import detect_git_context
from desloppify.core.output_api import colorize
from desloppify.engine._plan.commit_tracking import (
    get_uncommitted_findings,
    record_commit,
)
from desloppify.engine.plan import append_log_entry, load_plan, save_plan


def cmd_commit_log_dispatch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Dispatch plan commit-log subcommands."""
    action = getattr(args, "commit_log_action", None)
    if action == "record":
        _cmd_commit_log_record(args)
    elif action == "history":
        _cmd_commit_log_history(args)
    else:
        _cmd_commit_log_show(args)


def _cmd_commit_log_show(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Show uncommitted findings (default action)."""
    runtime = command_runtime(args)
    state = runtime.state
    plan = load_plan()
    uncommitted = get_uncommitted_findings(plan)

    if not uncommitted:
        print(colorize("  All resolved findings have been committed.", "green"))
        return

    print(colorize(f"  {len(uncommitted)} uncommitted finding(s):", "bold"))
    findings = state.get("findings", {})
    for fid in uncommitted:
        f = findings.get(fid, {})
        summary = f.get("summary", "(resolved)")[:70]
        detector = f.get("detector", "?")
        print(f"    {fid}  [{detector}]  {summary}")

    git = detect_git_context()
    if git.available and git.branch:
        print(colorize(f"\n  Branch: {git.branch}", "dim"))
    if git.available and git.has_uncommitted:
        print(colorize("  Working tree has uncommitted changes.", "yellow"))

    print(colorize(
        "\n  Record after committing: `desloppify plan commit-log record`",
        "dim",
    ))


def _cmd_commit_log_record(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Record that uncommitted findings have been committed."""
    plan = load_plan()
    uncommitted = get_uncommitted_findings(plan)
    if not uncommitted:
        print(colorize("  Nothing to record — all findings are committed.", "green"))
        return

    sha = getattr(args, "sha", None)
    branch = getattr(args, "branch", None)
    note = getattr(args, "note", None)
    only_patterns = getattr(args, "only", None)

    # Auto-detect from git if not provided
    if sha is None or branch is None:
        git = detect_git_context()
        if git.available:
            if sha is None:
                sha = git.head_sha
            if branch is None:
                branch = git.branch
        elif sha is None:
            print(colorize(
                "  Git not available. Provide --sha manually.",
                "red",
            ))
            return

    finding_ids = None
    if only_patterns:
        finding_ids = [fid for fid in uncommitted if fid in set(only_patterns)]
        if not finding_ids:
            print(colorize("  No uncommitted findings match the provided IDs.", "yellow"))
            return

    record = record_commit(
        plan,
        sha=sha,
        branch=branch,
        finding_ids=finding_ids,
        note=note,
    )
    append_log_entry(
        plan,
        "commit_record",
        finding_ids=record["finding_ids"],
        actor="user",
        note=note,
        detail={"sha": sha, "branch": branch},
    )
    save_plan(plan)

    count = len(record["finding_ids"])
    print(colorize(f"  Recorded {count} finding(s) to commit {sha}.", "green"))
    if branch:
        print(colorize(f"  Branch: {branch}", "dim"))
    remaining = get_uncommitted_findings(plan)
    if remaining:
        print(colorize(f"  {len(remaining)} finding(s) still uncommitted.", "yellow"))


def _cmd_commit_log_history(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    """Show commit history."""
    plan = load_plan()
    commit_log = plan.get("commit_log", [])
    top = getattr(args, "top", 10)

    if not commit_log:
        print(colorize("  No commit records yet.", "dim"))
        return

    recent = commit_log[-top:] if top else commit_log
    recent.reverse()  # Most recent first

    print(colorize(f"  Commit History ({len(recent)}/{len(commit_log)})", "bold"))
    print(colorize("  " + "-" * 50, "dim"))
    for record in recent:
        sha = record.get("sha", "?")[:8]
        branch = record.get("branch", "")
        count = len(record.get("finding_ids", []))
        timestamp = record.get("recorded_at", "?")[:10]
        note_str = f" — {record['note'][:40]}" if record.get("note") else ""
        branch_str = f" ({branch})" if branch else ""
        print(f"    {sha}{branch_str}  {count} finding(s)  {timestamp}{note_str}")
```

2. Add `CommitRecord` TypedDict and new fields to `PlanModel` in schema.py

**Phase 2: Core integration**

**Phase 3: CLI subcommand**

**Phase 4: Polish**

### 12. Trade-offs Considered

**Why not a top-level `desloppify commit` command?**
A separate top-level command would suggest the tool manages git, which it does not. Nesting under `plan commit-log` keeps it clearly associated with the plan lifecycle and avoids confusion. The post-resolve guidance block provides the primary user-facing nudge.

**Why not store commit data per-finding in state?**
State is per-language and the tool carefully separates state (findings, scores) from plan (prioritization, workflow). Commit metadata is workflow data, not detection data. Putting it in plan keeps the clean separation.

**Why not use interactive prompts?**
The tool currently uses zero interactive prompts (all via CLI args). Adding `input()` calls would break agent workflows, piped commands, and CI usage. The information-push model (show guidance, let user act externally) is more composable.

**Why observation-only for git?**
Running `git add/commit` introduces enormous complexity: handling merge conflicts, staged vs unstaged, .gitignore, submodules, worktrees, and all the edge cases. The tool is a health tracker, not a VCS manager. Observation-only means it can never corrupt a user's repo.
